using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace PavementBuildup.Core;

/// <summary>The parts of a detail that each get their own CAD layer.</summary>
public enum DetailElement
{
    Outline,    // course interfaces and side edges
    Hatch,      // default layer for hatches (a hatch rule may override it)
    Membrane,   // zero-thickness courses: geotextile, DPM, slip membrane
    Leader,     // label leader lines and dots
    Text,       // course labels
    Dimension,  // thickness dimensions
    Title,      // title, scale and total depth
    Reinforcement, // bars (dots) and transverse bars (lines)
    ExistingHatch, // hatch of anything existing (existing slab/pavement/base)
    ExistingOutline, // closed polyline round anything existing
}

/// <summary>Layer name and properties for one <see cref="DetailElement"/>.</summary>
public sealed class LayerStyle
{
    public DetailElement Element { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>ACI number ("7"), "R,G,B" true colour.</summary>
    public string Color { get; set; } = "7";
    public string Linetype { get; set; } = "Continuous";
    /// <summary>Lineweight in mm (0.35); negative means the AutoCAD default.</summary>
    public double LineWeightMm { get; set; } = -1;
    public bool Plot { get; set; } = true;

    public LayerStyle Clone() => (LayerStyle)MemberwiseClone();
}

/// <summary>
/// "When a course description contains one of these keywords, hatch it like this."
/// Rules are checked top to bottom and the first match wins.
/// </summary>
public sealed class HatchRule
{
    public string Name { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>Comma-separated, case-insensitive keywords, e.g. "type 1, sub-base, granular".</summary>
    public string Keywords { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>Pattern name from acadiso.pat, a custom .pat on the support path, SOLID or NONE (unhatched).</summary>
    public string Pattern { get; set; } = "ANSI31";
    /// <summary>Pattern scale for a 1:1 detail in a millimetre drawing; multiplied by the detail scale.</summary>
    public double Scale { get; set; } = 0.5;
    public double Angle { get; set; }
    /// <summary>Layer for this hatch. Blank uses the Hatch element layer.</summary>
    public string Layer { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>"BYLAYER", "BYBLOCK", an ACI number or "R,G,B".</summary>
    public string Color { get; set; } = "BYLAYER";
    /// <summary>Hatch background fill colour; blank for none.</summary>
    public string BackgroundColor { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>Transparency 0-90 %; -1 = by layer.</summary>
    public int Transparency { get; set; } = -1;
    /// <summary>Hatch lineweight in mm; -1 = by layer.</summary>
    public double LineWeightMm { get; set; } = -1;

    public IEnumerable<string> KeywordList() =>
        Keywords.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries)
            .Select(k => k.ToLowerInvariant());

    /// <summary>
    /// True when a keyword appears at the start of a word ("mpa" matches "32MPa" but not "compacted";
    /// "sand" matches "sharp sand" but not "thousand"; "pa" matches "PA" but not "pavement").
    /// </summary>
    public bool Matches(string description)
    {
        var text = " " + description.ToLowerInvariant() + " ";
        return KeywordList().Any(k => StartsAWord(text, k));
    }

    private static bool StartsAWord(string text, string keyword)
    {
        // Abbreviations (3 letters or fewer: PA, SMA, MOT, DGB) must be whole words, though a number may
        // follow ("SMA10", "DGB20"); longer keywords may start a longer word ("paver" in "pavers").
        bool shortWord = keyword.Count(char.IsLetter) <= 3;
        for (int i = text.IndexOf(keyword, StringComparison.Ordinal); i >= 0; i = text.IndexOf(keyword, i + 1, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            bool starts = i == 0 || !char.IsLetter(text[i - 1]);
            int end = i + keyword.Length;
            bool ends = !shortWord || end >= text.Length || !char.IsLetter(text[end]);
            if (starts && ends)
                return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    public bool IsNone => string.Equals(Pattern?.Trim(), MaterialLibrary.None, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    public HatchRule Clone() => (HatchRule)MemberwiseClone();
}

/// <summary>A company CAD standard: what layers, hatches, styles and wording the detail uses.</summary>
public sealed class CadStandard
{
    public string Name { get; set; } = "Default";

    /// <summary>
    /// Units the company draws in: M (1 unit = 1 metre, so 40mm draws as 0.04), MM, CM, or AUTO (read INSUNITS).
    /// Thicknesses are always typed in millimetres.
    /// </summary>
    public string DrawingUnits { get; set; } = "M";

    /// <summary>
    /// Name for each detail's block (or group): '#' is replaced by the next free number, {name} by the
    /// build-up name. E.g. "TTW_pavement-profile_#" gives TTW_pavement-profile_1, _2, ...
    /// </summary>
    public string DetailNameFormat { get; set; } = DetailNaming.DefaultFormat;

    /// <summary>
    /// Company standard drawing or template (.dwg/.dwt). Before a detail is drawn, any layer, linetype,
    /// text style, dimension style or block this standard names that the current drawing doesn't have
    /// is copied in from it. A file name alone is looked for next to the standard (.json). Blank = TTW_Pave_Temp.dwg
    /// next to the standard, if it's there.
    /// </summary>
    public string StandardDrawing { get; set; } = "";

    /// <summary>The company template drawing used when <see cref="StandardDrawing"/> is blank, if it sits next to the standard.</summary>
    public const string DefaultTemplate = "TTW_Pave_Temp.dwg";

    /// <summary>The folder the standard was loaded from (not saved). A relative standard drawing is found there.</summary>
    [System.Text.Json.Serialization.JsonIgnore]
    public string SourceFolder { get; set; } = "";

    /// <summary>
    /// The template drawing to copy layers, styles and blocks from: <see cref="StandardDrawing"/> (a full path, or a
    /// file name next to the standard), else TTW_Pave_Temp.dwg next to the standard. Null when there is none.
    /// </summary>
    public string? ResolveStandardDrawing()
    {
        var name = (StandardDrawing ?? "").Trim();
        var candidates = new List<string>();
        if (name.Length > 0)
        {
            candidates.Add(name);
            if (!Path.IsPathRooted(name) && SourceFolder.Length > 0) candidates.Insert(0, Path.Combine(SourceFolder, name));
        }
        else if (SourceFolder.Length > 0)
            candidates.Add(Path.Combine(SourceFolder, DefaultTemplate));
        var found = candidates.FirstOrDefault(File.Exists);
        return found ?? (name.Length > 0 ? candidates[0] : null);
    }

    /// <summary>Instructions file for "Interpret with AI" (e.g. a company-edited prompt on the shared drive). Blank = built-in.</summary>
    public string AiPromptFile { get; set; } = "";

    /// <summary>
    /// How the side break lines are made: "BREAKLINE" builds them the way Express Tools _BREAKLINE does,
    /// with <see cref="BreakLineBlock"/> at the middle of each edge; "BUILTIN" draws a simple Z break.
    /// </summary>
    public string BreakLineMethod { get; set; } = BreakLineCommand;
    public const string BreakLineCommand = "BREAKLINE";
    public const string BreakLineBuiltIn = "BUILTIN";

    /// <summary>Break symbol block (in the drawing, the standard drawing, or NAME.dwg on the support path).
    /// Like _BREAKLINE, its two POINT objects mark where the line joins the symbol.</summary>
    public string BreakLineBlock { get; set; } = "TTW_stdCountry_Block_Break";

    /// <summary>Symbol scale (_BREAKLINE "Size"), as plotted mm; multiplied by the detail scale and drawing units.</summary>
    public double BreakLineSizeMm { get; set; } = 5;

    /// <summary>How far the break line runs past the top and bottom of the detail (_BREAKLINE "Extension"), plotted mm.</summary>
    public double BreakLineExtensionMm { get; set; }

    public List<LayerStyle> Layers { get; set; } = new();

    /// <summary>Text style name; blank or missing uses the drawing's current style.</summary>
    public string TextStyle { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>Dimension style name; blank or missing uses the drawing's current style.</summary>
    public string DimensionStyle { get; set; } = "";

    public double TextHeightMm { get; set; } = 2.5;
    public double TitleHeightMm { get; set; } = 3.5;
    /// <summary>Plotted height of the "SCALE 1:10" line under the title.</summary>
    public double ScaleHeightMm { get; set; } = 2.5;
    /// <summary>Plotted dimension text height and arrow size. Always applied (dimensions are made non-annotative).</summary>
    public double DimensionTextHeightMm { get; set; } = 2.5;
    public double DimensionArrowSizeMm { get; set; } = 2.0;
    public bool UpperCaseLabels { get; set; } = true;
    /// <summary>Label lines longer than this many characters wrap onto the next line (0 = never wrap).</summary>
    public int LabelWrapChars { get; set; } = 60;
    /// <summary>Plotted width of membrane lines (mm); 0 for a plain line.</summary>
    public double MembraneWidthMm { get; set; } = 0.35;

    /// <summary>Course label. Tokens: {thickness} {description}.</summary>
    public string LabelFormat { get; set; } = "{thickness}mm {description}";
    /// <summary>Label for zero-thickness courses. Tokens: {description}.</summary>
    public string MembraneLabelFormat { get; set; } = "{description}";
    /// <summary>Thickness dimension text. Tokens: {thickness}.</summary>
    public string DimensionFormat { get; set; } = "{thickness}mm";
    /// <summary>Title lines. Tokens: {name} {scale} {total}. Blank lines are skipped.</summary>
    public string TitleFormat { get; set; } = "{name}";
    public string ScaleFormat { get; set; } = "SCALE 1:{scale}";
    public string TotalFormat { get; set; } = "TOTAL CONSTRUCTION DEPTH = {total}mm";

    /// <summary>
    /// Label for a bare subgrade ("subgrade", "natural subgrade", "formation" with nothing else said): the soil
    /// and its compaction. Blank = keep the plain wording. A subgrade written with more detail keeps its own text.
    /// </summary>
    public string SubgradeNote { get; set; } = "SUBGRADE COMPACTED TO 98% SMDD AT ±2% OMC";

    /// <summary>Bar type prefix when none is typed, e.g. "H" (UK B500), "N" (AU), "T", "Ø".</summary>
    public string BarPrefix { get; set; } = "H";
    /// <summary>One set of bars. Tokens: {prefix} {diameter} {spacing}.</summary>
    public string BarFormat { get; set; } = "{prefix}{diameter} @ {spacing} c/c";
    /// <summary>Reinforcement label. Tokens: {bars} {transverse} {face} {cover}. Lines with an empty {transverse} drop the " + ".</summary>
    public string ReinforcementLabelFormat { get; set; } = "{bars} {face} + {transverse} - {cover}mm COVER";
    public string TopFaceText { get; set; } = "TOP";
    public string BottomFaceText { get; set; } = "BTM";
    /// <summary>Dimension the cover from the face to the bars.</summary>
    public bool ShowCoverDimension { get; set; } = true;
    /// <summary>Cover dimension text. Tokens: {cover}.</summary>
    public string CoverDimensionFormat { get; set; } = "{cover}";

    public List<HatchRule> HatchRules { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>Used when no rule matches a course.</summary>
    public HatchRule DefaultHatch { get; set; } = new() { Name = "Default", Pattern = "ANSI31", Scale = 0.5 };
    /// <summary>Used for the subgrade strip.</summary>
    public HatchRule SubgradeHatch { get; set; } = new() { Name = "Subgrade", Pattern = "EARTH", Scale = 0.5, Angle = 45 };

    public LayerStyle Layer(DetailElement element) =>
        Layers.FirstOrDefault(l => l.Element == element) ?? DefaultLayer(element);

    /// <summary>The first rule whose keywords appear in the description, else <see cref="DefaultHatch"/>.</summary>
    public HatchRule RuleFor(string description) =>
        HatchRules.FirstOrDefault(r => r.Matches(description)) ?? DefaultHatch;

    /// <summary>Settings for an explicit pattern: a rule using that pattern if there is one, else a plain rule.</summary>
    public HatchRule RuleForPattern(string pattern) =>
        HatchRules.Append(DefaultHatch).Append(SubgradeHatch)
            .FirstOrDefault(r => string.Equals(r.Pattern, pattern, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        ?? new HatchRule { Name = pattern, Pattern = pattern.ToUpperInvariant(), Scale = 1.0 };

    /// <summary>Every layer name the standard can put objects on.</summary>
    public IEnumerable<string> AllLayerNames() =>
        Enum.GetValues<DetailElement>().Select(e => Layer(e).Name)
            .Concat(HatchRules.Append(DefaultHatch).Append(SubgradeHatch).Select(r => r.Layer))
            .Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n))
            .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    /// <summary>Fills in anything missing (e.g. a layer row deleted from the JSON by hand).</summary>
    public CadStandard Normalize()
    {
        Layers ??= new();
        HatchRules ??= new();
        DefaultHatch ??= new() { Name = "Default", Pattern = "ANSI31", Scale = 0.5 };
        SubgradeHatch ??= new() { Name = "Subgrade", Pattern = "EARTH", Scale = 0.5, Angle = 45 };
        foreach (var e in Enum.GetValues<DetailElement>())
            if (Layers.All(l => l.Element != e))
                Layers.Add(DefaultLayer(e));
        Layers = Layers.OrderBy(l => l.Element).ToList();
        return this;
    }

    /// <summary>Problems that would stop the detail being drawn properly. Empty when the standard is usable.</summary>
    public List<string> Validate()
    {
        var errors = new List<string>();
        foreach (var l in Layers)
        {
            if (!IsValidSymbolName(l.Name))
                errors.Add($"Layer for {l.Element}: \"{l.Name}\" is not a valid layer name.");
            if (!ColorSpec.TryParse(l.Color, out var c) || c.Kind is ColorKind.ByLayer or ColorKind.ByBlock)
                errors.Add($"Layer {l.Name}: colour \"{l.Color}\" must be 1-255 or R,G,B.");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l.Linetype))
                errors.Add($"Layer {l.Name}: linetype is blank.");
        }

        foreach (var r in HatchRules.Append(DefaultHatch).Append(SubgradeHatch))
        {
            string who = $"Hatch \"{(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.Name) ? r.Pattern : r.Name)}\"";
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.Pattern))
                errors.Add($"{who}: pattern is blank (use NONE for no hatch).");
            if (!r.IsNone && !(r.Scale > 0))
                errors.Add($"{who}: scale must be greater than zero.");
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.Layer) && !IsValidSymbolName(r.Layer))
                errors.Add($"{who}: \"{r.Layer}\" is not a valid layer name.");
            if (!ColorSpec.TryParse(r.Color, out _))
                errors.Add($"{who}: colour \"{r.Color}\" is not BYLAYER, BYBLOCK, 1-255 or R,G,B.");
            if (r.Transparency is < -1 or > 90)
                errors.Add($"{who}: transparency must be 0-90 % (or -1 for by layer).");
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.BackgroundColor) && !ColorSpec.TryParse(r.BackgroundColor, out _))
                errors.Add($"{who}: background colour \"{r.BackgroundColor}\" is not valid.");
        }
        foreach (var r in HatchRules.Where(r => !r.KeywordList().Any()))
            errors.Add($"Hatch rule \"{r.Name}\" has no keywords, so it never matches.");

        if (!(TextHeightMm > 0) || !(TitleHeightMm > 0) || !(ScaleHeightMm > 0) || !(DimensionTextHeightMm > 0) || !(DimensionArrowSizeMm > 0))
            errors.Add("Text heights must be greater than zero.");
        if (!DetailLayout.StandardUnitChoices.Contains((DrawingUnits ?? "").Trim().ToUpperInvariant()))
            errors.Add($"Drawing units \"{DrawingUnits}\" must be one of {string.Join(", ", DetailLayout.StandardUnitChoices)}.");
        if (!string.Equals(BreakLineMethod, BreakLineCommand, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
            !string.Equals(BreakLineMethod, BreakLineBuiltIn, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            errors.Add($"Break line method must be {BreakLineCommand} or {BreakLineBuiltIn}.");
        if (string.Equals(BreakLineMethod, BreakLineCommand, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            if (!IsValidSymbolName(BreakLineBlock))
                errors.Add($"Break line block \"{BreakLineBlock}\" is not a valid block name.");
            if (!(BreakLineSizeMm > 0))
                errors.Add("Break line size must be greater than zero.");
            if (BreakLineExtensionMm < 0)
                errors.Add("Break line extension cannot be negative.");
        }
        if (BarFormat is null || !BarFormat.Contains("{diameter}", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            errors.Add("Bar format must include {diameter}.");
        if (MembraneWidthMm < 0)
            errors.Add("Membrane line width cannot be negative.");
        return errors;
    }

    // AutoCAD symbol names: up to 255 chars, none of <>/\":;?*|,='`
    private static readonly Regex InvalidSymbolChars = new(@"[<>/\\"":;?*|,='`]");

    public static bool IsValidSymbolName(string? name) =>
        !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name) && name.Length <= 255 && !InvalidSymbolChars.IsMatch(name) && name.Trim() == name;

    public static string Fill(string format, params (string Token, string Value)[] values)
    {
        var s = format ?? "";
        foreach (var (token, value) in values)
            s = s.Replace("{" + token + "}", value, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        return s.Trim();
    }

    /// <summary>Label for one reinforcement mat, e.g. "H16 @ 150 c/c BTM + H10 @ 300 c/c - 50mm COVER".</summary>
    public string ReinforcementLabel(Reinforcement r)
    {
        string prefix = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.Prefix) ? BarPrefix ?? "" : r.Prefix;
        string Bars(double dia, double spacing) =>
            Fill(BarFormat ?? "{prefix}{diameter} @ {spacing} c/c", ("prefix", prefix), ("diameter", Number(dia)), ("spacing", Number(spacing)));

        string format = ReinforcementLabelFormat ?? "";
        string transverse = r.HasTransverse ? Bars(r.TransverseDiameterMm, r.TransverseSpacingMm) : "";
        if (transverse.Length == 0)
            format = Regex.Replace(format, @"\s*(?:\+|&|and)\s*\{transverse\}", "", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);

        string text = Fill(format,
            ("bars", Bars(r.DiameterMm, r.SpacingMm)),
            ("transverse", transverse),
            ("face", r.Face == BarFace.Top ? TopFaceText ?? "" : BottomFaceText ?? ""),
            ("cover", Number(r.CoverMm)));
        return Regex.Replace(text, @"\s{2,}", " ").Trim(); // not upper-cased: "c/c" stays lower case
    }

    public static string Number(double v) => v.ToString("0.##", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

    public CadStandard Clone()
    {
        var c = (CadStandard)MemberwiseClone();
        c.Layers = Layers.Select(l => l.Clone()).ToList();
        c.HatchRules = HatchRules.Select(r => r.Clone()).ToList();
        c.DefaultHatch = DefaultHatch.Clone();
        c.SubgradeHatch = SubgradeHatch.Clone();
        return c;
    }

    private static LayerStyle DefaultLayer(DetailElement e) => e switch
    {
        DetailElement.Outline => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-OUTLINE", Color = "7", LineWeightMm = 0.35 },
        DetailElement.Hatch => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-HATCH", Color = "8", LineWeightMm = 0.13 },
        DetailElement.Membrane => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-MEMBRANE", Color = "1", LineWeightMm = 0.35 },
        DetailElement.Leader => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-TEXT", Color = "2", LineWeightMm = 0.18 },
        DetailElement.Text => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-TEXT", Color = "2", LineWeightMm = 0.18 },
        DetailElement.Dimension => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-DIM", Color = "3", LineWeightMm = 0.18 },
        DetailElement.Title => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-TITLE", Color = "4", LineWeightMm = 0.25 },
        DetailElement.Reinforcement => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-REBAR", Color = "1", LineWeightMm = 0.35 },
        DetailElement.ExistingHatch => new() { Element = e, Name = "PEN-GREY-H", Color = "8", LineWeightMm = 0.13 },
        DetailElement.ExistingOutline => new() { Element = e, Name = "PEN-GREY-C", Color = "8", LineWeightMm = 0.25 },
        _ => new() { Element = e, Name = "PAV-" + e.ToString().ToUpperInvariant() },
    };

    /// <summary>The built-in standard (also what a new standard file starts as).</summary>
    public static CadStandard CreateDefault() => new CadStandard
    {
        Name = "Default",
        HatchRules = new()
        {
            Rule("Membrane", "geotextile, geogrid, geocomposite, membrane, dpm, separator, tack, bond coat, spray", "NONE", 0),
            Rule("Block paving", "block, paver, sett, flag, brick, cobble", "ANSI37", 0.5),
            Rule("Recycled aggregate", "crushed concrete, recycled concrete, recycled aggregate, recycled sub-base, rca, crushed brick", "GRAVEL", 0.15),
            Rule("Concrete / bound", "concrete, pqc, crcp, jpcp, urcp, cbgm, cbm, hbm, lean mix, leanmix, cement, c8/10, c32/40, c40/50, ctb, ctcr, cement treated, cement stabilised, cement stabilized, mpa, lime stabil, stabilised, stabilized", "AR-CONC", 0.08),
            Rule("Bedding / laying course", "bedding, laying course, sand, mortar, screed, grout", "AR-SAND", 0.1),
            Rule("Soils", "topsoil, subgrade, clay, earth, soil, formation, select fill, fill material", "EARTH", 0.5, 45),
            Rule("Granular", "type 1, type1, type 2, type 3, type 4, mot, sub-base, subbase, sub base, granular, crushed, aggregate, capping, 6f, hardcore, stone, gravel, rubble, scalpings, dgb, dgs, crushed rock, road base, roadbase, sub base, fcr, class 1, class 2, class 3, class 4, quarry, crusher dust, gravel", "GRAVEL", 0.15),
            Rule("Asphalt surface", "sma, hra, surface, wearing, thin surf, porous, pa , chip, hot mix, hotmix, dga, dense graded asphalt, ogfc, open graded, ac5, ac7, ac10, ac14, ac 5, ac 7, ac 10, ac 14", "AR-SAND", 0.15),
            Rule("Asphalt binder", "binder", "AR-SAND", 0.3),
            Rule("Asphalt base", "dbm, hdm, ac 32, ac32, base, macadam, asphalt, bitum, tarmac, emac", "ANSI31", 0.5),
        },
    }.Normalize();

    private static HatchRule Rule(string name, string keywords, string pattern, double scale, double angle = 0) =>
        new() { Name = name, Keywords = keywords, Pattern = pattern, Scale = scale, Angle = angle };
}

public enum ColorKind { ByLayer, ByBlock, Index, Rgb }

/// <summary>A colour as written in a standard: BYLAYER, BYBLOCK, ACI 1-255, or "R,G,B".</summary>
public readonly record struct ColorSpec(ColorKind Kind, short Index, byte R, byte G, byte B)
{
    public static bool TryParse(string? text, out ColorSpec color)
    {
        color = new ColorSpec(ColorKind.ByLayer, 256, 0, 0, 0);
        var s = (text ?? "").Trim();
        if (s.Length == 0 || s.Equals("BYLAYER", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return true;
        if (s.Equals("BYBLOCK", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            color = new ColorSpec(ColorKind.ByBlock, 0, 0, 0, 0);
            return true;
        }
        if (short.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var aci))
        {
            if (aci is < 1 or > 255) return false;
            color = new ColorSpec(ColorKind.Index, aci, 0, 0, 0);
            return true;
        }
        var parts = s.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
        if (parts.Length == 3 && parts.All(p => byte.TryParse(p, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out _)))
        {
            color = new ColorSpec(ColorKind.Rgb, -1, byte.Parse(parts[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                byte.Parse(parts[1], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), byte.Parse(parts[2], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    public override string ToString() => Kind switch
    {
        ColorKind.ByLayer => "BYLAYER",
        ColorKind.ByBlock => "BYBLOCK",
        ColorKind.Index => Index.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
        _ => $"{R},{G},{B}",
    };
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/StandardStore.cs   (108 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;

namespace PavementBuildup.Core;

/// <summary>Reads and writes <see cref="CadStandard"/> JSON files.</summary>
public static class StandardStore
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions Options = new()
    {
        WriteIndented = true,
        Converters = { new JsonStringEnumConverter() },
        ReadCommentHandling = JsonCommentHandling.Skip,
        AllowTrailingCommas = true,
    };

    /// <summary>%APPDATA%\PavementBuildup\standard.json</summary>
    public static string DefaultPath => Path.Combine(
        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData), "PavementBuildup", "standard.json");

    /// <summary>The company standard's file name, looked for in "_support\_Standards".</summary>
    public const string CompanyFileName = "TTW.json";

    /// <summary>
    /// Folders the app runs from (the plugin's Contents folder, the standalone app's folder). The company
    /// standard is looked for in "_support\_Standards" there and up to four folders above.
    /// </summary>
    public static List<string> SearchFrom { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>The nearest "_support\_Standards\TTW.json", or null if there isn't one.</summary>
    public static string? CompanyDefault
    {
        get
        {
            foreach (var start in SearchFrom)
            {
                try
                {
                    var dir = new DirectoryInfo(start);
                    for (int up = 0; up <= 4 && dir is not null; up++, dir = dir.Parent)
                    {
                        var file = Path.Combine(dir.FullName, "_support", "_Standards", CompanyFileName);
                        if (File.Exists(file)) return file;
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or ArgumentException or System.Security.SecurityException) { }
            }
            return null;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The file a settings object points at. Blank = the default: the company "_support\_Standards\TTW.json",
    /// else the personal standard.
    /// </summary>
    public static string PathFor(DetailSettings settings) =>
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.StandardPath) ? CompanyDefault ?? DefaultPath : settings.StandardPath;

    /// <summary>What to store in the settings for a chosen file: blank when it is the default anyway.</summary>
    public static string ToSetting(string path) =>
        SamePath(path, CompanyDefault) || (CompanyDefault is null && SamePath(path, DefaultPath)) ? "" : path;

    public static bool SamePath(string? a, string? b)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(a) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b)) return false;
        try { return string.Equals(Path.GetFullPath(a), Path.GetFullPath(b), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase); }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ArgumentException or NotSupportedException or PathTooLongException) { return false; }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Loads a standard. A missing personal standard gives the built-in one; a missing or unreadable
    /// file anywhere else is an error, because silently drawing to the wrong standard is worse.
    /// </summary>
    public static CadStandard Load(string path)
    {
        if (!File.Exists(path))
        {
            if (string.Equals(Path.GetFullPath(path), Path.GetFullPath(DefaultPath), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return CadStandard.CreateDefault();
            throw new FileNotFoundException($"CAD standard not found: {path}", path);
        }

        try
        {
            var standard = Parse(File.ReadAllText(path));
            standard.SourceFolder = Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(path)) ?? "";
            return standard;
        }
        catch (JsonException ex)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException($"CAD standard {path} is not valid: {ex.Message}", ex);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>A standard from its JSON text (throws JsonException / InvalidDataException).</summary>
    public static CadStandard Parse(string json) =>
        (JsonSerializer.Deserialize<CadStandard>(json, Options) ?? throw new InvalidDataException("The file is empty.")).Normalize();

    public static void Save(CadStandard standard, string path)
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(path))!);
        string tmp = path + ".tmp";
        File.WriteAllText(tmp, Serialize(standard));
        File.Move(tmp, path, overwrite: true);
    }

    public static string Serialize(CadStandard standard) => JsonSerializer.Serialize(standard, Options);
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/MaterialLibrary.cs   (73 lines)
################################################################################

namespace PavementBuildup.Core;

/// <summary>A resolved hatch for one band of the detail.</summary>
/// <remarks>
/// <see cref="BaseScale"/> is the pattern scale for a 1:1 detail in a millimetre drawing; the layout
/// multiplies it by the detail scale and drawing units. <see cref="Layer"/> blank means the Hatch layer.
/// </remarks>
public sealed record HatchSpec(
    string Pattern,
    double BaseScale,
    double AngleDeg,
    string Layer = "",
    string Color = "BYLAYER",
    string BackgroundColor = "",
    string RuleName = "",
    int Transparency = -1,
    double LineWeightMm = -1);

/// <summary>Picks the hatch for a course using the hatch rules of a <see cref="CadStandard"/>.</summary>
public static partial class MaterialLibrary
{
    public const string Auto = "AUTO";
    public const string None = "NONE";

    /// <summary>Patterns offered in the course table (all ship with AutoCAD / Civil 3D).</summary>
    public static readonly string[] KnownPatterns =
    {
        Auto, None, "AR-SAND", "AR-CONC", "GRAVEL", "EARTH", "ANSI31", "ANSI32", "ANSI37",
        "AR-HBONE", "DOTS", "HONEY", "CROSS", "NET", "SOLID",
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// The hatch for a course, or null when it is not hatched (zero thickness, NONE, or a rule with pattern NONE).
    /// An explicit pattern on the course overrides the keyword rules.
    /// </summary>
    public static HatchSpec? Resolve(PavementLayer layer, CadStandard standard)
    {
        if (layer.ThicknessMm <= 0)
            return null;

        var rule = RuleFor(layer, standard);
        if (rule is null)
            return null;

        var spec = ToSpec(rule);
        return spec is null ? null : spec with { AngleDeg = layer.HatchAngle ?? spec.AngleDeg };
    }

    /// <summary>The rule a course uses, or null for an explicit NONE.</summary>
    public static HatchRule? RuleFor(PavementLayer layer, CadStandard standard)
    {
        var pattern = layer.HatchPattern?.Trim();
        if (string.Equals(pattern, None, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return null;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pattern) || string.Equals(pattern, Auto, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            // Brackets and remark lines don't pick the hatch; the layer name ("Binder Course") helps after the material.
            var text = $"{BuildupParser.Core(layer.Description)} {layer.Role}".Trim();
            return standard.RuleFor(AsphaltConcreteRegex().Replace(text, "asphalt"));
        }
        return standard.RuleForPattern(pattern);
    }

    public static HatchSpec? ToSpec(HatchRule rule) => rule.IsNone
        ? null
        : new HatchSpec(rule.Pattern.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(), rule.Scale, rule.Angle,
            rule.Layer?.Trim() ?? "", rule.Color ?? "BYLAYER", rule.BackgroundColor ?? "", rule.Name,
            rule.Transparency, rule.LineWeightMm);

    /// <summary>"Asphalt concrete", "asphaltic concrete", "bituminous concrete" are asphalt, not concrete.</summary>
    [System.Text.RegularExpressions.GeneratedRegex(@"\b(?:asphalt(?:ic)?|bituminous)\s+concrete\b", System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex AsphaltConcreteRegex();
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/SmartReader.cs   (748 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace PavementBuildup.Core;

/// <summary>A build-up read from free text, plus what the reader had to interpret to get there.</summary>
public sealed class SmartReadResult
{
    public BuildupParseResult Parsed { get; init; } = new();

    /// <summary>Plain-English notes on what was interpreted ("Converted 0.15m to 150mm", ...), for the user to check.</summary>
    public List<string> Notes { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>The tidied text actually read (shown in the quick-entry box, editable).</summary>
    public string Text { get; init; } = "";
}

/// <summary>
/// The built-in build-up reader: turns build-ups written any reasonable way into courses, without any
/// online AI. It tidies the text into the strict form <see cref="BuildupParser"/> reads, step by step,
/// and says what it interpreted. Already-tidy text passes through unchanged.
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Handles: thickness anywhere in a line ("DGB20 ROAD BASE 150mm THICK", "Basecourse - thickness 150"),
/// metres / cm / inches, "two layers of 150mm", "150mm x 2", "300mm in 2 lifts", ranges ("100-150mm"),
/// min./nom./approx., sentences and "then / followed by / underlain by", "comprising / consisting of",
/// tables pasted from Excel or Word, build-ups written bottom-up, membranes and seals with no thickness,
/// and total lines. Anything in (round brackets) stays a label note and isn't interpreted.
/// </remarks>
public static partial class SmartReader
{
    public static SmartReadResult Read(string? text, List<string> errors)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
            return new SmartReadResult();

        var notes = new List<string>();
        var t = NormaliseCharacters(text);
        bool bottomUp = false;
        t = BottomUpCueRegex().Replace(t, _ => { bottomUp = true; return " "; });
        t = Tables(t, notes);
        t = Lists(t);
        t = Lines(t);
        t = IntroRegex().Replace(t, ": ");
        t = Sentences(t);
        t = OutsideBrackets(t, UnitRegex(), m => Unit(m, notes));
        t = NumberWordRegex().Replace(t, m => NumberWords[m.Groups[1].Value.ToLowerInvariant()]);
        t = OutsideBrackets(t, RangeRegex(), m =>
        {
            notes.Add($"Read the range {m.Value.Trim()} as {Num(m.Groups[2].Value)}mm (range kept as a note).");
            return $"{Num(m.Groups[2].Value)}mm ({Num(m.Groups[1].Value)}-{Num(m.Groups[2].Value)}mm)";
        });
        t = Multiples(t, notes);
        // "... over subgrade", "on existing slab": what it sits on is its own item.
        t = OutsideBrackets(t, BaseConnectorRegex(), _ => "; ");
        t = OutsideBrackets(t, MembraneConnectorRegex(), _ => "; ");

        // Item by item: put the thickness first, membranes as 0mm, drop totals.
        var items = new List<string>();
        int firstBase = -1, firstCourse = -1;
        // "Surface Layer: 100mm ...", "Base Course: 250mm ...": layer names before a colon on several
        // lines are labels, not headings. (One "PAVEMENT TYPE A: 60mm ..." stays a heading.)
        var rawItems = BuildupParser.SplitItems(t).ToList();
        List<string>? roles = null;
        var courseRoles = new List<string>();
        if (rawItems.Count(i => RoleLabelRegex().IsMatch(StripBullet(i.Trim()))) >= 2)
        {
            roles = rawItems.Select(RoleOf).ToList();
            rawItems = rawItems.Select(StripRoleLabel).ToList();
            notes.Add("Left out the layer names before the colons (e.g. \"Surface Layer:\"); the material is the label.");
        }

        else if (rawItems.Count > 0 && FirstLabelRegex().Match(StripBullet(rawItems[0].Trim())) is { Success: true } label
                 && !HeadingRegex().IsMatch(Mask(StripBullet(rawItems[0].Trim()))))
        {
            // "Asphalt overlay: mill 50mm and replace ..." → title + the rest.
            rawItems[0] = label.Groups["rest"].Value;
            rawItems.Insert(0, label.Groups["head"].Value.Trim() + ":");
        }

        bool first = true;
        for (int r = 0; r < rawItems.Count; r++)
        {
            var raw = rawItems[r];
            var item = FixItem(raw, notes, out var kind, first);
            if (item.Length > 0) first = false;
            if (item.Length == 0)
                continue;
            if (kind == ItemKind.Course) courseRoles.Add(roles?[r] ?? "");
            if (kind == ItemKind.Course && firstCourse < 0) firstCourse = items.Count;
            if (kind == ItemKind.Base && firstBase < 0) firstBase = items.Count;
            items.Add(item);
        }

        // Subgrade first, surface last: written bottom-up.
        if (firstBase >= 0 && firstCourse >= 0 && firstBase < firstCourse)
            bottomUp = true;

        var tidy = string.Join("; ", items.Select(MoveRangeNote));
        var parsed = BuildupParser.ParseBuildup(tidy, errors);
        tidy = RestoreCommas(tidy);
        if (parsed.Name is not null) parsed.Name = RestoreCommas(parsed.Name);
        if (parsed.SubgradeText is not null) parsed.SubgradeText = RestoreCommas(parsed.SubgradeText);
        foreach (var layer in parsed.Layers)
            layer.Description = RestoreCommas(layer.Description);
        for (int i = 0; i < errors.Count; i++)
            errors[i] = RestoreCommas(errors[i]);
        if (parsed.Name is { } name && (GenericHeadingRegex().IsMatch(name) || TotalLineRegex().IsMatch(name)))
            parsed.Name = null;
        if (parsed.Name is { } heading && TitleOnly(heading) is var cut && cut != heading)
        {
            parsed.Name = cut;
            notes.Add($"Used \"{cut}\" as the title (left out the description after it).");
        }
        // The layer names help pick hatches ("Surface Course" → surface asphalt). Only when every course lines up.
        if (roles != null && parsed.Layers.Count == courseRoles.Count)
            for (int i = 0; i < courseRoles.Count; i++)
                parsed.Layers[i].Role = courseRoles[i];
        if (bottomUp && parsed.Layers.Count > 1)
        {
            parsed.Layers.Reverse();
            notes.Add("Written bottom-up: the layers have been put in top-to-bottom order.");
        }

        var result = new SmartReadResult { Parsed = parsed, Text = BottomUpText(parsed, tidy, bottomUp) };
        result.Notes.AddRange(notes.Distinct());
        return result;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ steps ----------------------

    private static string NormaliseCharacters(string s) => MarkdownRegex().Replace(BareThickRegex().Replace(s, "$1mm "), "")
        .Replace('×', 'x').Replace('–', '-').Replace('—', '-').Replace('−', '-')
        .Replace(' ', ' ').Replace('‘', '\'').Replace('’', '\'')
        .Replace('“', '"').Replace('”', '"')
        .Replace("•", "\n- ").Replace("●", "\n- ").Replace("▪", "\n- "); // bullets pasted inline start a new line

    /// <summary>Rows from Excel/Word (tab or | separated): "Pavers | 60" → "60mm Pavers". A header like "Thickness (m)" sets the unit.</summary>
    private static string Tables(string s, List<string> notes)
    {
        var lines = s.Replace("\r", "").Split('\n').Where(l => l.Trim().Length > 0).ToList();
        if (lines.Count(l => l.Contains('\t') || l.Contains('|')) < 2)
            return s;

        double factor = 1;
        int thicknessColumn = -1;
        var rows = new List<string>();
        foreach (var line in lines)
        {
            var all = line.Split('\t', '|').Select(c => c.Trim()).ToList();
            if (all.Count > 0 && all[0].Length == 0) all.RemoveAt(0);          // "| a | b |"
            if (all.Count > 0 && all[^1].Length == 0) all.RemoveAt(all.Count - 1);
            if (all.Count(c => ThicknessHeaderRegex().IsMatch(c)) == 1 && all.All(c => !TableNumberRegex().IsMatch(c)))
                thicknessColumn = all.FindIndex(c => ThicknessHeaderRegex().IsMatch(c));
            var cells = all.Where(c => c.Length > 0 && !EmptyCellRegex().IsMatch(c)).ToList();
            if (cells.Count == 0)
                continue;
            // The thickness: the Thickness column if there's a header, else a number with a unit, else the last number
            // (a first column of 1, 2, 3... is a row number).
            int numeric = thicknessColumn >= 0 && thicknessColumn < all.Count && TableNumberRegex().IsMatch(all[thicknessColumn])
                ? cells.IndexOf(all[thicknessColumn])
                : cells.FindIndex(c => TableUnitNumberRegex().IsMatch(c)) is var u and >= 0 ? u
                : cells.FindLastIndex(c => TableNumberRegex().IsMatch(c));
            if (numeric < 0)
            {
                bool header = cells.Any(c => HeaderWordRegex().IsMatch(c));
                if (header)
                {
                    if (cells.Any(c => MetreHeaderRegex().IsMatch(c))) factor = 1000;
                    else if (cells.Any(c => CentimetreHeaderRegex().IsMatch(c))) factor = 10;
                    continue;
                }
                rows.Add(string.Join(" ", cells)); // e.g. "Subgrade | compacted to 98%" or a title
                continue;
            }

            var m = TableNumberRegex().Match(cells[numeric]);
            double value = double.Parse(m.Groups["v"].Value.Replace(',', '.'), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            string unit = m.Groups["u"].Value.ToLowerInvariant();
            double mm = unit switch { "m" => value * 1000, "cm" => value * 10, "mm" => value, _ => value * factor };
            var description = string.Join(" ", cells.Where((c, i) => i != numeric && !(i == 0 && RowNumberRegex().IsMatch(c))));
            rows.Add($"{Num(mm)}mm {description}".Trim());
        }
        notes.Add("Read the text as a table (one layer per row).");
        return string.Join("; ", rows);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// A numbered or bulleted list (2+ lines starting "1." / "a)" / "-"): each numbered line is an item, and
    /// lines without a number are wrapped text belonging to the item above (or a heading before the list).
    /// </summary>
    private static string Lists(string s)
    {
        var lines = s.Replace("\r", "").Split('\n').Select(l => l.Trim()).Where(l => l.Length > 0).ToList();
        if (lines.Count(l => ListLineRegex().IsMatch(l)) < 2)
            return s;

        var items = new List<string>();
        bool inList = false;
        foreach (var line in lines)
        {
            if (ListLineRegex().IsMatch(line))
            {
                items.Add(ListLineRegex().Replace(line, ""));
                inList = true;
            }
            else if (inList && items.Count > 0)
                items[^1] += " " + line;           // wrapped text
            else
                items.Add(line);                   // heading before the list
        }
        return string.Join("; ", items.Select(i => ProtectCommas(i.Trim().TrimEnd(',', ';'))));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// In a list, a comma inside a line is part of that line's wording ("40mm of loose, decorative gravel"), not a
    /// new layer, unless what follows has its own thickness. Such commas are swapped for <see cref="KeptComma"/>
    /// while reading and put back at the end.
    /// </summary>
    private static string ProtectCommas(string line) =>
        CommaRegex().Replace(line, m =>
        {
            // What follows up to the next comma, without (bracket notes): "(10-20mm sizing)" isn't a new layer.
            var next = BracketGroupRegex().Replace(line[(m.Index + 1)..], " ").Split(',')[0];
            // A new thickness, or a remark ("REFER TO ..."), still starts its own item / label line.
            return ThicknessAheadRegex().IsMatch(next) || RemarkRegex().IsMatch(next.TrimStart().Split(' ')[0]) ? m.Value : KeptComma + " ";
        });

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\([^()]*\)")]
    private static partial Regex BracketGroupRegex();

    private const char KeptComma = '\u201A';

    private static string RestoreCommas(string? s) => s?.Replace(KeptComma, ',') ?? "";

    [GeneratedRegex(@",\s+")]
    private static partial Regex CommaRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\d\s*(?:mm|cm|m|thk|thick)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ThicknessAheadRegex();

    /// <summary>
    /// One layer per line ("Surfacing: 50 mm SMA" / "40mm SMA" on each line): each line is an item even if
    /// some lines contain commas. Not used when brackets wrap across lines (a wrapped note).
    /// </summary>
    private static string Lines(string s)
    {
        if (!s.Contains('\n'))
            return s;
        var lines = s.Replace("\r", "").Split('\n').Select(l => l.Trim()).Where(l => l.Length > 0).ToList();
        if (lines.Any(l => l.Count(c => c == '(') != l.Count(c => c == ')')))
            return s;
        if (lines.Count(l => LineItemRegex().IsMatch(l)) < 2)
            return s;
        return string.Join("; ", lines.Select(l => ProtectCommas(l.TrimEnd(',', ';'))));
    }

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:[-•*]|\(?\d{1,2}[.)]|[a-z][.)])?\s*(?:[^:\d]{2,40}:\s*)?(?:\d+\s*x\s*)?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?\s*(?:mm|cm|m|thk|thick)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex LineItemRegex();

    /// <summary>Sentences and "then / followed by / underlain by" separate layers.</summary>
    private static string Sentences(string s)
    {
        s = OutsideBrackets(s, SentenceEndRegex(), _ => "; ");
        return OutsideBrackets(s, SequenceWordRegex(), _ => "; ");
    }

    private static string Unit(Match m, List<string> notes)
    {
        var value = double.Parse(m.Groups["v"].Value.Replace(',', '.'), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        var unit = m.Groups["u"].Value.ToLowerInvariant();
        double? mm = unit switch
        {
            "mm" or "millimetre" or "millimetres" or "millimeter" or "millimeters" => value,
            "cm" or "centimetre" or "centimetres" or "centimeter" or "centimeters" => value * 10,
            "m" or "metre" or "metres" or "meter" or "meters" => value <= 3 ? value * 1000 : null, // >3 m isn't a pavement layer
            "inch" or "inches" or "\"" => Math.Round(value * 25.4),
            _ => null,
        };
        if (mm is null)
            return m.Value;
        if (!unit.StartsWith("mm") && !unit.StartsWith("millim"))
            notes.Add($"Converted {m.Value.Trim()} to {Num(mm.Value)}mm.");
        return $"{Num(mm.Value)}mm";
    }

    /// <summary>"2 layers of 150mm", "two 150mm layers", "150mm x 2", "300mm in 2 lifts" → "2 x 150mm".</summary>
    private static string Multiples(string s, List<string> notes)
    {
        s = LayersOfRegex().Replace(s, m => Lifts(m.Value, m.Groups["n"].Value, m.Groups["t"].Value, notes));
        s = CountThenThicknessRegex().Replace(s, m => Lifts(m.Value, m.Groups["n"].Value, m.Groups["t"].Value, notes));
        s = ThicknessTimesRegex().Replace(s, m => Lifts(m.Value, m.Groups["n"].Value, m.Groups["t"].Value, notes));
        s = TotalInLayersRegex().Replace(s, m =>
        {
            double total = double.Parse(m.Groups["t"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            int n = int.Parse(m.Groups["n"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            if (n < 1) return m.Value;
            notes.Add($"Read {m.Value.Trim()} as {n} x {Num(total / n)}mm (total split equally, check this).");
            return $"{n} x {Num(total / n)}mm ";
        });
        return s;
    }

    private static string Lifts(string original, string count, string thickness, List<string> notes)
    {
        int n = int.Parse(count, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        if (n < 1) return original;
        var result = $"{n} x {Num(double.Parse(thickness, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))}mm ";
        if (!original.TrimStart().StartsWith(result.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            notes.Add($"Read {original.Trim()} as {result.Trim()}.");
        return result;
    }

    private enum ItemKind { Course, Base, Other }

    /// <summary>One item: thickness to the front, qualifiers into a note, membranes as 0mm, totals dropped.</summary>
    private static string FixItem(string raw, List<string> notes, out ItemKind kind, bool first = false)
    {
        var fixedItem = FixItemCore(raw, notes, out kind, first);
        // A sheet or seal thinner than 2mm is drawn as a line.
        var tiny = TinyThicknessRegex().Match(fixedItem);
        if (tiny.Success && double.Parse(tiny.Groups["t"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) is > 0 and < 2)
        {
            notes.Add($"\"{fixedItem}\" is thinner than 2mm: drawn as a line.");
            fixedItem = "0mm" + fixedItem[tiny.Length..];
        }
        return fixedItem;
    }

    private static string FixItemCore(string raw, List<string> notes, out ItemKind kind, bool first)
    {
        kind = ItemKind.Other;
        var item = StripBullet(raw.Trim()).TrimEnd('.', ';', ',').Trim();
        item = ConnectorRegex().Replace(item, "").Trim(); // "over 150mm FCR" → "150mm FCR"
        item = ArticleRegex().Replace(item, "").Trim();   // "the subgrade" → "subgrade"
        if (item.Length == 0)
            return "";

        // "HEADING: 60mm ..." is read by the parser as name + first course.
        var heading = HeadingRegex().Match(Mask(item));
        if (heading.Success)
        {
            var head = HeadingSeparatorRegex().Replace(item[..heading.Length].Trim(), "").Trim();
            var rest = FixItemCore(item[heading.Length..], notes, out kind, false);
            return rest.Length == 0 ? head + ":" : $"{head}: {rest}";
        }

        var core = BuildupParser.Core(item);
        if (TotalLineRegex().IsMatch(core) && !first)
        {
            notes.Add($"Ignored the total line \"{item}\".");
            return "";
        }
        if (InstructionRegex().IsMatch(core))
        {
            notes.Add($"Skipped \"{item}\" (an instruction, not a layer).");
            return "";
        }
        // "2 coat seal" is a seal, not 2mm.
        if (CoatSealRegex().IsMatch(core))
        {
            kind = ItemKind.Course;
            notes.Add($"Treated \"{item}\" as a seal (drawn as a line).");
            return "0mm " + item;
        }

        // min. / nom. / approx. before a thickness: keep as a note on the label.
        string qualifier = "";
        item = OutsideBrackets(item, QualifierRegex(), m => { qualifier = m.Groups[1].Value.Trim().TrimEnd('.').ToUpperInvariant(); return ""; });
        if (qualifier.Length > 0)
            item = $"{item.Trim()} ({qualifier}.)";

        var lead = LeadingThicknessRegex().Match(StripBullet(item));
        if (lead.Success)
        {
            kind = ItemKind.Course;
            return InLayers(LeadingOfAfterThicknessRegex().Replace(item, "$1 "), notes);
        }

        // Thickness further along the line: move it to the front.
        var masked = Mask(item);
        var tm = ThicknessAnywhereRegex().Match(masked);
        if (tm.Success)
        {
            var before = item[..tm.Index];
            var after = item[(tm.Index + tm.Length)..];
            bool thick = false;
            before = ThicknessWordBeforeRegex().Replace(before, _ => { thick = true; return ""; });
            string? title = null;
            if (LeadInRegex().IsMatch(before))
                before = ""; // "the pavement will be 40mm of SMA" → "40mm SMA"
            else if (LeadInVerbRegex().Match(before) is { Success: true } verb)
            {
                // "Proposed carpark pavement to be 30mm AC10": the words before the verb are the title (first item only).
                if (first && verb.Groups["head"].Value.Trim().Length > 0)
                    title = verb.Groups["head"].Value.Trim().TrimEnd(',', '-', ':').Trim();
                before = "";
            }
            after = LeadingOfRegex().Replace(after, "");
            after = ThicknessWordAfterRegex().Replace(after, _ => { thick = true; return ""; });
            var description = Regex.Replace($"{before.Trim().TrimEnd('-', ':', '=', ',', '@').Trim()} {after.Trim().TrimStart('-', ':', '=', ',').Trim()}", @"\s{2,}", " ").Trim();
            if (description.Length > 0)
            {
                var thickWord = thick ? (IsMostlyUpper(item) ? "THICK " : "thick ") : "";
                var rebuilt = $"{tm.Value.Trim()} {thickWord}{description}";
                notes.Add($"Read \"{item}\" as \"{rebuilt}\".");
                kind = ItemKind.Course;
                var layer = InLayers(rebuilt, notes);
                return title is null ? layer : $"{title}: {layer}";
            }
        }

        // "Basecourse - 150" / "Pavers: 60" (a bare number after a separator).
        var bare = TrailingBareNumberRegex().Match(Mask(item));
        if (bare.Success)
        {
            var desc = item[..bare.Index].Trim();
            var rebuilt = $"{Num(double.Parse(bare.Groups["v"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))}mm {desc}";
            notes.Add($"Read \"{item}\" as \"{rebuilt}\" (number taken as mm).");
            kind = ItemKind.Course;
            return rebuilt;
        }

        var plain = BuildupParser.Core(ConnectorRegex().Replace(item, ""));
        if (BuildupParser.IsBaseNote(plain))
        {
            kind = ItemKind.Base;
            return item;
        }
        if (MembraneRegex().IsMatch(plain) && !LooksLikeRemark(plain))
        {
            notes.Add($"Treated \"{item}\" as a membrane/seal (drawn as a line).");
            kind = ItemKind.Course;
            return "0mm " + ConnectorRegex().Replace(item, "");
        }
        return item;
    }

    private static bool LooksLikeRemark(string s) => RemarkRegex().IsMatch(s);

    /// <summary>
    /// "Cellular Grass Paving (Fire Lanes)An eco-friendly surface ..." → "Cellular Grass Paving (Fire Lanes)":
    /// a heading followed by a description sentence keeps only the heading.
    /// </summary>
    private static string TitleOnly(string name)
    {
        var glued = GluedSentenceRegex().Match(name);           // ")An ..." pasted without a space
        if (glued.Success)
            return name[..(glued.Index + 1)].Trim();
        var described = DescriptionAfterRegex().Match(name);     // "... (Fire Lanes) An eco-friendly ..."
        if (described.Success)
            return name[..described.Index].Trim();
        var sentence = SentenceBreakRegex().Match(name);         // "Heading. A description."
        return sentence.Success ? name[..sentence.Index].Trim() : name;
    }

    /// <summary>"150mm (100-150mm) of crushed stone" → "150mm crushed stone (100-150mm)": the range reads as a note at the end.</summary>
    private static string MoveRangeNote(string item)
    {
        var m = RangeFirstRegex().Match(item);
        return m.Success ? $"{m.Groups["t"].Value} {m.Groups["rest"].Value.Trim()} {m.Groups["r"].Value}" : item;
    }

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\)(?=[A-Z])")]
    private static partial Regex GluedSentenceRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<=\))\s+(?=(?:An?|The|This|Ideal|Suitable|Used|Designed)\s+(?:\S+\s+){3})")]
    private static partial Regex DescriptionAfterRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\.\s+(?=[A-Z])")]
    private static partial Regex SentenceBreakRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?<t>\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*mm)\s*(?<r>\(\d[^()]*mm\))\s+(?:of\s+)?(?<rest>[^()\n].*)$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex RangeFirstRegex();

    /// <summary>"Base Course: 250mm RCC" → "Base Course".</summary>
    private static string RoleOf(string item)
    {
        var trimmed = StripBullet(item.Trim());
        var m = RoleLabelRegex().Match(trimmed);
        return m.Success ? trimmed[..m.Length].Trim().TrimEnd(':').Trim() : "";
    }

    /// <summary>"Base Course: 250mm RCC" → "250mm RCC"; "Subgrade: CBR 5" keeps "Subgrade" (it says what the item is).</summary>
    private static string StripRoleLabel(string item)
    {
        var trimmed = StripBullet(item.Trim());
        var m = RoleLabelRegex().Match(trimmed);
        if (!m.Success)
            return item;
        var label = trimmed[..m.Length].Trim().TrimEnd(':').Trim();
        var rest = trimmed[m.Length..].Trim();
        if (rest.Length == 0)
            return label;
        bool restHasThickness = ThicknessAnywhereRegex().IsMatch(Mask(rest)) || LeadingThicknessRegex().IsMatch(rest);
        // "Geotextile Membrane: heavy-duty fabric ...": the name says it's a membrane, so keep it.
        if (!restHasThickness && MembraneRegex().IsMatch(label) && !MembraneRegex().IsMatch(rest))
            return $"{label} - {rest}";
        if (!restHasThickness && BuildupParser.IsBaseNote(label) && !rest.Contains(label, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return BuildupParser.IsBaseNote(rest) ? $"{label}: {rest}" : $"{label} {rest}";
        // "Binder: 70mm AC20" → "70mm AC20 Binder": a bare material code keeps the layer name, which also picks the hatch.
        var restWords = BuildupParser.Core(ThicknessAnywhereRegex().Replace(Mask(rest), " ")).Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
        if (restHasThickness && restWords.Length <= 2 && !rest.Contains(label, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return $"{rest} {label}";
        return rest;
    }

    /// <summary>"300mm DGB20 compacted in 2 layers" → "2 x 150mm DGB20".</summary>
    private static string InLayers(string item, List<string> notes)
    {
        var phrase = InLayersAnywhereRegex().Match(Mask(item));
        var single = SingleLeadingThicknessRegex().Match(item);
        if (!phrase.Success || !single.Success)
            return item;
        int n = int.Parse(phrase.Groups["n"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        double total = double.Parse(single.Groups["t"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        if (n < 2) return item;
        var rest = (item[single.Length..phrase.Index] + item[(phrase.Index + phrase.Length)..]).Trim().TrimEnd(',').Trim();
        var rebuilt = $"{n} x {Num(total / n)}mm {rest}".Trim();
        notes.Add($"Read \"{item}\" as \"{rebuilt}\" (total split equally, check this).");
        return rebuilt;
    }

    private static bool IsMostlyUpper(string s)
    {
        int upper = s.Count(char.IsUpper), lower = s.Count(char.IsLower);
        return upper >= lower;
    }

    private static string StripBullet(string s) => BulletRegex().Replace(s, "");

    /// <summary>The tidied text, in top-to-bottom order when the original was bottom-up.</summary>
    private static string BottomUpText(BuildupParseResult parsed, string tidy, bool reversed)
    {
        if (!reversed)
            return tidy;
        var b = new Buildup { Name = parsed.Name ?? "PAVEMENT BUILD-UP", Layers = parsed.Layers, SubgradeText = parsed.SubgradeText ?? "SUBGRADE" };
        return BuildupParser.Format(b);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers --------------------

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> NumberWords = new()
    {
        ["one"] = "1", ["two"] = "2", ["three"] = "3", ["four"] = "4", ["five"] = "5", ["six"] = "6",
        ["single"] = "1", ["double"] = "2", ["triple"] = "3",
    };

    private static string Num(double v) => v.ToString("0.##", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    private static string Num(string v) => Num(double.Parse(v.Replace(',', '.'), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));

    /// <summary>Same length as <paramref name="s"/>, with bracketed text blanked so matches inside brackets are ignored.</summary>
    private static string Mask(string s)
    {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(s);
        int depth = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < sb.Length; i++)
        {
            char c = sb[i];
            if (c is '(' or '[') depth++;
            else if (c is ')' or ']') depth = Math.Max(0, depth - 1);
            else if (depth > 0) sb[i] = '_';
        }
        return sb.ToString();
    }

    /// <summary>Regex replace that leaves anything inside (...) or [...] alone.</summary>
    private static string OutsideBrackets(string s, Regex regex, Func<Match, string> replace)
    {
        var masked = Mask(s);
        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        int at = 0;
        foreach (Match m in regex.Matches(masked))
        {
            sb.Append(s, at, m.Index - at);
            // Re-run the match on the original text (from the same start, so look-arounds still see
            // the surrounding text) so groups hold the real characters.
            var real = regex.Match(s, m.Index);
            sb.Append(real.Success && real.Index == m.Index && real.Length == m.Length ? replace(real) : s.Substring(m.Index, m.Length));
            at = m.Index + m.Length;
        }
        sb.Append(s, at, s.Length - at);
        return sb.ToString();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ patterns -------------------

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(?:bottom\s*(?:-|to)\s*top|from\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:bottom|subgrade|formation)\s+up(?:wards)?|upwards)\b\s*:?", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex BottomUpCueRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(?:comprising(?:\s+of)?|consisting\s+of|made\s+up\s+of|constructed\s+(?:of|from|as)|built\s+up\s+as\s+follows|as\s+follows)\s*:?", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex IntroRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:proposed\s+|new\s+|typical\s+)?(?:pavement\s*)?(?:build[\s-]*up|construction|make[\s-]*up|profile|details?|section|specification|spec)?\s*$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex GenericHeadingRegex();

    // ". " ending a sentence (not after min./nom./approx./No./e.g. etc.)
    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<!\b(?:min|nom|approx|no|nos|typ|max|incl|approx|c|e\.g|i\.e|cl|ref))\.\s+(?=[A-Z0-9\[])")]
    private static partial Regex SentenceEndRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\s*\b(?:and\s+then|then|and\s+finally|finally|lastly|next|followed\s+by|underlain\s+by|over\s+which|beneath\s+which\s+is)\b\s*|\s+and\s+(?=\d|(?:replace|overlay|resurface|reinstate|lay|provide|install|place|construct)\b)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex SequenceWordRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(?<v>\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*(?<u>millimet(?:re|er)s?|mm|centimet(?:re|er)s?|cm|met(?:re|er)s?|m|inch(?:es)?|"")(?![\w²³/])", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex UnitRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(one|two|three|four|five|six|single|double|triple)\b(?=\s*(?:x\b|no\.?\b|nos\b|layers?\b|lifts?\b|courses?\b|\d))", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex NumberWordRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:-|to)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*mm\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex RangeRegex();

    // "2 layers of 150mm (thick) (each)", "2 No. lifts @ 150mm"
    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(?<n>\d+)\s*(?:no\.?|nos\.?)?\s*(?:layers?|lifts?|courses?|passes)\s*(?:of|@|at|each\s+of)?\s*(?<t>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*mm(?:\s*(?:thick|thk))?(?:\s*each)?\s*(?:of\s+)?", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex LayersOfRegex();

    // "2 150mm (thick) layers (of)"
    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(?<n>\d)\s+(?<t>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*mm\s+(?:thick\s+)?(?:layers?|lifts?)\s*(?:of\s+)?", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex CountThenThicknessRegex();

    // "150mm x 2 (layers)", "150mm thick x2"
    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(?<t>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*mm(?:\s*(?:thick|thk))?\s*(?:x|\*)\s*(?<n>\d+)(?![\d.]|\s*mm)(?:\s*(?:layers?|lifts?|no\.?))?\s*", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ThicknessTimesRegex();

    // "300mm (thick) (placed/compacted) in 2 (equal) layers", "300mm (2 layers)"
    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(?<t>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*mm(?:\s*(?:thick|thk))?\s*(?:(?:placed|compacted|laid|constructed)\s+)?(?:in\s+|\(\s*)(?<n>\d+)\s*(?:equal\s+)?(?:layers?|lifts?)\s*\)?\s*(?:of\s+)?", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex TotalInLayersRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:-|\d+[.)]|[a-z][.)]|\([a-z0-9]{1,2}\))?\s*(?:\d+\s*x\s*)?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?\s*(?:mm\b|\s+[A-Za-z])", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex LeadingThicknessRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(?:\d+\s*x\s*)?\d+(?:\.\d+)?mm\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ThicknessAnywhereRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\s*\b(?:thickness|thick|thk|depth|deep)\b\s*(?:of|=|:|-|@|at)?\s*$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ThicknessWordBeforeRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*\b(?:thick|thk|deep|depth)\b\.?", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ThicknessWordAfterRegex();

    // "Basecourse - 150", "Pavers: 60", "DGB thickness 150" - but not "CV-TTW-1030" (no space before the dash).
    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?:\s+-|:|=|\s+(?:thickness|thk|depth))\s*(?<v>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex TrailingBareNumberRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(min(?:imum)?\.?|nom(?:inal)?\.?|approx(?:imately)?\.?|typ(?:ical)?\.?|max(?:imum)?\.?)\s*(?=\d)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex QualifierRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\s+(?=(?:laid\s+on|placed\s+on|on\s+top\s+of|onto|upon|over|on)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?(?:layer\s+of\s+)?(?:geotextile|geo-?fabric|geogrid|geocomposite|membrane|dpm|separation\s+layer|filter\s+fabric)\b)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex MembraneConnectorRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?:a|an|the)\s+", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ArticleRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:the\s+)?(?:new\s+|proposed\s+)?(?:pavement|build[\s-]*up|surface|construction|finish)?\s*(?:will\s+be|shall\s+be|is\s+to\s+be|to\s+be|is|be|use|provide|lay|install)?\s*$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex LeadInRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*of\s+", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex LeadingOfRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:total|overall|nett?)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex TotalLineRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(?:geotextile|geo-?fabric|geogrid|geocomposite|membrane|dpm|vapou?r\s+barrier|separation\s+layer|filter\s+fabric|tack\s+coat|prime(?:\s+coat)?|primer\s*seal|bond\s+coat|spray(?:ed)?\s+seal|seal\s+coat)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex MembraneRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(?:refer|see|as\s+per|to\s+suit|tbc|tba|note)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex RemarkRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?:on\s+top\s+of|laid\s+on|bedded\s+on|placed\s+on|onto|upon|over|on)\s+", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ConnectorRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\s+(?=(?:on\s+top\s+of|laid\s+on|bedded\s+on|placed\s+on|onto|upon|over|on)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:compacted\s+|prepared\s+|proof[\s-]?rolled\s+)?(?:subgrade|sub-grade|formation|natural\s+ground|existing|in[\s-]?situ)\b)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex BaseConnectorRegex();

    // "HEADING: 60mm ..." (a heading that doesn't start with a number, then a thickness).
    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?=\D)[^:]{2,80}?(?::|\s+-\s+)\s*(?=(?:\d+\s*x\s*)?\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*mm\b)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex HeadingRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\s*(?::|-)\s*$")]
    private static partial Regex HeadingSeparatorRegex();

    // First item "Title: anything" (title without digits).
    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?<head>[^:\d\[\]()]{2,60}):\s*(?<rest>\S.*)$")]
    private static partial Regex FirstLabelRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\*\*|__|(?m)^\s*#{1,6}\s*|`")]
    private static partial Regex MarkdownRegex();

    // "60 thk", "150 thick" (no unit) → mm.
    [GeneratedRegex(@"(?<![\w.])(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?=(?:thk|thick)\b)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex BareThickRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?<t>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)mm")]
    private static partial Regex TinyThicknessRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:mill|plane|cold\s+plane|remove|excavate|strip|cut\s+out|saw\s*cut|scarify|break\s+out|demolish)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex InstructionRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*\d\s*(?:-|\s)?\s*coat\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex CoatSealRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(\s*(?:\d+\s*x\s*)?\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*mm)\s+of\s+", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex LeadingOfAfterThicknessRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?<head>.*?)\s*\b(?:to\s+be|will\s+be|shall\s+be|is|are|comprises?|consists?\s+of|includes?|use|provide|lay|install|replace\s+with|overlay\s+with|resurface\s+with|reinstate\s+with)\s*:?\s*$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex LeadInVerbRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(?:thickness|depth|thk)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ThicknessHeaderRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^~?\s*\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?\s*(?:mm|cm|m)$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex TableUnitNumberRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\d{1,2}[.)]?$")]
    private static partial Regex RowNumberRegex();

    // A short layer name then a colon, at the start of an item.
    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?=\D)[^:;()\[\]]{2,40}:\s*")]
    private static partial Regex RoleLabelRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\s*,?\s*(?:(?:placed|compacted|laid|constructed)\s+)?in\s+(?<n>\d)\s*(?:equal\s+)?(?:layers?|lifts?)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex InLayersAnywhereRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?<t>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)mm\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex SingleLeadingThicknessRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?:[-/]+|n/?a|nil|none)$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex EmptyCellRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:[-•*]|\(?[a-z0-9]{1,2}[.)])\s+", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex BulletRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\s*(?:[-•*]|\(?(?:\d{1,2}|[a-z])[.)])\s+", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex ListLineRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^~?\s*(?<v>\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*(?<u>mm|cm|m)?$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex TableNumberRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\b(?:thickness|depth|layer|material|description|course|item|mm)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex HeaderWordRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"[(\[]\s*m\s*[)\]]|\bmetres?\b|\bmeters?\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex MetreHeaderRegex();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"[(\[]\s*cm\s*[)\]]|\bcentimet", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)]
    private static partial Regex CentimetreHeaderRegex();
}
