# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/Design/Inputs.cs   (52 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

/// <summary>
/// Reads numbers the way engineers type them: "1e6", "1 x 10^6", "100,000", "5%", "20t" (to kN), "196 kN",
/// "0.5m", "150mm".
/// </summary>
public static partial class Inputs
{
    /// <param name="unit">The unit the value is wanted in ("kN", "tonne", "m", "mm", "%", ...), used to convert t ↔ kN and m ↔ mm.</param>
    public static bool TryRead(string? text, string unit, out double value, out string? error)
    {
        value = 0;
        error = null;
        var s = (text ?? "").Trim().ToLowerInvariant().Replace(" ", "").Replace(",", "").Replace("×", "x");
        if (s.Length == 0) { error = "Enter a value."; return false; }

        var m = NumberRegex().Match(s);
        if (!m.Success) { error = $"\"{text}\" isn't a number."; return false; }
        double number = double.Parse(m.Groups["n"].Value, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        if (m.Groups["p"].Success) number *= Math.Pow(10, int.Parse(m.Groups["p"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        string suffix = m.Groups["u"].Value;
        string want = unit.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();

        value = (suffix, want) switch
        {
            ("" or "%", _) => number,
            ("t" or "tonne" or "tonnes", "kn") => number * 9.81,
            ("kn", "tonne") => number / 9.81,
            ("t" or "tonne" or "tonnes", "tonne") => number,
            ("kn", "kn") => number,
            ("mm", "m") => number / 1000,
            ("m", "mm") => number * 1000,
            ("mm", "mm") or ("m", "m") => number,
            ("mpa", "mpa") or ("kpa", "kpa") or ("esa", "esa") => number,
            ("mpa", "kpa") => number * 1000,
            ("kpa", "mpa") => number / 1000,
            _ => double.NaN,
        };
        if (double.IsNaN(value))
        {
            error = $"\"{suffix}\" can't be used here (this box is in {unit}).";
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?<n>-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:e[+-]?\d+)?)(?:x10\^?(?<p>-?\d+))?(?<u>%|t|tonnes?|kn|mm|m|mpa|kpa|esa)?$")]
    private static partial Regex NumberRegex();
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/Design/FlexibleDesign.cs   (163 lines)
################################################################################

namespace PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

/// <summary>
/// Granular pavement with thin bituminous surfacing (Austroads AGPT02), as in the company workbook
/// "Pavement build-ups": Fig 8.4 (heavily trafficked) and Fig 12.2 (lightly trafficked) give the total
/// granular thickness; the company table gives the AC / DGB20 / DGS20 split.
/// </summary>
public static class FlexibleDesign
{
    /// <summary>Austroads Fig 8.4: (219 − 211·log CBR + 58·log²CBR) · log(DESA / 120), mm.</summary>
    public static double Fig84(double cbr, double desa) => Curve(cbr) * Math.Log10(desa / 120);

    /// <summary>Austroads Fig 12.2 (lightly trafficked): max(100, 0.475 · (…) · log(14 · DESA)), mm.</summary>
    public static double Fig122(double cbr, double desa) => Math.Max(100, 0.475 * Curve(cbr) * Math.Log10(14 * desa));

    private static double Curve(double cbr)
    {
        double l = Math.Log10(cbr);
        return 219 - 211 * l + 58 * l * l;
    }

    /// <summary>Lightly-trafficked chart below 1×10⁵ ESA, otherwise Fig 8.4 (they meet at 1×10⁵).</summary>
    public const double LightTrafficLimit = 1e5;

    /// <summary>Traffic levels (rows) of the company table, with their descriptions.</summary>
    public static readonly (double Desa, string Label, string Description)[] TrafficRows =
    {
        (1e4, "1 x 10^4", "Minor road, infrequent heavy vehicles"),
        (5e4, "5 x 10^4", "Minor road with occasional heavy vehicles"),
        (1e5, "1 x 10^5", "Local access, infrequent heavy vehicles (SI EFSG min. for vehicular traffic)"),
        (5e5, "5 x 10^5", "Local access with occasional heavy vehicles (SI EFSG min. for buses & trucks)"),
        (1e6, "1 x 10^6", "Collector road with heavy vehicles"),
        (5e6, "5 x 10^6", "Main road (liaise with the relevant authorities)"),
        (1e7, "1 x 10^7", "Main road (liaise with the relevant authorities)"),
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// Company table (Pavement Design Table Updated): DGB20 base and DGS20 subbase (mm) by traffic row and
    /// CBR 1..10 %. Surfacing: 40 AC10 up to 5×10⁵ ESA, 50 AC14 above.
    /// </summary>
    private static readonly int[,] Base =
    {
        { 150, 150, 150, 140, 120, 100, 100, 100, 100, 100 },
        { 150, 150, 150, 150, 130, 100, 100, 100, 100, 100 },
        { 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 115, 120, 100, 100, 100 },
        { 200, 200, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 125, 125, 120 },
        { 200, 200, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 140, 130 },
        { 200, 200, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150 },
        { 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 160, 160, 160 },
    };

    private static readonly int[,] Subbase =
    {
        { 385, 245, 175, 140, 125, 120, 105, 100, 100, 100 },
        { 460, 300, 215, 165, 150, 150, 130, 110, 100, 100 },
        { 490, 320, 235, 180, 150, 150, 120, 125, 110, 100 },
        { 595, 385, 330, 260, 215, 180, 150, 150, 130, 120 },
        { 660, 430, 370, 295, 245, 205, 175, 150, 140, 130 },
        { 815, 545, 460, 375, 315, 265, 230, 200, 180, 155 },
        { 880, 595, 450, 360, 295, 245, 205, 215, 190, 165 },
    };

    /// <summary>Designs the pavement for a subgrade CBR (%) and design traffic (ESA).</summary>
    public static FlexibleResult Design(double cbr, double desa)
    {
        if (!(cbr > 0)) throw new ArgumentException("Enter a subgrade CBR greater than 0%.");
        if (!(desa >= 1000)) throw new ArgumentException("Enter the design traffic in ESA (at least 1,000).");

        double fig84 = Fig84(cbr, desa), fig122 = Fig122(cbr, desa);
        bool light = desa < LightTrafficLimit;
        double required = light ? fig122 : fig84;
        var notes = new List<string>();

        // Company table: the next traffic row up and the next CBR column down (both on the safe side).
        int row = Array.FindIndex(TrafficRows, r => r.Desa >= desa * 0.999);
        int col = (int)Math.Clamp(Math.Floor(cbr + 1e-9), 1, 10) - 1;
        int baseMm, subbaseMm;
        string surfacing;
        if (row >= 0)
        {
            baseMm = Base[row, col];
            subbaseMm = Subbase[row, col];
            surfacing = TrafficRows[row].Desa <= 5e5 ? "40 AC10" : "50 AC14";
            notes.Add($"Company table: {TrafficRows[row].Label} ESA row, CBR {col + 1}% column ({TrafficRows[row].Description}).");
            if (cbr > 10) notes.Add("CBR above 10%: the CBR 10% column is used (conservative).");
            if (cbr < 1) notes.Add("CBR below 1%: the CBR 1% column is used - treat the subgrade first.");
        }
        else
        {
            // Beyond the table: Austroads total, DGB 200 and the rest as subbase.
            surfacing = "50 AC14";
            baseMm = 200;
            subbaseMm = Math.Max(100, RoundUp5(required) - baseMm);
            notes.Add("Design traffic is above the company table (1 x 10^7 ESA): total from Austroads Fig 8.4, base 200 mm. Liaise with the relevant authority.");
        }

        int granular = baseMm + subbaseMm;
        if (granular + 0.5 < required)
        {
            // Never less than Austroads: add the shortfall to the subbase.
            int add = RoundUp5(required) - granular;
            subbaseMm += add;
            granular += add;
            notes.Add($"Subbase increased by {add} mm to meet the Austroads thickness.");
        }
        if (cbr < 3) notes.Add("Subgrade stabilisation is generally required for CBR < 3%.");
        notes.Add("Granular totals do not include the AC thickness (Austroads allows it to be included).");
        notes.Add("Granular layers over 150 mm are placed in 100-150 mm layers.");

        return new FlexibleResult(cbr, desa, fig84, fig122, required, light, surfacing, baseMm, subbaseMm, granular, notes);
    }

    private static int RoundUp5(double mm) => (int)(Math.Ceiling(mm / 5 - 1e-9) * 5);

    /// <summary>The design as a worked calculation sheet, with the build-up for the detail.</summary>
    public static CalcResult Calc(double cbr, double desa, string? title = null)
    {
        var d = Design(cbr, desa);
        var r = new CalcResult
        {
            Title = "Flexible pavement - granular with thin bituminous surfacing",
            Reference = "Austroads AGPT02 Fig 8.4 / Fig 12.2; company design table",
        };
        r.In("Subgrade CBR", cbr, "%");
        r.In("Design traffic DESA", desa, "ESA", "0.##E+0");
        r.Step("fig84", "Fig 8.4  t = (219 − 211 log CBR + 58 log²CBR) log(DESA/120)", d.Fig84, "mm", "0.0", "heavily trafficked");
        r.Step("fig122", "Fig 12.2 t = max(100, 0.475 (…) log(14 DESA))", d.Fig122, "mm", "0.0", "lightly trafficked");
        r.Result("required", d.LightlyTrafficked ? "Required granular thickness (Fig 12.2, DESA < 1x10^5)" : "Required granular thickness (Fig 8.4)", d.Required, "mm");
        r.Result("ac", "Surfacing " + d.AcType, d.AcMm, "mm", "0");
        r.Result("base", "DGB20 base", d.BaseMm, "mm", "0");
        r.Result("subbase", "DGS20/40 subbase", d.SubbaseMm, "mm", "0");
        r.Result("granular", "Granular total (base + subbase)", d.GranularMm, "mm", "0");
        r.Check("Granular total ≥ Austroads", d.GranularMm + 0.5 >= d.Required,
            $"{d.GranularMm} vs {CalcResult.Num(d.Required, "0")} mm");
        foreach (var n in d.Notes) r.Notes.Add(n);
        r.ThicknessMm = d.AcMm + d.GranularMm;
        r.Headline = $"{d.Surfacing} / {d.BaseMm} DGB20 / {d.SubbaseMm} DGS20 = {d.GranularMm} mm granular (Austroads {CalcResult.Num(d.Required, "0")} mm)";
        r.BuildupText = d.BuildupText(title);
        return r;
    }
}

public sealed record FlexibleResult(
    double Cbr, double Desa, double Fig84, double Fig122, double Required, bool LightlyTrafficked,
    string Surfacing, int BaseMm, int SubbaseMm, int GranularMm, IReadOnlyList<string> Notes)
{
    public int AcMm => int.Parse(Surfacing.Split(' ')[0]);
    public string AcType => Surfacing.Split(' ')[1];

    /// <summary>The build-up in the company's specification wording, ready for the detail.</summary>
    public string BuildupText(string? title = null)
    {
        string cbr = Cbr.ToString("0.#", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        var lines = new List<string>();
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title)) lines.Add($"[{title}]");
        lines.Add($"{AcMm}mm COMPACTED THICKNESS {AcType} WEARING COURSE");
        lines.Add("PRIME");
        lines.Add($"{BaseMm}mm COMPACTED THICKNESS DGB20 CLASS 1 BASE TO 98% MMDD AT ±2% OMC");
        lines.Add($"{SubbaseMm}mm COMPACTED THICKNESS DGS20/40 SUBBASE TO 98% MMDD AT ±2% OMC");
        lines.Add($"SUBGRADE MINIMUM CBR {cbr}% COMPACTED TO 98% SMDD AT ±2% OMC");
        return string.Join("\n", lines);
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/Design/ConcreteDesign.cs   (446 lines)
################################################################################

namespace PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

/// <summary>
/// Concrete industrial floors and pavements, ported formula for formula from the company workbook
/// "T48 and T34 Industrial Pavement Design" (Cement &amp; Concrete Services, v1.4, 2007): CCAA T48-1999 chart
/// fits for wheels, posts and UDL, AS3600 punching shear, and the Westergaard / Ioannides / Pickett checks
/// of T34-1985. Working-strength (unfactored) loads unless stated.
/// </summary>
public static class ConcreteDesign
{
    public const string T48 = "CCAA T48-1999";
    public const string T34 = "CCAA T34-1985";

    /// <summary>Concrete grades the T48 charts cover.</summary>
    public static readonly double[] Grades = { 25, 32, 40, 50 };

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ soil ------------------

    /// <summary>Modulus of subgrade reaction k (kPa/mm) from CBR (sheet 4).</summary>
    public static double KFromCbr(double cbr) => cbr < 20 ? 3.99 + 21.3 * Math.Log(cbr) : 23.7 + 2.18 * cbr;

    /// <summary>Long-term soil modulus Es (MPa) from CBR, T48 Fig 1.15 (sheets 4-6).</summary>
    public static double EsFromCbr(double cbr) =>
        cbr <= 0 ? 0 : cbr < 30 ? 11.57 * Math.Log(cbr) + 1.642 : 57.401 * Math.Log(cbr) - 151.38;

    /// <summary>Es (MPa) from k: 0.54 (k − 3.99), CBR &lt; 20.</summary>
    public static double EsFromK(double k) => 0.54 * (k - 3.99);

    /// <summary>k from Es: 1.84 Es + 3.99, CBR &lt; 20.</summary>
    public static double KFromEs(double es) => 1.84 * es + 3.99;

    /// <summary>CBR from Es: e^((Es − 1.64) / 11.57), CBR &lt; 20.</summary>
    public static double CbrFromEs(double es) => Math.Exp((es - 1.64) / 11.57);

    public static CalcResult Soil(double cbr)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = "Soil: CBR vs k vs Es", Reference = "Sheet 4; " + T48 + " Fig 1.15" };
        r.In("CBR", cbr, "%");
        if (!(cbr > 0)) throw new ArgumentException("Enter a CBR greater than 0%.");
        double k = r.Result("k", "Modulus of subgrade reaction k (760 mm plate)", KFromCbr(cbr), "kPa/mm", "0.0");
        double es = r.Result("es", "Soil modulus Es (long term)", EsFromCbr(cbr), "MPa", "0.0");
        r.Step("esk", "Es from k  = 0.54 (k − 3.99)", EsFromK(k), "MPa", "0.0");
        r.Headline = $"k = {CalcResult.Num(k, "0.0")} kPa/mm, Es = {CalcResult.Num(es, "0.0")} MPa";
        r.Notes.Add("k depends on the plate size: if in doubt use the 760 mm plate value (the smaller k).");
        return r;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ common ----------------

    /// <summary>Flexural strength 0.7 √f'c (MPa).</summary>
    public static double Fcf(double fc) => 0.7 * Math.Sqrt(fc);

    /// <summary>Load repetitions factor k2: 0.73 − 0.0846 (log N − 3), 0.5 for N ≥ 400,000.</summary>
    public static double K2(double n) => n <= 399999 ? 0.73 - 0.0846 * (Math.Log10(n) - 3) : 0.5;

    /// <summary>Concrete calibration factor k4 by grade.</summary>
    public static double K4(double fc) => fc switch
    {
        20 => 1.03, 25 => 1.07, 32 => 1.12, 40 => 1.16, 50 => 1.2,
        _ => throw new ArgumentException("f'c must be 25, 32, 40 or 50 MPa (the T48 charts)."),
    };

    /// <summary>Repetitions from a reckoner: passes per day x 5 days x 52 weeks x years.</summary>
    public static double Repetitions(double perDay, double years) => perDay * 5 * 52 * years;

    private static double RoundUp5(double mm) => Math.Ceiling(mm / 5 - 1e-9) * 5;

    private static void Thickness(CalcResult r, double t, double min, string what)
    {
        double adopt = Math.Max(min, RoundUp5(t));
        r.ThicknessMm = adopt;
        r.Values["adopt"] = adopt;
        r.Lines.Add(new CalcLine("Adopt (rounded up to 5 mm, min " + CalcResult.Num(min) + ")", CalcResult.Num(adopt, "0"), "mm", LineKind.Ok));
        r.Headline = $"{what}: {CalcResult.Num(t, "0.0")} mm required, adopt {CalcResult.Num(adopt, "0")} mm";
        r.Pass = true;
    }

    /// <summary>Linear interpolation through (x, y) points; null outside the range.</summary>
    private static double? Interpolate(IReadOnlyList<(double X, double Y)> pts, double x)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < pts.Count - 1; i++)
            if (x >= pts[i].X - 1e-9 && x <= pts[i + 1].X + 1e-9)
                return pts[i].Y + (x - pts[i].X) / (pts[i + 1].X - pts[i].X) * (pts[i + 1].Y - pts[i].Y);
        return null;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ wheels (T48 charts 1.1 / 1.2) -----

    /// <summary>Wheel loads, interior (sheet 6) or edge (sheet 7): thickness for an axle load from T48 Chart 1.1 / 1.2.</summary>
    public static CalcResult Wheel(bool edge, double fc, double k1, double passesPerDay, double years, double k3,
        double wheelSpacing, double soilDepth, double es, double axleLoadKn)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult
        {
            Title = edge ? "Wheel loading - edge" : "Wheel loading - interior",
            Reference = T48 + (edge ? " Chart 1.2" : " Chart 1.1"),
        };
        r.In("f'c concrete", fc, "MPa");
        r.In("k1 material factor", k1);
        r.In("Passes per day", passesPerDay);
        r.In("Years", years);
        r.In("k3 geotechnical calibration", k3);
        r.In("S wheel spacing", wheelSpacing, "m");
        r.In("H soil depth", soilDepth, "m");
        r.In("E soil modulus", es, "MPa");
        r.In("P axle load", axleLoadKn, "kN");

        double n = r.Step("n", "N repetitions = passes x 5 x 52 x years", Repetitions(passesPerDay, years), "", "0");
        double k2 = r.Step("k2", "k2 repetitions factor", K2(n));
        double k4 = r.Step("k4", "k4 concrete calibration", K4(fc));
        double fcf = r.Step("fcf", "f'cf = 0.7 √f'c", Fcf(fc), "MPa");
        double fall = r.Step("fall", "f all = k1 k2 f'cf", k1 * k2 * fcf, "MPa");
        double s = wheelSpacing, h = soilDepth, e = es;
        double fs = r.Step("fs", edge ? "FS2 (wheel spacing)" : "FS1 (wheel spacing)", edge
            ? 0.013 * s * s * s - 0.1186 * s * s + 0.4131 * s + 0.6078
            : 0.0095 * s * s * s - 0.1001 * s * s + 0.4102 * s + 0.5806);
        double fh = r.Step("fh", edge ? "FH2 (soil depth)" : "FH1 (soil depth)", edge
            ? (h < 7 ? -0.0023 * Math.Pow(h, 3) + 0.0353 * h * h - 0.1796 * h + 1.295 : 0.99389 - 0.000556 * h)
            : (h < 7 ? 0.0000007 * Math.Pow(h, 6) - 0.00004 * Math.Pow(h, 5) + 0.0009 * Math.Pow(h, 4) - 0.0108 * Math.Pow(h, 3) + 0.0712 * h * h - 0.2495 * h + 1.3675 : 0.9972 - 0.001389 * h));
        double fe = r.Step("fe", edge ? "FE2 (soil modulus)" : "FE1 (soil modulus)", edge
            ? (e <= 50 ? 0.5284 * Math.Pow(e, 0.2696) : e < 75 ? -0.00005 * e * e + 0.0095 * e + 1.1507 : 1.71 + Math.Sin((e - 105) * 2 * 3.142 / 180) * (1.88 - 1.71))
            : (e <= 50 ? 0.6277 * Math.Pow(e, 0.203) : e < 75 ? -0.00006 * e * e + 0.0103 * e + 1.03 : 1.57 + Math.Sin((e - 105) * 2 * 3.142 / 180) * (1.675 - 1.57)));
        double f = r.Step("f", edge ? "F2 = f all k3 k4 FS2 FH2 FE2" : "F1 = f all k3 k4 FS1 FH1 FE1", fall * k3 * k4 * fs * fh * fe);
        if (f > 5)
        {
            r.Warn($"Stress factor {CalcResult.Num(f, "0.00")} is above the chart range (0-5).");
            r.Pass = false;
            r.Headline = "Outside the T48 chart (stress factor > 5)";
            return r;
        }

        // Thickness curves at 100, 200, 400 and 600 kN; straight lines between (and down to 50 kN).
        double F(double c6, double c5, double c4, double c3, double c2, double c1, double c0) =>
            c6 * Math.Pow(f, 6) + c5 * Math.Pow(f, 5) + c4 * Math.Pow(f, 4) + c3 * Math.Pow(f, 3) + c2 * f * f + c1 * f + c0;
        double t100, t200, t400, t600;
        if (edge)
        {
            t100 = F(0.0124, -1.0545, 17.187, -122.63, 456.11, -932.52, 1129.1);
            t200 = F(-0.7542, 15.62, -130.09, 546.74, -1167.5, 989.75, 451.4);
            t400 = F(0, 0, -3.0634, 51.288, -304.17, 670.99, 158.01);
            t600 = -75.909 * f + 882.05;
        }
        else
        {
            t100 = F(0.8791, -16.187, 119.59, -456.65, 975.1, -1194.3, 920.95);
            t200 = F(-0.1126, 1.9127, -10.568, 7.4724, 138.42, -554.82, 954.72);
            t400 = F(0.0749, -0.4369, -5.5592, 60.51, -187.77, 68.883, 735.13);
            t600 = F(-0.7298, 16.878, -158.56, 769.75, -2006.4, 2504.1, -472.39);
        }
        double t50 = t100 - 50 * ((t200 - t100) / 100);
        var curve = new List<(double, double)> { (50, t50), (100, t100), (200, t200), (400, t400), (600, t600) };
        foreach (var load in new double[] { 50, 75, 100, 150, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600 })
            r.Step("t" + load, $"  t for {load} kN axle", Interpolate(curve, load)!.Value, "mm", "0.0");

        if (Interpolate(curve, axleLoadKn) is not { } t)
        {
            r.Warn("Axle load outside the chart range (50-600 kN). Read the table above.");
            r.Pass = false;
            r.Headline = "Axle load outside 50-600 kN";
            return r;
        }
        r.Result("t", $"Thickness for P = {CalcResult.Num(axleLoadKn)} kN", t, "mm");
        Thickness(r, t, 130, edge ? "Edge" : "Interior");
        r.Notes.Add("Minimum thickness 130 mm; allow for (−) construction tolerances (T48 Table 1.14).");
        r.Notes.Add("10 kN = 1 tonne. Repetitions assume 5 working days a week; over 400,000 is unlimited.");
        return r;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ posts (T48 chart 1.3) ---------

    /// <summary>Post (rack) loads, interior (sheet 8) or edge (sheet 10).</summary>
    public static CalcResult Post(bool edge, double fc, double k1, double k2, double spacingX, double spacingY,
        double soilDepth, double es, double postLoadKn)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = edge ? "Post loading - edge" : "Post loading - interior", Reference = T48 + " Chart 1.3" };
        r.In("f'c concrete", fc, "MPa");
        r.In("k1 material factor", k1);
        r.In("k2 repetitions factor", k2);
        r.In("x post spacing", spacingX, "m");
        r.In("y post spacing", spacingY, "m");
        r.In("H soil depth", soilDepth, "m");
        r.In("E soil modulus", es, "MPa");
        r.In("P post load (unfactored)", postLoadKn, "kN");

        double s = r.Step("s", "S average spacing", (spacingX + spacingY) / 2, "m");
        double fcf = r.Step("fcf", "f'cf = 0.7 √f'c", Fcf(fc), "MPa");
        double fall = r.Step("fall", "f all = k1 k2 f'cf", k1 * k2 * fcf, "MPa");
        double fs = r.Step("fs", "FS3 (spacing)", -0.058 * Math.Pow(s, 5) + 0.5755 * Math.Pow(s, 4) - 2.213 * Math.Pow(s, 3) + 3.9613 * s * s - 2.7045 * s + 1.1595);
        double h = soilDepth, e = es;
        double fh = r.Step("fh", "FH3 (soil depth)", h < 7 ? -0.0059 * h * h * h + 0.0898 * h * h - 0.4489 * h + 1.7518 : 1.0044 - 0.00278 * h);
        double fe = r.Step("fe", "FE3 (soil modulus)", edge
            ? (e <= 50 ? 0.446 * Math.Pow(e, 0.2876) : 0.00267 * e + 1.243)
            : (e < 55 ? 0.408 * Math.Pow(e, 0.33) : 0.004 * e + 1.285));
        double f = r.Step("f", "F3 = 1000 (f all / P) FS3 FH3 FE3", 1000 * (fall / postLoadKn) * fs * fh * fe);
        if (f > 130)
        {
            r.Warn("Stress factor F3 is above the chart range (10-130).");
            r.Pass = false;
            r.Headline = "Outside the T48 chart (F3 > 130)";
            return r;
        }
        double t = r.Result("t", "Thickness", edge
            ? (f <= 50 ? 0.0002 * f * f * f + 0.011 * f * f - 9.9689 * f + 856.23 : 0.0211 * f * f - 6.957 * f + 703.61)
            : (f <= 50 ? -0.0023 * f * f * f + 0.4227 * f * f - 29.445 * f + 933.74 : 7409.9 * Math.Pow(f, -0.8851)), "mm");
        if (f < 10) r.Warn("F3 is below the chart range (10-130): check the result.");
        Thickness(r, t, 130, edge ? "Post edge" : "Post interior");
        if (!edge)
            r.Notes.Add("T48 uses k2 = 1 for posts; use the wheel k2 (e.g. 0.5 for N > 400,000) if racks are loaded and unloaded often.");
        r.Notes.Add("Extreme rack layouts may not be the worst case: use an industrial-floor FEM program for those.");
        return r;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ punching shear (AS3600) -------

    /// <summary>Punching shear at an interior post (sheet 9, AS3600-2001 9.2.3).</summary>
    public static CalcResult PunchingShear(double fc, double basePlateMm, double slabMm, double factoredLoadKn)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = "Punching shear - interior post", Reference = "AS3600-2001 cl. 9.2.3" };
        r.In("f'c concrete", fc, "MPa");
        r.In("b base plate width", basePlateMm, "mm");
        r.In("t pavement thickness", slabMm, "mm");
        r.In("P* factored load (e.g. 1.2G + 1.5Q)", factoredLoadKn, "kN");
        double fcv = r.Step("fcv", "fcv = 0.34 √f'c", 0.34 * Math.Sqrt(fc), "MPa");
        double u = r.Step("u", "u = 4 (b + d), d = 0.9 t", 4 * (basePlateMm + 0.9 * slabMm), "mm", "0");
        double cap = r.Result("phiVuo", "φVuo = 0.7 u d fcv", 0.7 * (u * 0.9 * slabMm * fcv) / 1000, "kN", "0.0");
        bool ok = factoredLoadKn < cap;
        r.Check("φVuo ≥ P*", ok, $"{CalcResult.Num(cap, "0.0")} vs {CalcResult.Num(factoredLoadKn, "0.0")} kN");
        r.Headline = ok ? $"OK for punching shear (φVuo {CalcResult.Num(cap, "0")} kN > P* {CalcResult.Num(factoredLoadKn, "0")} kN)"
                        : $"FAILS in punching shear (φVuo {CalcResult.Num(cap, "0")} kN ≤ P* {CalcResult.Num(factoredLoadKn, "0")} kN)";
        r.Notes.Add("Assumes the full perimeter: a post closer than d to an edge or corner must be checked by hand.");
        return r;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ UDL (T48 chart 1.4) -----------

    /// <summary>Thickness under a uniformly distributed load (sheet 11).</summary>
    public static CalcResult Udl(double fc, double k1, double repetitions, double aisleWidth, double soilDepth, double es, double udlKpa)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = "Uniformly distributed load (UDL) - thickness", Reference = T48 + " Chart 1.4" };
        r.In("f'c concrete", fc, "MPa");
        r.In("k1 material factor", k1);
        r.In("N repetitions (0 = no calc)", repetitions, "", "0");
        r.In("W stack or aisle width", aisleWidth, "m");
        r.In("H soil depth", soilDepth, "m");
        r.In("E soil modulus", es, "MPa");
        r.In("p UDL", udlKpa, "kPa");
        double n = repetitions;
        double k2 = r.Step("k2", "k2 repetitions factor", n <= 0 ? 0.75 : n <= 399999 ? 0.73 - 0.0846 * (Math.Log10(n) - 3) : 0.5);
        double fcf = r.Step("fcf", "f'cf = 0.7 √f'c", Fcf(fc), "MPa");
        double fall = r.Step("fall", "f all = k1 k2 f'cf", k1 * k2 * fcf, "MPa");
        double w = aisleWidth, h = soilDepth, e = es;
        double fs = r.Step("fs", "FS4 (width)", w < 3
            ? 0.0039 * Math.Pow(w, 6) - 0.0776 * Math.Pow(w, 5) + 0.6244 * Math.Pow(w, 4) - 2.6195 * Math.Pow(w, 3) + 6.0877 * w * w - 7.6425 * w + 5.2165
            : 0.0147 * Math.Pow(w, 4) - 0.2364 * Math.Pow(w, 3) + 1.4536 * w * w - 4.0648 * w + 5.2443);
        double fh = r.Step("fh", "FH4 (soil depth)", h < 8 ? 0.00191 * Math.Pow(h, 4) - 0.0422 * Math.Pow(h, 3) + 0.347 * h * h - 1.2849 * h + 2.8298 : -0.005 * h + 0.98);
        double fe = r.Step("fe", "FE4 (soil modulus)", e < 50 ? 0.2421 * Math.Pow(e, 0.5321) : 0.0235 * e + 0.8235);
        double f = r.Step("f", "F4 = 1000 (f all / p) FS4 FH4 FE4", 1000 * (fall / udlKpa) * fs * fh * fe);
        if (f > 100)
        {
            r.Warn("F4 is above the chart range (30-100): the thickness is under 125 mm.");
            r.Pass = false;
            r.Headline = "Outside the T48 chart (F4 > 100)";
            return r;
        }
        double raw = f < 83 ? -417.76 * Math.Log(f) + 2115 : -0.2418 * f * f + 36.3 * f - 1087.1;
        double t = r.Result("t", "Thickness", Math.Max(120, raw), "mm");
        if (f < 30) r.Warn("F4 is below the chart range (30-100): check the result.");
        Thickness(r, t, 125, "UDL");
        return r;
    }

    /// <summary>Ec (MPa) for deflection, AS3600 draft 2005 values used in sheet 12.</summary>
    public static double EcForDeflection(double fc) => fc switch
    {
        <= 25 => 26700, <= 32 => 30100, <= 40 => 32800, <= 50 => 34800, _ => 37400,
    };

    /// <summary>Deflection under a UDL (sheet 12, T48 eqn 9).</summary>
    public static CalcResult UdlDeflection(double ec, double slabMm, double udlKpa, double width, double vSoil, double cbr, double soilDepth)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = "Uniformly distributed load (UDL) - deflection", Reference = T48 + " eqn 9 (3.4.14)" };
        r.In("Ec concrete modulus", ec, "MPa", "0");
        r.In("t pavement thickness", slabMm, "mm");
        r.In("p UDL", udlKpa, "kPa");
        r.In("W loaded width", width, "m");
        r.In("vs soil Poisson's ratio", vSoil);
        r.In("CBR", cbr, "%");
        r.In("H soil depth", soilDepth, "m");
        double es = r.Step("es", "Es long term (Fig 1.15)", cbr <= 0 ? 0 : cbr <= 20 ? 10.781 * Math.Log(cbr) + 2.7034 : 58.27 * Math.Log(cbr) - 155.02, "MPa");
        double lc = r.Step("lc", "Lc = t [Ec / (Es (1 − vs²))]^0.33", slabMm * Math.Pow(ec / (es * (1 - vSoil * vSoil)), 0.33) / 1000, "m");
        double S(double ws) => udlKpa * width * (1 - vSoil * vSoil) * ws / es;
        double w25 = lc <= 10 ? -0.0004 * lc * lc * lc + 0.0147 * lc * lc - 0.166 * lc + 0.8685 : 0.23;
        double w5 = lc < 8 ? 0.000008 * Math.Pow(lc, 5) - 0.0003 * Math.Pow(lc, 4) + 0.0039 * lc * lc * lc - 0.0103 * lc * lc - 0.1499 * lc + 1.2588 : 0.0025 * lc * lc - 0.0628 * lc + 0.7656;
        double w10 = lc < 8 ? -0.0006 * lc * lc * lc + 0.0209 * lc * lc - 0.2653 * lc + 1.6968 : 0.0018 * lc * lc - 0.0504 * lc + 0.9064;
        double w20 = lc < 8 ? -0.0005 * lc * lc * lc + 0.0199 * lc * lc - 0.261 * lc + 1.8684 : 0.003 * lc * lc - 0.0801 * lc + 1.2393;
        var curve = new List<(double, double)> { (2.5, S(w25)), (5, S(w5)), (10, S(w10)), (20, S(w20)) };
        foreach (var depth in new[] { 2.5, 3.75, 5, 7.5, 10, 15, 20 })
            r.Step("s" + CalcResult.Num(depth), $"  s for H = {CalcResult.Num(depth)} m", Interpolate(curve, depth)!.Value, "mm", "0.00");
        if (Interpolate(curve, soilDepth) is not { } s)
        {
            r.Warn("Soil depth outside 2.5-20 m. Read the table above.");
            r.Headline = "Soil depth outside 2.5-20 m";
            return r;
        }
        r.Result("s", $"Deflection s = p W (1 − vs²) ws / Es at H = {CalcResult.Num(soilDepth)} m", s, "mm", "0.00");
        r.Headline = $"Deflection {CalcResult.Num(s, "0.0")} mm";
        return r;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ T34 closed-form checks -------

    public enum WheelPosition { Interior, NearEdge, OnEdge, Corner }

    /// <summary>
    /// Wheel stress check at a chosen slab thickness: Westergaard interior (sheet 13), Ioannides near the edge
    /// (14) or on the edge (15), Pickett corner (16). Also finds the least thickness (5 mm steps) that passes.
    /// </summary>
    public static CalcResult WheelStress(WheelPosition where, double axleTonnes, double cbr, double fc, double ninetyDayFactor,
        double poisson, double slabMm, double tyreKpa, double repetitions)
    {
        var (title, reference, estimateFactor) = where switch
        {
            WheelPosition.Interior => ("Wheel stress - interior (Westergaard)", T34 + ", Westergaard", 1.1),
            WheelPosition.NearEdge => ("Wheel stress - close to edge (Ioannides)", "TRB 1043-1985, Ioannides", 1.66),
            WheelPosition.OnEdge => ("Wheel stress - on the edge (Ioannides)", T34 + ", Ioannides", 1.8),
            _ => ("Wheel stress - on the corner (Pickett)", "PCA 1951, Pickett", 1.6),
        };
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = title, Reference = reference };
        r.In("P single axle", axleTonnes, "tonne");
        r.In("CBR", cbr, "%");
        r.In("f'c concrete", fc, "MPa");
        r.In("90-day flexural factor (1 or 1.1)", ninetyDayFactor);
        r.In("v Poisson's ratio (concrete)", poisson);
        r.In("h slab thickness chosen", slabMm, "mm");
        r.In("p tyre pressure", tyreKpa, "kPa");
        r.In("N repetitions", repetitions, "", "0");

        double pw = r.Step("pw", "Pw = P x 9.81 / 2", 0.5 * axleTonnes * 9.81, "kN");
        double k = r.Step("k", "k = 3.99 + 21.3 ln(CBR)", 3.99 + 21.3 * Math.Log(cbr, 2.71828), "kPa/mm");
        double fcf = r.Step("fcf", "f'cf = MF x 0.7 √f'c", ninetyDayFactor * Fcf(fc), "MPa");
        double ec = r.Step("ec", "Ec (AS3600 draft 2005 fit)", -8.0907 * fc * fc + 927.7 * fc + 8644.8, "MPa", "0");
        r.Step("hest", $"h estimate = {CalcResult.Num(estimateFactor)} √(Pw x 1000 / (0.5 f'cf))", estimateFactor * Math.Sqrt(pw * 1000 / (0.5 * fcf)), "mm", "0");
        double ff = r.Step("ff", "Fatigue factor 0.73 − 0.0846 (log N − 3)", repetitions < 400000 ? 0.73 - 0.0846 * (Math.Log10(repetitions) - 3) : 0.5);
        double fall = r.Step("fall", "f'cf allowable = FF x f'cf", ff * fcf, "MPa");

        // The stress at a thickness (the sheet's formulas, including its characteristic-length expression).
        (double L, double B, double Stress) At(double h)
        {
            double l = Math.Pow(ec * h * h * h / (12 * (1 - Math.Pow(poisson, 12)) * k / 1000), 0.25);
            double area = pw / tyreKpa * 1_000_000;
            double radius = Math.Sqrt(area / 3.142);
            double b = radius > 1.72 * h ? radius : Math.Sqrt(1.6 * radius * radius + h * h) - 0.675 * h;
            double ratio = l / b;
            double stress = where switch
            {
                WheelPosition.Interior => 0.316 * (pw * 1000 / (h * h)) * (4 * Math.Log10(ratio) + 1.069),
                WheelPosition.NearEdge => 0.803 * (pw * 1000 / (h * h)) * (4 * Math.Log10(ratio) + 0.666 / ratio - 0.034),
                WheelPosition.OnEdge => 0.803 * (pw * 1000 / (h * h)) * (4 * Math.Log10(ratio) + 0.282 / ratio + 0.65),
                _ => 4.2 * (pw * 1000 / (h * h)) * (1 - Math.Sqrt(b / l) / (0.925 + 0.22 * (b / l))),
            };
            return (l, b, stress);
        }

        var (lc, bc, sigma) = At(slabMm);
        r.Step("l", "l characteristic length", lc, "mm", "0");
        r.Step("b", "b equivalent tyre radius", bc, "mm", "0");
        r.Result("stress", "Applied stress σ", sigma, "MPa", "0.000");
        bool ok = fall >= 0.995 * sigma;
        r.Check("f'cf allowable ≥ σ", ok, $"{CalcResult.Num(fall, "0.000")} vs {CalcResult.Num(sigma, "0.000")} MPa");

        double min = 100;
        while (min < 1000 && ff * fcf < 0.995 * At(min).Stress) min += 5;
        r.Result("hmin", "Least thickness that passes (5 mm steps)", min, "mm", "0");
        r.Values["adopt"] = min;
        r.ThicknessMm = min;
        r.Headline = (ok ? "OK" : "NO GOOD") + $" at {CalcResult.Num(slabMm, "0")} mm (σ {CalcResult.Num(sigma, "0.00")} vs {CalcResult.Num(fall, "0.00")} MPa); least that passes {CalcResult.Num(min, "0")} mm";
        r.Notes.Add("f'c 25 to 50 MPa only. Uses the workbook's characteristic-length formula, which has (1 − v¹²) where Westergaard has (1 − v²); the difference in l is under 1%.");
        return r;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ equivalent soil modulus ------

    /// <summary>One soil layer for <see cref="EquivalentModulus"/>. Zero means "not known".</summary>
    public sealed record SoilLayer(double DepthM, double Cbr = 0, double SptN = 0, double Pi = 0, double Qc = 0, double A = 0, double B = 1);

    /// <summary>Equivalent Young's modulus Ese of up to 4 soil layers for wheels, posts and UDL (sheet 5, T48 p78-9).</summary>
    public static CalcResult EquivalentModulus(IReadOnlyList<SoilLayer> layers, double wheelSpacing, double postSpacing, double udlSpacing)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = "Equivalent soil modulus Ese", Reference = T48 + " p78-79, Fig 1.15, 1.16, 1.19" };
        if (layers.Count is 0 or > 4) throw new ArgumentException("Give 1 to 4 soil layers.");
        int n = layers.Count;
        var shortE = new double[n];
        var longE = new double[n];
        var z = new double[n];
        double depth = 0;
        static double MinNonZero(double a, double b) => a > b ? (b == 0 ? a : b) : (a == 0 ? b : a);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            var L = layers[i];
            r.InText($"Layer {i + 1}", $"H {CalcResult.Num(L.DepthM)} m, CBR {CalcResult.Num(L.Cbr)}%, SPT N {CalcResult.Num(L.SptN)}, PI {CalcResult.Num(L.Pi)}, qc {CalcResult.Num(L.Qc)} MPa, a {CalcResult.Num(L.A)}, b {CalcResult.Num(L.B)}");
            double esLcbr = EsFromCbr(L.Cbr);
            double cubic = i < 2 ? 0.00002 : 0.0000275; // as in the workbook (columns D-E vs F-G)
            double ratio = L.Pi < 35
                ? -0.0000008 * Math.Pow(L.Pi, 4) + cubic * Math.Pow(L.Pi, 3) + 0.00165 * L.Pi * L.Pi - 0.1276 * L.Pi + 3.5
                : 11.315 * Math.Pow(L.Pi, -0.7462);
            double essCbr = L.B == 0 ? 0 : esLcbr / L.B;
            double essSpt = ratio * L.SptN;
            double eslSpt = L.B * essSpt;
            double essCpt = L.A * L.Qc;
            double eslCpt = L.B * essCpt;
            shortE[i] = MinNonZero(MinNonZero(essSpt, essCbr), essCpt);
            longE[i] = MinNonZero(MinNonZero(eslSpt, esLcbr), eslCpt);
            z[i] = L.DepthM > 0 || i < 2 ? depth + L.DepthM / 2 : 0;
            depth += L.DepthM;
            r.Step("ess" + (i + 1), $"Layer {i + 1}: Ess short term (wheels)", shortE[i], "MPa", "0.0");
            r.Step("esl" + (i + 1), $"Layer {i + 1}: EsL long term (posts, UDL)", longE[i], "MPa", "0.0");
        }

        double Combine(string key, string label, double spacing, Func<int, double, double> wf, double[] e)
        {
            double top = 0, bottom = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                double w = wf(i, z[i] / spacing) * layers[i].DepthM;
                top += w;
                if (e[i] != 0) bottom += w / e[i];
            }
            return r.Result(key, label, e.Sum() == 0 || bottom == 0 ? 0 : top / bottom, "MPa", "0.0");
        }

        double wheel = Combine("wheel", $"Ese for WHEELS (S = {CalcResult.Num(wheelSpacing)} m)", wheelSpacing, (i, x) => i switch
        {
            0 => x < 1.51 ? 1 - x / 3 : x > 3.021 ? 0.6114 * Math.Exp(-0.2054 * x) : -0.0139 * Math.Pow(x, 5) + 0.191 * Math.Pow(x, 4) - 1.0032 * Math.Pow(x, 3) + 2.6302 * x * x - 3.5446 * x + 2.446,
            1 => x < 1.51 ? 1 - x / 3 : x > 3.01 ? 0.59 * Math.Exp(-0.2054 * x) : -0.0139 * Math.Pow(x, 5) + 0.1889 * Math.Pow(x, 4) - 1.0032 * Math.Pow(x, 3) + 2.6302 * x * x - 3.5446 * x + 2.446,
            _ => x < 1.51 ? 1 - x / 3.05 : x > 3.01 ? 0.59 * Math.Exp(-0.2054 * x) : -0.0139 * Math.Pow(x, 5) + 0.1889 * Math.Pow(x, 4) - 1.0032 * Math.Pow(x, 3) + 2.6302 * x * x - 3.5446 * x + 2.446,
        }, shortE);
        double post = Combine("post", $"Ese for POSTS (S = {CalcResult.Num(postSpacing)} m)", postSpacing, (_, x) =>
            x < 7 ? -0.000003 * Math.Pow(x, 6) + 0.0001 * Math.Pow(x, 5) - 0.0014 * Math.Pow(x, 4) + 0.0077 * Math.Pow(x, 3) + 0.0078 * x * x - 0.266 * x + 0.997 : 2.786 * Math.Pow(x, -1.6073), longE);
        double udl = Combine("udl", $"Ese for UDL (S = {CalcResult.Num(udlSpacing)} m)", udlSpacing, (_, x) =>
            x < 1.51 ? -0.04 * x * x * x + 0.12 * x * x - 0.31 * x + 1 : -0.0009 * x * x * x + 0.0235 * x * x - 0.2195 * x + 0.9338, longE);

        r.Headline = $"Ese wheels {CalcResult.Num(wheel, "0.0")} MPa, posts {CalcResult.Num(post, "0.0")} MPa, UDL {CalcResult.Num(udl, "0.0")} MPa";
        r.Notes.Add("Ese should not be outside the range of the layers' own Es; it is usually close to the average of the first few layers.");
        return r;
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/Design/RoadDesign.cs   (102 lines)
################################################################################

namespace PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

/// <summary>Traffic for a flexible pavement design, either known or worked out from counts.</summary>
public sealed record TrafficInputs(
    double KnownDesa,              // > 0: use this DESA and ignore the counts
    double Aadt = 0,               // vehicles per day, both directions
    double HeavyPercent = 0,       // % heavy vehicles
    double DirectionFactor = 0.5,  // share in the design direction
    double LaneFactor = 1,         // share in the design lane
    double GrowthPercent = 0,      // annual growth, % per year
    double Years = 20,             // design life
    double EsaPerHeavy = 1);       // ESA per heavy vehicle (= axle groups per HV x ESA per group)

/// <summary>
/// Road / car park design: traffic (known or from counts), Austroads thickness, the company build-up, and a
/// thickness-vs-CBR table, as one calculation.
/// </summary>
public static class RoadDesign
{
    /// <summary>Cumulative growth factor ((1 + r)^P − 1) / r, or P with no growth.</summary>
    public static double GrowthFactor(double growthPercent, double years)
    {
        double r = growthPercent / 100;
        return Math.Abs(r) < 1e-9 ? years : (Math.Pow(1 + r, years) - 1) / r;
    }

    /// <summary>DESA = 365 x AADT x DF x LDF x %HV/100 x CGF x ESA/HV (Austroads AGPT02 form).</summary>
    public static CalcResult Traffic(TrafficInputs t)
    {
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = "Design traffic", Reference = "Austroads AGPT02 (design traffic)" };
        r.In("AADT (both directions)", t.Aadt, "veh/day", "0");
        r.In("Heavy vehicles", t.HeavyPercent, "%");
        r.In("Direction factor DF", t.DirectionFactor);
        r.In("Lane distribution factor LDF", t.LaneFactor);
        r.In("Annual growth", t.GrowthPercent, "%");
        r.In("Design life", t.Years, "years");
        r.In("ESA per heavy vehicle", t.EsaPerHeavy);
        if (!(t.Aadt > 0) || !(t.HeavyPercent > 0) || !(t.Years > 0))
            throw new ArgumentException("Enter the AADT, % heavy vehicles and design life (or a known DESA).");
        double hv = r.Step("hvday", "Heavy vehicles per day in the design lane = AADT x DF x LDF x %HV", t.Aadt * t.DirectionFactor * t.LaneFactor * t.HeavyPercent / 100, "HV/day", "0.0");
        double cgf = r.Step("cgf", "Cumulative growth factor ((1+r)^P − 1)/r", GrowthFactor(t.GrowthPercent, t.Years), "", "0.00");
        double nhv = r.Step("nhv", "Heavy vehicles over the design life = 365 x HV/day x CGF", 365 * hv * cgf, "HV", "0");
        double desa = r.Result("desa", "DESA = heavy vehicles x ESA/HV", nhv * t.EsaPerHeavy, "ESA", "0");
        r.Headline = $"DESA = {desa:0.00E+0} ESA";
        r.Notes.Add("ESA per heavy vehicle depends on the road and vehicle mix: use project traffic data where you have it.");
        return r;
    }

    /// <summary>The whole flexible design: traffic, Austroads check, company build-up and a thickness-vs-CBR table.</summary>
    public static CalcResult Design(double cbr, TrafficInputs traffic, string? title = null)
    {
        CalcResult? trafficCalc = null;
        double desa = traffic.KnownDesa;
        if (!(desa > 0))
        {
            trafficCalc = Traffic(traffic);
            desa = trafficCalc.Values["desa"];
        }
        var design = FlexibleDesign.Calc(cbr, desa, title);

        var r = new CalcResult
        {
            Title = "Road / car park pavement design",
            Reference = design.Reference,
            Headline = design.Headline,
            Pass = design.Pass,
            ThicknessMm = design.ThicknessMm,
            BuildupText = design.BuildupText,
        };
        r.In("Subgrade CBR", cbr, "%");
        r.In("Design traffic", desa, "ESA", "0.##E+0");
        r.Values["desa"] = desa;
        foreach (var line in design.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind != LineKind.Input)) r.Lines.Add(line);
        foreach (var key in design.Values.Keys) r.Values[key] = design.Values[key];
        r.Notes.AddRange(design.Notes);
        if (trafficCalc is not null) r.Parts.Add(trafficCalc);

        // What if the CBR is different? (same traffic)
        var rows = new List<string[]>();
        foreach (var c in new[] { 2.0, 3, 4, 5, 7, 10, 15 }.Append(cbr).Distinct().OrderBy(x => x))
        {
            var alt = FlexibleDesign.Design(c, desa);
            rows.Add(new[]
            {
                (Math.Abs(c - cbr) < 1e-9 ? "► " : "") + CalcResult.Num(c, "0.#") + "%",
                alt.Surfacing, alt.BaseMm.ToString(), alt.SubbaseMm.ToString(), alt.GranularMm.ToString(), CalcResult.Num(alt.Required, "0"),
            });
        }
        r.Tables.Add(("Thickness vs subgrade CBR (same traffic)", new[] { "CBR", "Surfacing", "DGB20", "DGS20", "Granular", "Austroads" }, rows));

        // And if the traffic is one level up or down?
        var trows = new List<string[]>();
        foreach (var row in FlexibleDesign.TrafficRows)
        {
            var alt = FlexibleDesign.Design(cbr, row.Desa);
            trows.Add(new[] { (Math.Abs(row.Desa - desa) / desa < 0.01 ? "► " : "") + row.Label, alt.Surfacing, alt.BaseMm.ToString(), alt.SubbaseMm.ToString(), alt.GranularMm.ToString() });
        }
        r.Tables.Add(($"Thickness vs traffic (CBR {CalcResult.Num(cbr, "0.#")}%)", new[] { "DESA", "Surfacing", "DGB20", "DGS20", "Granular" }, trows));
        r.Parts.Add(design);
        return r;
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/Design/SlabDesign.cs   (190 lines)
################################################################################

namespace PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

/// <summary>Everything a concrete slab design needs, entered once.</summary>
public sealed record SlabInputs
{
    // Concrete and ground
    public double Fc { get; init; } = 32;
    public double Cbr { get; init; } = 5;
    /// <summary>Soil modulus for wheels / posts / UDL (MPa); 0 = from the CBR (T48 Fig 1.15).</summary>
    public double EsOverride { get; init; }
    public double SoilDepth { get; init; } = 5;
    /// <summary>Check loads at free edges (joints without dowels / slab edges) as well as the interior.</summary>
    public bool CheckEdges { get; init; } = true;

    // Wheels (forklifts, trucks)
    public bool Wheels { get; init; } = true;
    public double AxleTonnes { get; init; } = 10;
    public double WheelSpacing { get; init; } = 1.5;
    public double PassesPerDay { get; init; } = 10;
    public double Years { get; init; } = 20;
    public double TyreKpa { get; init; } = 700;
    public double K1Wheel { get; init; } = 0.85;
    public double K3Interior { get; init; } = 1.2;
    public double K3Edge { get; init; } = 1.05;

    // Racks (post loads)
    public bool Posts { get; init; }
    public double PostKn { get; init; } = 70;
    public double PostX { get; init; } = 2;
    public double PostY { get; init; } = 1.5;
    public double K1Post { get; init; } = 0.8;
    public double K2Post { get; init; } = 0.85;
    public double BasePlateMm { get; init; } = 165;
    /// <summary>P* = factor x P for punching shear (e.g. 1.5 for live load).</summary>
    public double LoadFactor { get; init; } = 1.5;

    // Distributed load (storage)
    public bool Udl { get; init; }
    public double UdlKpa { get; init; } = 30;
    public double AisleWidth { get; init; } = 4;
    public double UdlRepetitions { get; init; } = 1000;
    public double K1Udl { get; init; } = 0.8;

    /// <summary>Also cross-check wheels with Westergaard / Ioannides / Pickett (T34).</summary>
    public bool T34Checks { get; init; } = true;
}

/// <summary>
/// Concrete industrial slab: every T48 check the loads call for, at once, the governing thickness, punching
/// shear at that thickness, T34 cross-checks and a thickness-vs-grade table.
/// </summary>
public static class SlabDesign
{
    public static CalcResult Design(SlabInputs s, string? title = null) => Run(s, title, whatIf: true);

    private static CalcResult Run(SlabInputs s, string? title, bool whatIf)
    {
        if (!(s.Cbr > 0)) throw new ArgumentException("Enter the subgrade CBR (greater than 0%).");
        if (!s.Wheels && !s.Posts && !s.Udl) throw new ArgumentException("Tick at least one load: wheels, racks or a distributed load.");
        var r = new CalcResult { Title = "Concrete slab design", Reference = ConcreteDesign.T48 + "; AS3600; " + ConcreteDesign.T34 };

        double es = s.EsOverride > 0 ? s.EsOverride : ConcreteDesign.EsFromCbr(s.Cbr);
        double k = ConcreteDesign.KFromCbr(s.Cbr);
        r.In("f'c concrete", s.Fc, "MPa");
        r.In("Subgrade CBR", s.Cbr, "%");
        r.In("Soil depth H", s.SoilDepth, "m");
        if (s.Wheels)
        {
            r.In("Heaviest axle", s.AxleTonnes, "tonne");
            r.In("Wheel spacing", s.WheelSpacing, "m");
            r.In("Passes per day / design life", s.PassesPerDay, $"/ {s.Years:0} years");
        }
        if (s.Posts)
        {
            r.In("Rack post load (unfactored)", s.PostKn, "kN");
            r.InText("Post spacing x / y", $"{s.PostX:0.##} / {s.PostY:0.##} m");
        }
        if (s.Udl) r.In("Distributed load", s.UdlKpa, "kPa");
        r.InText("Free edges checked", s.CheckEdges ? "Yes" : "No");
        r.Step("es", s.EsOverride > 0 ? "Soil modulus Es (entered)" : "Soil modulus Es from CBR (T48 Fig 1.15)", es, "MPa", "0.0");
        r.Step("k", "Modulus of subgrade reaction k from CBR", k, "kPa/mm", "0.0");

        var checks = new List<(string Name, CalcResult Calc)>();
        double axleKn = s.AxleTonnes * 9.81;
        if (s.Wheels)
        {
            checks.Add(("Wheels - interior", ConcreteDesign.Wheel(false, s.Fc, s.K1Wheel, s.PassesPerDay, s.Years, s.K3Interior, s.WheelSpacing, s.SoilDepth, es, axleKn)));
            if (s.CheckEdges)
                checks.Add(("Wheels - edge", ConcreteDesign.Wheel(true, s.Fc, s.K1Wheel, s.PassesPerDay, s.Years, s.K3Edge, s.WheelSpacing, s.SoilDepth, es, axleKn)));
        }
        if (s.Posts)
        {
            checks.Add(("Racks - interior post", ConcreteDesign.Post(false, s.Fc, s.K1Post, s.K2Post, s.PostX, s.PostY, s.SoilDepth, es, s.PostKn)));
            if (s.CheckEdges)
                checks.Add(("Racks - edge post", ConcreteDesign.Post(true, s.Fc, s.K1Post, 1, s.PostX, s.PostY, s.SoilDepth, es, s.PostKn)));
        }
        if (s.Udl)
            checks.Add(("Distributed load (UDL)", ConcreteDesign.Udl(s.Fc, s.K1Udl, s.UdlRepetitions, s.AisleWidth, s.SoilDepth, es, s.UdlKpa)));

        // Governing T48 thickness.
        double governing = 0;
        string governedBy = "";
        foreach (var (name, calc) in checks)
        {
            if (calc.Pass == false || calc.ThicknessMm is null)
            {
                r.Lines.Add(new CalcLine(name, "-", "", LineKind.Fail, calc.Headline));
                r.Warn($"{name}: {calc.Headline}");
                continue;
            }
            r.Lines.Add(new CalcLine(name, CalcResult.Num(calc.Values["t"], "0"), "mm", LineKind.Step, "adopt " + CalcResult.Num(calc.ThicknessMm.Value, "0")));
            if (calc.ThicknessMm.Value > governing)
            {
                governing = calc.ThicknessMm.Value;
                governedBy = name;
            }
        }

        // Punching shear at the governing thickness; thicken until it passes.
        if (s.Posts && governing > 0)
        {
            double t = governing;
            double pStar = s.PostKn * s.LoadFactor;
            while (t < 1000 && ConcreteDesign.PunchingShear(s.Fc, s.BasePlateMm, t, pStar).Pass != true) t += 5;
            var punch = ConcreteDesign.PunchingShear(s.Fc, s.BasePlateMm, t, pStar);
            checks.Add(("Racks - punching shear", punch));
            r.Lines.Add(new CalcLine("Racks - punching shear (P* = " + CalcResult.Num(s.LoadFactor) + " x P)", CalcResult.Num(punch.Values["phiVuo"], "0"), "kN",
                LineKind.Step, $"φVuo at {CalcResult.Num(t, "0")} mm vs P* {CalcResult.Num(pStar, "0")} kN"));
            if (t > governing)
            {
                governing = t;
                governedBy = "Racks - punching shear";
            }
        }

        if (governing <= 0)
        {
            r.Pass = false;
            r.Headline = "No thickness found: see the warnings";
            foreach (var (_, calc) in checks) r.Parts.Add(calc);
            return r;
        }

        r.Result("t", "Governing thickness", governing, "mm", "0");
        r.Lines.Add(new CalcLine("Governed by", governedBy, "", LineKind.Ok));
        r.ThicknessMm = governing;
        r.Pass = true;
        r.Headline = $"Adopt {governing:0} mm f'c {s.Fc:0} MPa concrete (governed by {governedBy.ToLowerInvariant()})";

        // T34 cross-checks at the adopted thickness.
        if (s.Wheels && s.T34Checks)
        {
            double n = ConcreteDesign.Repetitions(s.PassesPerDay, s.Years);
            var positions = s.CheckEdges
                ? new[] { ConcreteDesign.WheelPosition.Interior, ConcreteDesign.WheelPosition.NearEdge, ConcreteDesign.WheelPosition.OnEdge, ConcreteDesign.WheelPosition.Corner }
                : new[] { ConcreteDesign.WheelPosition.Interior };
            foreach (var where in positions)
            {
                var c = ConcreteDesign.WheelStress(where, s.AxleTonnes, s.Cbr, s.Fc, 1, 0.15, governing, s.TyreKpa, n);
                checks.Add(("T34 " + where, c));
                r.Lines.Add(new CalcLine("Cross-check: " + c.Title.Replace("Wheel stress - ", ""), c.Pass == true ? "OK" : "NO GOOD", "",
                    c.Pass == true ? LineKind.Ok : LineKind.Warning, $"least {CalcResult.Num(c.ThicknessMm ?? 0, "0")} mm"));
                if (c.Pass != true)
                    r.Notes.Add($"T34 {c.Title.Replace("Wheel stress - ", "").ToLowerInvariant()} needs {CalcResult.Num(c.ThicknessMm ?? 0, "0")} mm (T48 governs the design; consider it for unjointed edges / corners).");
            }
        }

        // What if the concrete grade changes?
        var rows = new List<string[]>();
        foreach (var grade in whatIf ? ConcreteDesign.Grades : Array.Empty<double>())
        {
            string text;
            try
            {
                var alt = Run(s with { Fc = grade, T34Checks = false }, null, whatIf: false);
                text = alt.ThicknessMm is { } t2 ? $"{t2:0} mm" : "-";
            }
            catch (ArgumentException) { text = "-"; }
            rows.Add(new[] { (Math.Abs(grade - s.Fc) < 1e-9 ? "► " : "") + $"{grade:0} MPa", text });
        }
        if (whatIf) r.Tables.Add(("Thickness vs concrete grade", new[] { "f'c", "Governing thickness" }, rows));

        r.BuildupText = (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title) ? "" : $"[{title}]\n")
            + $"{governing:0}mm F'c {s.Fc:0}MPa CONCRETE PAVEMENT\nSUBGRADE MINIMUM CBR {s.Cbr:0.#}% COMPACTED TO 98% SMDD AT ±2% OMC";
        r.Notes.Add("Working-strength design (unfactored loads) to T48; punching shear uses P* = load factor x P.");
        r.Notes.Add("Base / subbase under the slab, joints, reinforcement and curing are not part of this calculation.");
        foreach (var (_, calc) in checks) r.Parts.Add(calc);
        return r;
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Core/Design/CalcReport.cs   (112 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Globalization;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;

namespace PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

/// <summary>
/// A design calculation as a TTW-styled HTML page (opens in any browser, prints to PDF): summary first, then
/// each check's inputs, steps and results, the "what if" tables, notes and references.
/// </summary>
public static class CalcReport
{
    /// <param name="excelFileName">The Excel workbook's file name next to the report (linked from the page), or null.</param>
    public static string Html(CalcResult design, string? project, string? engineer, string? checker = null, string? excelFileName = null)
    {
        static string E(string? s) => WebUtility.HtmlEncode(s ?? "");
        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.Append("<!doctype html><html lang=\"en\"><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width,initial-scale=1\">");
        sb.Append($"<title>{E(design.Title)}{(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(project) ? "" : " - " + E(project))}</title><style>");
        sb.Append(@"
:root{--band:#024360;--alab:#F7F4E7;--line:#DED7C4;--fg:#1E1711;--muted:#6F6A5D;--ok:#325442;--bad:#A84933}
*{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;background:#fff;color:var(--fg);font:10.5pt/1.45 'Arial Nova',Arial,sans-serif}
header{background:var(--band);color:var(--alab);padding:18px 28px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;gap:16px}
header h1{margin:0;font-size:18pt;font-weight:400}header .sub{color:#8ECAEF;font-size:9pt}
main{padding:18px 28px 32px;max-width:1000px}
.meta{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:4px 16px;border:1px solid var(--line);padding:10px 12px;margin-bottom:16px}
.meta b{display:block;font-size:8pt;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em}
.headline{font-size:13pt;font-weight:700;padding:10px 12px;border-left:5px solid var(--ok);background:#F2F6F3;margin:0 0 16px}
.headline.bad{border-color:var(--bad);background:#FBF1EE}
h2{font-size:12pt;color:var(--band);margin:22px 0 6px;border-bottom:2px solid var(--band);padding-bottom:3px}
h3{font-size:10.5pt;margin:14px 0 4px}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin:4px 0 10px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
th,td{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding:3px 8px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top}
th{font-size:8.5pt;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em}
td.v{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}tr.result td{font-weight:700}tr.ok td{color:var(--ok);font-weight:700}
tr.fail td,tr.warn td{color:var(--bad);font-weight:700}td.ref{color:var(--muted);font-size:9pt}
ul{margin:4px 0 0 18px;padding:0}li{margin:2px 0}.muted{color:var(--muted)}
.xl{margin:0 0 12px}.xl a{color:var(--band);font-weight:700}@media print{.xl{display:none}}
.sign{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:16px;margin-top:28px}.sign div{border-top:1px solid var(--fg);padding-top:4px;font-size:9pt;color:var(--muted)}
@media print{header{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}.part{break-inside:avoid}}
");
        sb.Append("</style></head><body>");
        sb.Append($"<header><div><h1>{E(design.Title)}</h1><div class=\"sub\">TTW Pavement design calculation</div></div><div class=\"sub\">{E(design.Reference)}</div></header><main>");
        sb.Append("<div class=\"meta\">");
        sb.Append($"<div><b>Project</b>{E(Blank(project))}</div><div><b>Designed</b>{E(Blank(engineer))}</div><div><b>Checked</b>{E(Blank(checker))}</div>");
        sb.Append($"<div><b>Date</b>{DateTime.Now.ToString("d MMM yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}</div></div>");
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(excelFileName))
            sb.Append($"<p class=\"xl\"><a href=\"{E(Uri.EscapeDataString(excelFileName))}\">Open the Excel workbook ({E(excelFileName)})</a></p>");
        sb.Append($"<p class=\"headline{(design.Pass == false ? " bad" : "")}\">{(design.Pass == false ? "✖ " : "✔ ")}{E(design.Headline)}</p>");

        Part(sb, design, summary: true);
        foreach (var (title, columns, rows) in design.Tables)
        {
            sb.Append($"<h3>{E(title)}</h3><table><tr>{string.Concat(columns.Select(c => $"<th>{E(c)}</th>"))}</tr>");
            foreach (var row in rows)
                sb.Append($"<tr{(row[0].StartsWith("►") ? " class=\"result\"" : "")}>{string.Concat(row.Select(c => $"<td>{E(c.TrimStart('►', ' '))}</td>"))}</tr>");
            sb.Append("</table>");
        }
        if (design.Notes.Count > 0)
            sb.Append("<h3>Notes</h3><ul>" + string.Concat(design.Notes.Distinct().Select(n => $"<li>{E(n)}</li>")) + "</ul>");

        if (design.Parts.Count > 0)
        {
            sb.Append("<h2>Calculations</h2>");
            foreach (var part in design.Parts)
            {
                sb.Append("<div class=\"part\">");
                sb.Append($"<h3>{E(part.Title)} <span class=\"muted\">({E(part.Reference)})</span></h3>");
                Part(sb, part, summary: false);
                sb.Append($"<p><b>{(part.Pass == false ? "✖ " : "")}{E(part.Headline)}</b></p>");
                if (part.Notes.Count > 0)
                    sb.Append("<ul class=\"muted\">" + string.Concat(part.Notes.Distinct().Select(n => $"<li>{E(n)}</li>")) + "</ul>");
                sb.Append("</div>");
            }
        }
        sb.Append("<div class=\"sign\"><div>Designed</div><div>Checked</div><div>Approved</div></div>");
        sb.Append("<p class=\"muted\">Calculated with TTW Pavement from the company design workbooks. Results are a design aid: check them before use.</p>");
        sb.Append("</main></body></html>");
        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private static string Blank(string? s) => string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s) ? "-" : s.Trim();

    private static void Part(StringBuilder sb, CalcResult r, bool summary)
    {
        static string E(string? s) => WebUtility.HtmlEncode(s ?? "");
        var inputs = r.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind == LineKind.Input).ToList();
        var steps = r.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind != LineKind.Input).ToList();
        if (inputs.Count > 0)
        {
            sb.Append(summary ? "<h2>Inputs</h2>" : "");
            sb.Append("<table><tr><th>Input</th><th class=\"v\">Value</th><th>Unit</th></tr>");
            foreach (var l in inputs) sb.Append($"<tr><td>{E(l.Label)}</td><td class=\"v\">{E(l.Value)}</td><td>{E(l.Unit)}</td></tr>");
            sb.Append("</table>");
        }
        if (steps.Count > 0)
        {
            sb.Append(summary ? "<h2>Results</h2>" : "");
            sb.Append("<table><tr><th>Step</th><th class=\"v\">Value</th><th>Unit</th><th>Note</th></tr>");
            foreach (var l in steps)
            {
                string cls = l.Kind switch
                {
                    LineKind.Result => " class=\"result\"", LineKind.Ok => " class=\"ok\"",
                    LineKind.Fail => " class=\"fail\"", LineKind.Warning => " class=\"warn\"", _ => "",
                };
                sb.Append($"<tr{cls}><td>{E(l.Label)}</td><td class=\"v\">{E(l.Value)}</td><td>{E(l.Unit)}</td><td class=\"ref\">{E(l.Reference)}</td></tr>");
            }
            sb.Append("</table>");
        }
    }
}
