################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Export/DxfExport.cs   (191 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Globalization;
using netDxf;
using netDxf.Entities;
using netDxf.Header;
using netDxf.Tables;
using PavementBuildup.Core;
using DxfLayer = netDxf.Tables.Layer;

namespace PavementBuildup.Export;

/// <summary>
/// Writes a pavement detail to a DXF file (AutoCAD 2010 format) with the CAD standard's layers, so it
/// can be inserted into any drawing later (INSERT or drag in). Same layout as the Civil 3D plugin draws,
/// with a simple Z break at the sides (the TTW break block is only available inside Civil 3D).
/// </summary>
public static class DxfExport
{
    /// <summary>Builds the detail and saves it to <paramref name="stream"/>. Throws ArgumentException for an invalid build-up.</summary>
    public static void Write(Buildup buildup, DetailSettings settings, CadStandard standard, Stream stream)
    {
        double unitsPerMm = DetailLayout.ResolveUnitsPerMm(settings.UnitsOverride, standard.DrawingUnits, null);
        var g = DetailLayout.Build(buildup, settings, standard, unitsPerMm);
        var doc = Build(g, standard, unitsPerMm);
        doc.Save(stream);
    }

    private static DxfDocument Build(DetailGeometry g, CadStandard standard, double unitsPerMm)
    {
        var doc = new DxfDocument(DxfVersion.AutoCad2010) { BuildDimensionBlocks = true };
        doc.DrawingVariables.InsUnits = unitsPerMm switch
        {
            0.001 => netDxf.Units.DrawingUnits.Meters,
            0.1 => netDxf.Units.DrawingUnits.Centimeters,
            _ => netDxf.Units.DrawingUnits.Millimeters,
        };

        var layers = Enum.GetValues<DetailElement>().ToDictionary(e => e, e => LayerFor(doc, standard.Layer(e)));

        var left = g.Edges.Count > 0 ? g.Edges[0] : new List<Pt> { new(0, 0), new(0, -1) };
        var right = g.Edges.Count > 1 ? g.Edges[1] : new List<Pt> { new(g.Width, 0), new(g.Width, -1) };

        // Hatches (kept inside the side edges) and existing-work outlines.
        foreach (var band in g.Bands.Append(g.Subgrade).OfType<Band>())
        {
            if (band.Hatch is { } spec)
            {
                var loop = EdgeProfile.Band(left, right, band.Top, band.Bottom);
                var boundary = new Polyline2D(loop.Select(p => new Polyline2DVertex(p.X, p.Y)), true);
                var hatch = new Hatch(HatchPatterns.For(spec.Pattern, band.HatchScale, spec.AngleDeg),
                    new[] { new HatchBoundaryPath(new EntityObject[] { boundary }) }, false)
                {
                    Layer = band.Existing ? layers[DetailElement.ExistingHatch]
                        : string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(spec.Layer) ? layers[DetailElement.Hatch]
                        : LayerFor(doc, new LayerStyle { Name = spec.Layer.Trim(), Color = "7" }),
                };
                if (Color(spec.Color) is { } c) hatch.Color = c;
                doc.Entities.Add(hatch);
            }
            if (band.Existing)
                doc.Entities.Add(new Polyline2D(new[]
                {
                    new Polyline2DVertex(0, band.Top), new Polyline2DVertex(g.Width, band.Top),
                    new Polyline2DVertex(g.Width, band.Bottom), new Polyline2DVertex(0, band.Bottom),
                }, true) { Layer = layers[DetailElement.ExistingOutline] });
        }

        // Course lines, layer lines and membranes stop on the side edges.
        foreach (var y in g.InterfaceLevels.Concat(g.LiftLevels))
            doc.Entities.Add(new Line(new Vector2(EdgeProfile.XAt(left, y), y), new Vector2(EdgeProfile.XAt(right, y), y))
                { Layer = layers[DetailElement.Outline] });
        foreach (var y in g.MembraneLevels)
        {
            var pl = new Polyline2D(new[] { new Polyline2DVertex(EdgeProfile.XAt(left, y), y), new Polyline2DVertex(EdgeProfile.XAt(right, y), y) })
                { Layer = layers[DetailElement.Membrane] };
            pl.SetConstantWidth(Math.Max(0, standard.MembraneWidthMm) * g.AnnotationScale);
            doc.Entities.Add(pl);
        }
        foreach (var edge in g.Edges)
            doc.Entities.Add(new Polyline2D(edge.Select(p => new Polyline2DVertex(p.X, p.Y))) { Layer = layers[DetailElement.Outline] });

        // Reinforcement.
        foreach (var bar in g.BarLines)
        {
            var pl = new Polyline2D(new[] { new Polyline2DVertex(0, bar.Y), new Polyline2DVertex(g.Width, bar.Y) })
                { Layer = layers[DetailElement.Reinforcement] };
            pl.SetConstantWidth(bar.Diameter);
            doc.Entities.Add(pl);
        }
        foreach (var bar in g.Bars)
            doc.Entities.Add(Dot(bar.Center, bar.Diameter, layers[DetailElement.Reinforcement]));

        // Labels with leaders (a branch to each layer of "2 x 150mm ...").
        foreach (var label in g.Labels)
        {
            var leader = layers[DetailElement.Leader];
            doc.Entities.Add(new Polyline2D(new[]
            {
                new Polyline2DVertex(label.Anchor.X, label.Anchor.Y),
                new Polyline2DVertex(label.Elbow.X, label.Elbow.Y),
                new Polyline2DVertex(label.TextAt.X - g.TextHeight * 0.5, label.TextAt.Y),
            }) { Layer = leader });
            doc.Entities.Add(Dot(label.Anchor, 0.6 * g.AnnotationScale, leader));
            foreach (var extra in label.ExtraAnchors)
            {
                doc.Entities.Add(new Line(new Vector2(extra.X, extra.Y), new Vector2(label.Elbow.X, label.Elbow.Y)) { Layer = leader });
                doc.Entities.Add(Dot(extra, 0.6 * g.AnnotationScale, leader));
            }
            doc.Entities.Add(Text(label.Text, label.TextAt, g.TextHeight, false, false, layers[DetailElement.Text]));
        }

        // Dimensions: real dimension objects, sized for the detail scale.
        if (g.Dimensions.Count > 0)
        {
            var style = new DimensionStyle("TTW_PAVEMENT")
            {
                DimScaleOverall = g.AnnotationScale,
                TextHeight = standard.DimensionTextHeightMm,
                ArrowSize = standard.DimensionArrowSizeMm,
                ExtLineExtend = 1.0,
                ExtLineOffset = 0.6,
                TextOffset = 0.6,
            };
            foreach (var d in g.Dimensions)
            {
                var dim = new LinearDimension(new Vector2(d.RefX, d.Top), new Vector2(d.RefX, d.Bottom), d.RefX - d.X, 90, style)
                {
                    UserText = d.Text,
                    Layer = layers[DetailElement.Dimension],
                };
                doc.Entities.Add(dim);
            }
        }

        foreach (var t in g.Titles)
            doc.Entities.Add(Text(t.Text, t.At, t.Height, t.Underline, t.Centered, layers[DetailElement.Title]));

        return doc;
    }

    private static MText Text(string text, Pt at, double height, bool underline, bool centered, DxfLayer layer)
    {
        string escaped = text.Replace(@"\", @"\\").Replace("{", @"\{").Replace("}", @"\}").Replace("\r", "").Replace("\n", @"\P");
        return new MText(underline ? @"\L" + escaped + @"\l" : escaped, new Vector2(at.X, at.Y), height)
        {
            AttachmentPoint = centered ? MTextAttachmentPoint.MiddleCenter : MTextAttachmentPoint.MiddleLeft,
            Layer = layer,
        };
    }

    /// <summary>A filled dot: a closed two-arc polyline as wide as its radius (like DONUT).</summary>
    private static Polyline2D Dot(Pt c, double diameter, DxfLayer layer)
    {
        double r = diameter / 4;
        var pl = new Polyline2D(new[] { new Polyline2DVertex(c.X - r, c.Y, 1), new Polyline2DVertex(c.X + r, c.Y, 1) }, true) { Layer = layer };
        pl.SetConstantWidth(diameter / 2);
        return pl;
    }

    private static DxfLayer LayerFor(DxfDocument doc, LayerStyle style)
    {
        var name = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(style.Name) ? "0" : style.Name.Trim();
        if (doc.Layers.TryGetValue(name, out var existing))
            return existing;
        var layer = new DxfLayer(name) { IsVisible = true, Plot = style.Plot };
        if (Color(style.Color) is { } c && !c.IsByLayer && !c.IsByBlock) layer.Color = c;
        if (style.LineWeightMm >= 0) layer.Lineweight = Lineweight(style.LineWeightMm);
        return doc.Layers.Add(layer);
    }

    /// <summary>"7", "R,G,B", BYLAYER, BYBLOCK; null when blank or not understood.</summary>
    private static AciColor? Color(string? text)
    {
        var s = (text ?? "").Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
        if (s.Length == 0) return null;
        if (s == "BYLAYER") return AciColor.ByLayer;
        if (s == "BYBLOCK") return AciColor.ByBlock;
        if (short.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var aci) && aci is >= 1 and <= 255)
            return new AciColor(aci);
        var rgb = s.Split(',');
        if (rgb.Length == 3 && rgb.All(p => byte.TryParse(p.Trim(), out _)))
            return new AciColor(byte.Parse(rgb[0].Trim()), byte.Parse(rgb[1].Trim()), byte.Parse(rgb[2].Trim()));
        return null;
    }

    private static Lineweight Lineweight(double mm)
    {
        var all = Enum.GetValues<Lineweight>().Where(w => (short)w >= 0).ToArray();
        int target = (int)Math.Round(mm * 100);
        return all.OrderBy(w => Math.Abs((short)w - target)).First();
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Export/HatchPatterns.cs   (90 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Globalization;
using netDxf;
using netDxf.Entities;

namespace PavementBuildup.Export;

/// <summary>
/// Pattern line definitions for the hatches the built-in standard uses, in acadiso (millimetre) units.
/// A DXF hatch carries its own pattern lines, so the DXF needs these to show the pattern.
/// ANSI31, ANSI37 and EARTH follow AutoCAD's acad.pat; AR-SAND, AR-CONC and GRAVEL are close
/// look-alikes (re-pick the hatch in AutoCAD to get the exact pattern).
/// </summary>
internal static class HatchPatterns
{
    // acad.pat style: "angle, x,y, dx,dy [, dash, gap ...]" in inches; converted to mm below.
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string[]> Definitions = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        ["ANSI31"] = new[] { "45, 0,0, 0,.125" },
        ["ANSI37"] = new[] { "45, 0,0, 0,.125", "135, 0,0, 0,.125" },
        ["EARTH"] = new[]
        {
            "0, 0,0, .25,.25, .25,-.25",
            "0, 0,.09375, .25,.25, .25,-.25",
            "0, 0,.1875, .25,.25, .25,-.25",
            "90, .03125,.21875, .25,.25, .25,-.25",
            "90, .125,.21875, .25,.25, .25,-.25",
            "90, .21875,.21875, .25,.25, .25,-.25",
        },
        ["AR-SAND"] = new[]
        {
            "37.5, 0,0, 1.123,.682, 0,-1.37",
            "7.5, 0,0, 2.25,.293, 0,-2.27",
            "-32.5, -.25,0, 1.11,-.69, 0,-1.32",
            "-82.5, -.4,0, .53,-.07, 0,-.55",
            "12.5, .3,.15, 1.6,.45, 0,-1.9",
        },
        ["AR-CONC"] = new[]
        {
            "50, 0,0, 4.12,-5.89, .75,-8.25",
            "355, 0,0, -2.5,6.08, .6,-7.65",
            "100.45, .59,-.05, 5.6,8.12, .64,-7.84",
            "46.18, 0,2, 6.24,-8.91, 1.12,-12.37",
            "96.18, .88,1.91, 7.48,10.68, .96,-11.77",
            "351.18, 0,2, 7.48,10.68, .9,-11.74",
            "21, 1,1.5, 4.32,-6.17, 0,-6.11",
            "326, 1.5,1.25, -3.65,5.04, 0,-5.6",
            "71, 2.5,.6, 5.6,8, 0,-9.3",
        },
        ["GRAVEL"] = new[]
        {
            "0, 0,0, 1,.8, .22,-.78",
            "62, .22,0, 1.1,.85, .2,-.9",
            "118, .31,.18, 1.2,.9, .2,-1",
            "195, .1,.35, .9,.75, .18,-.72",
            "300, .35,.5, 1.05,.95, .16,-.89",
            "25, .5,.7, 1.3,.7, .15,-1.15",
        },
    };

    /// <summary>The DXF pattern for a hatch spec, scaled and rotated; SOLID is a solid fill.</summary>
    public static HatchPattern For(string name, double scale, double angleDeg)
    {
        if (string.Equals(name, "SOLID", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return HatchPattern.Solid;

        var lines = Definitions.TryGetValue(name, out var known) ? known : Definitions["ANSI31"];
        var pattern = new HatchPattern(name.ToUpperInvariant(), name.ToUpperInvariant());
        foreach (var line in lines)
            pattern.LineDefinitions.Add(Parse(line));
        pattern.Type = HatchType.Predefined;
        pattern.Scale = scale > 0 ? scale : 1;
        pattern.Angle = angleDeg;
        return pattern;
    }

    private static HatchPatternLineDefinition Parse(string line)
    {
        const double inch = 25.4; // acad.pat inches -> acadiso millimetres
        var v = line.Split(',').Select(s => double.Parse(s.Trim(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)).ToArray();
        var def = new HatchPatternLineDefinition
        {
            Angle = v[0],
            Origin = new Vector2(v[1] * inch, v[2] * inch),
            Delta = new Vector2(v[3] * inch, v[4] * inch),
        };
        foreach (var dash in v.Skip(5))
            def.DashPattern.Add(dash * inch);
        return def;
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Export/SvgPreview.cs   (137 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Globalization;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;
using PavementBuildup.Core;

namespace PavementBuildup.Export;

/// <summary>The detail as an SVG picture (for the web page), drawn from the same layout as the DXF.</summary>
public static class SvgPreview
{
    /// <summary>SVG markup, or null when the build-up can't be laid out (<paramref name="error"/> says why).</summary>
    public static string? Render(Buildup buildup, DetailSettings settings, CadStandard standard, out string? error)
    {
        DetailGeometry g;
        try
        {
            g = DetailLayout.Build(buildup, settings, standard, 1.0);
            error = null;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ArgumentException or FormatException)
        {
            error = ex.Message;
            return null;
        }

        double th = g.TextHeight;
        double longest = g.Labels.Count == 0 ? 0 : g.Labels.Max(l => l.Text.Split('\n').Max(line => line.Length)) * th * 0.62;
        double titleWidth = g.Titles.Count == 0 ? 0 : g.Titles.Max(t => t.Text.Length * t.Height * 0.62);
        double minX = Math.Min(g.Dimensions.Count > 0 ? g.Dimensions.Min(d => d.X) - th * 3 : -th, g.Width / 2 - titleWidth / 2 - th);
        double maxX = Math.Max(g.Labels.Count > 0 ? g.Labels[0].TextAt.X + longest : g.Width, g.Width / 2 + titleWidth / 2) + th;
        double minY = new[] { g.Subgrade?.Bottom ?? g.InterfaceLevels[^1] }
            .Concat(g.Labels.Select(l => l.TextAt.Y - l.LineCount * th)).Concat(g.Titles.Select(t => t.At.Y - t.Height)).Min() - th * 2;
        double maxY = th * 2;

        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        string N(double v) => v.ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        string X(double x) => N(x - minX);
        string Y(double y) => N(maxY - y);
        double w = maxX - minX, h = maxY - minY;
        sb.Append($"<svg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" viewBox=\"0 0 {N(w)} {N(h)}\" font-family=\"Arial Nova, Arial, sans-serif\">");
        sb.Append(Patterns(th));
        sb.Append($"<rect width=\"{N(w)}\" height=\"{N(h)}\" fill=\"#fff\"/>");

        var left = g.Edges.Count > 0 ? g.Edges[0] : new List<Pt> { new(0, 0), new(0, -1) };
        var right = g.Edges.Count > 1 ? g.Edges[1] : new List<Pt> { new(g.Width, 0), new(g.Width, -1) };
        string Points(IEnumerable<Pt> pts) => string.Join(" ", pts.Select(p => X(p.X) + "," + Y(p.Y)));

        foreach (var band in g.Bands.Append(g.Subgrade).OfType<Band>())
        {
            var loop = EdgeProfile.Band(left, right, band.Top, band.Bottom);
            sb.Append($"<polygon points=\"{Points(loop)}\" fill=\"{Fill(band.Hatch?.Pattern)}\" stroke=\"none\"/>");
            if (band.Existing)
                sb.Append($"<polygon points=\"{Points(loop)}\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"#888\" stroke-width=\"{N(th * 0.12)}\"/>");
        }
        double stroke = th * 0.12;
        foreach (var y in g.InterfaceLevels.Concat(g.LiftLevels))
            sb.Append($"<line x1=\"{X(EdgeProfile.XAt(left, y))}\" y1=\"{Y(y)}\" x2=\"{X(EdgeProfile.XAt(right, y))}\" y2=\"{Y(y)}\" stroke=\"#111\" stroke-width=\"{N(stroke)}\"/>");
        foreach (var y in g.MembraneLevels)
            sb.Append($"<line x1=\"{X(EdgeProfile.XAt(left, y))}\" y1=\"{Y(y)}\" x2=\"{X(EdgeProfile.XAt(right, y))}\" y2=\"{Y(y)}\" stroke=\"#C0392B\" stroke-width=\"{N(stroke * 2.5)}\"/>");
        foreach (var edge in g.Edges)
            sb.Append($"<polyline points=\"{Points(edge)}\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"#111\" stroke-width=\"{N(stroke)}\"/>");
        foreach (var bar in g.BarLines)
            sb.Append($"<line x1=\"{X(0)}\" y1=\"{Y(bar.Y)}\" x2=\"{X(g.Width)}\" y2=\"{Y(bar.Y)}\" stroke=\"#A84933\" stroke-width=\"{N(bar.Diameter)}\"/>");
        foreach (var bar in g.Bars)
            sb.Append($"<circle cx=\"{X(bar.Center.X)}\" cy=\"{Y(bar.Center.Y)}\" r=\"{N(bar.Diameter / 2)}\" fill=\"#A84933\"/>");

        double thin = th * 0.06;
        foreach (var l in g.Labels)
        {
            sb.Append($"<polyline points=\"{Points(new[] { l.Anchor, l.Elbow, new Pt(l.TextAt.X - th * 0.5, l.TextAt.Y) })}\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"#333\" stroke-width=\"{N(thin)}\"/>");
            foreach (var a in l.ExtraAnchors.Prepend(l.Anchor))
            {
                sb.Append($"<line x1=\"{X(a.X)}\" y1=\"{Y(a.Y)}\" x2=\"{X(l.Elbow.X)}\" y2=\"{Y(l.Elbow.Y)}\" stroke=\"#333\" stroke-width=\"{N(thin)}\"/>");
                sb.Append($"<circle cx=\"{X(a.X)}\" cy=\"{Y(a.Y)}\" r=\"{N(th * 0.18)}\" fill=\"#111\"/>");
            }
            var lines = l.Text.Split('\n');
            double first = l.TextAt.Y + (lines.Length - 1) * th * 0.8; // middle-left, like the CAD MText
            for (int i = 0; i < lines.Length; i++)
                sb.Append($"<text x=\"{X(l.TextAt.X)}\" y=\"{Y(first - i * th * 1.6)}\" font-size=\"{N(th)}\" dominant-baseline=\"middle\" fill=\"#111\">{WebUtility.HtmlEncode(lines[i])}</text>");
        }

        foreach (var d in g.Dimensions)
        {
            sb.Append($"<line x1=\"{X(d.X)}\" y1=\"{Y(d.Top)}\" x2=\"{X(d.X)}\" y2=\"{Y(d.Bottom)}\" stroke=\"#024360\" stroke-width=\"{N(thin)}\"/>");
            foreach (var y in new[] { d.Top, d.Bottom })
            {
                sb.Append($"<line x1=\"{X(d.X - th * 0.4)}\" y1=\"{Y(y)}\" x2=\"{X(d.X + th * 0.4)}\" y2=\"{Y(y)}\" stroke=\"#024360\" stroke-width=\"{N(thin)}\"/>");
                sb.Append($"<line x1=\"{X(d.X)}\" y1=\"{Y(y)}\" x2=\"{X(d.RefX)}\" y2=\"{Y(y)}\" stroke=\"#024360\" stroke-width=\"{N(thin * 0.6)}\"/>");
            }
            double mid = (d.Top + d.Bottom) / 2;
            sb.Append($"<text x=\"{X(d.X - th * 0.35)}\" y=\"{Y(mid)}\" font-size=\"{N(th * 0.85)}\" text-anchor=\"middle\" fill=\"#024360\" transform=\"rotate(-90 {X(d.X - th * 0.35)} {Y(mid)})\">{WebUtility.HtmlEncode(d.Text)}</text>");
        }

        foreach (var t in g.Titles)
        {
            string anchor = t.Centered ? "middle" : "start";
            string deco = t.Underline ? " text-decoration=\"underline\" font-weight=\"bold\"" : "";
            sb.Append($"<text x=\"{X(t.At.X)}\" y=\"{Y(t.At.Y)}\" font-size=\"{N(t.Height)}\" text-anchor=\"{anchor}\" dominant-baseline=\"middle\" fill=\"#024360\"{deco}>{WebUtility.HtmlEncode(t.Text)}</text>");
        }

        sb.Append("</svg>");
        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private static string Fill(string? pattern) => pattern?.ToUpperInvariant() switch
    {
        null => "#fff",
        "SOLID" => "#555",
        "AR-SAND" => "url(#p-sand)",
        "AR-CONC" => "url(#p-conc)",
        "GRAVEL" => "url(#p-gravel)",
        "EARTH" => "url(#p-earth)",
        "ANSI37" => "url(#p-cross)",
        _ => "url(#p-diag)",
    };

    /// <summary>Look-alike fills for the common hatch patterns, sized from the text height.</summary>
    private static string Patterns(double th)
    {
        string N(double v) => v.ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        double s = th * 1.6, sw = th * 0.05;
        return "<defs>"
            + $"<pattern id=\"p-diag\" width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" patternUnits=\"userSpaceOnUse\" patternTransform=\"rotate(45)\"><rect width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" fill=\"#fff\"/><line x1=\"0\" y1=\"0\" x2=\"0\" y2=\"{N(s)}\" stroke=\"#666\" stroke-width=\"{N(sw)}\"/></pattern>"
            + $"<pattern id=\"p-cross\" width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" patternUnits=\"userSpaceOnUse\" patternTransform=\"rotate(45)\"><rect width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" fill=\"#fff\"/><path d=\"M0 0V{N(s)}M0 0H{N(s)}\" stroke=\"#666\" stroke-width=\"{N(sw)}\"/></pattern>"
            + $"<pattern id=\"p-sand\" width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" patternUnits=\"userSpaceOnUse\"><rect width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" fill=\"#FBF8EE\"/>"
                + string.Concat(new[] { (.1, .2), (.55, .1), (.8, .45), (.3, .6), (.65, .8), (.15, .9), (.45, .4) }.Select(p => $"<circle cx=\"{N(p.Item1 * s)}\" cy=\"{N(p.Item2 * s)}\" r=\"{N(sw * 1.4)}\" fill=\"#555\"/>"))
                + "</pattern>"
            + $"<pattern id=\"p-conc\" width=\"{N(s * 2)}\" height=\"{N(s * 2)}\" patternUnits=\"userSpaceOnUse\"><rect width=\"{N(s * 2)}\" height=\"{N(s * 2)}\" fill=\"#F2F0EA\"/>"
                + $"<path d=\"M{N(s * .2)} {N(s * .3)}l{N(s * .3)} {N(s * .15)}l-{N(s * .2)} {N(s * .25)}z M{N(s * 1.2)} {N(s * 1.1)}l{N(s * .25)} -{N(s * .2)}l{N(s * .1)} {N(s * .3)}z M{N(s * .5)} {N(s * 1.5)}l{N(s * .3)} 0\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"#666\" stroke-width=\"{N(sw)}\"/>"
                + string.Concat(new[] { (.9, .3), (1.6, .6), (.3, 1.1), (1.1, 1.7), (1.7, 1.5) }.Select(p => $"<circle cx=\"{N(p.Item1 * s)}\" cy=\"{N(p.Item2 * s)}\" r=\"{N(sw * 1.2)}\" fill=\"#666\"/>"))
                + "</pattern>"
            + $"<pattern id=\"p-gravel\" width=\"{N(s * 1.5)}\" height=\"{N(s * 1.5)}\" patternUnits=\"userSpaceOnUse\"><rect width=\"{N(s * 1.5)}\" height=\"{N(s * 1.5)}\" fill=\"#F6F1E2\"/>"
                + $"<path d=\"M{N(s * .1)} {N(s * .2)}l{N(s * .35)} -{N(s * .1)}l{N(s * .1)} {N(s * .3)}l-{N(s * .3)} {N(s * .1)}z M{N(s * .8)} {N(s * .7)}l{N(s * .4)} {N(s * .05)}l-{N(s * .1)} {N(s * .35)}l-{N(s * .35)} -{N(s * .1)}z M{N(s * .2)} {N(s * 1.05)}l{N(s * .3)} {N(s * .1)}l-{N(s * .15)} {N(s * .25)}z\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"#777\" stroke-width=\"{N(sw)}\"/></pattern>"
            + $"<pattern id=\"p-earth\" width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" patternUnits=\"userSpaceOnUse\" patternTransform=\"rotate(45)\"><rect width=\"{N(s)}\" height=\"{N(s)}\" fill=\"#F3EBDD\"/><path d=\"M0 {N(s * .15)}H{N(s * .5)}M0 {N(s * .3)}H{N(s * .5)}M0 {N(s * .45)}H{N(s * .5)}M{N(s * .6)} {N(s * .5)}V{N(s)}M{N(s * .75)} {N(s * .5)}V{N(s)}M{N(s * .9)} {N(s * .5)}V{N(s)}\" stroke=\"#8B6B4A\" stroke-width=\"{N(sw)}\"/></pattern>"
            + "</defs>";
    }
}

# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Reports/ExcelReport.cs   (259 lines)

using System.Globalization;
using ClosedXML.Excel;
using PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

namespace PavementBuildup.Reports;

/// <summary>
/// The design calculation as an Excel workbook: a Summary sheet (job, result, inputs, results, a link to the
/// HTML report), a What-if sheet, one sheet per check, and Notes. Numbers are real numbers, so they can be
/// used in other sheets.
/// </summary>
public static class ExcelReport
{
    private static readonly XLColor Band = XLColor.FromHtml("#024360");
    private static readonly XLColor Alabaster = XLColor.FromHtml("#F7F4E7");
    private static readonly XLColor Line = XLColor.FromHtml("#DED7C4");
    private static readonly XLColor Ok = XLColor.FromHtml("#325442");
    private static readonly XLColor Bad = XLColor.FromHtml("#A84933");
    private static readonly XLColor Muted = XLColor.FromHtml("#6F6A5D");

    /// <param name="htmlFileName">The HTML report's file name next to the workbook (linked from the Summary), or null.</param>
    public static void Save(CalcResult design, string path, string? project, string? engineer, string? checker, string? htmlFileName)
    {
        using var wb = new XLWorkbook();
        wb.Properties.Title = design.Title;
        wb.Properties.Subject = project ?? "";
        wb.Properties.Author = engineer ?? "";

        var ws = wb.Worksheets.Add("Summary");
        int row = Header(ws, design.Title, design.Reference);
        row = Meta(ws, row, project, engineer, checker);

        var headline = ws.Cell(row, 1);
        headline.Value = (design.Pass == false ? "✖ " : "✔ ") + design.Headline;
        headline.Style.Font.Bold = true;
        headline.Style.Font.FontSize = 13;
        headline.Style.Font.FontColor = design.Pass == false ? Bad : Ok;
        ws.Range(row, 1, row, 4).Merge();
        row += 1;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(htmlFileName))
        {
            var link = ws.Cell(row, 1);
            link.Value = "Open the HTML calculation report";
            link.SetHyperlink(new XLHyperlink(new Uri(Uri.EscapeDataString(htmlFileName).Replace("%2F", "/"), UriKind.Relative)));
            link.Style.Font.FontColor = Band;
            link.Style.Font.Underline = XLFontUnderlineValues.Single;
            row += 1;
        }
        row += 1;

        row = Lines(ws, row, "Inputs", design.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind == LineKind.Input).ToList(), withNote: false);
        row = Lines(ws, row + 1, "Results", design.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind != LineKind.Input).ToList(), withNote: true);
        if (design.Parts.Count > 0)
        {
            row++;
            ws.Cell(row, 1).Value = "Each check is on its own sheet:";
            ws.Cell(row, 1).Style.Font.FontColor = Muted;
            row++;
            for (int i = 0; i < design.Parts.Count; i++, row++)
            {
                var name = SheetName(i + 1, design.Parts[i].Title);
                var c = ws.Cell(row, 1);
                c.Value = $"{design.Parts[i].Title}  →  {design.Parts[i].Headline}";
                c.SetHyperlink(new XLHyperlink($"'{name}'!A1"));
                c.Style.Font.FontColor = design.Parts[i].Pass == false ? Bad : Band;
            }
        }
        Finish(ws);

        if (design.Tables.Count > 0)
        {
            var wi = wb.Worksheets.Add("What if");
            int r = Header(wi, "What if", design.Title);
            foreach (var (title, columns, rows) in design.Tables)
            {
                Section(wi, r++, title, columns.Length);
                for (int c = 0; c < columns.Length; c++) HeadCell(wi.Cell(r, c + 1), columns[c]);
                r++;
                foreach (var cells in rows)
                {
                    bool current = cells[0].StartsWith("►", StringComparison.Ordinal);
                    for (int c = 0; c < cells.Length; c++)
                    {
                        var cell = wi.Cell(r, c + 1);
                        SetValue(cell, cells[c].TrimStart('►', ' '));
                        if (current) { cell.Style.Font.Bold = true; cell.Style.Fill.BackgroundColor = Alabaster; }
                    }
                    r++;
                }
                r++;
            }
            Finish(wi);
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < design.Parts.Count; i++)
        {
            var part = design.Parts[i];
            var ps = wb.Worksheets.Add(SheetName(i + 1, part.Title));
            int r = Header(ps, part.Title, part.Reference);
            var h = ps.Cell(r, 1);
            h.Value = (part.Pass == false ? "✖ " : "") + part.Headline;
            h.Style.Font.Bold = true;
            h.Style.Font.FontColor = part.Pass == false ? Bad : Ok;
            ps.Range(r, 1, r, 4).Merge();
            r += 2;
            r = Lines(ps, r, "Inputs", part.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind == LineKind.Input).ToList(), withNote: false);
            r = Lines(ps, r + 1, "Calculation", part.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind != LineKind.Input).ToList(), withNote: true);
            if (part.Notes.Count > 0)
            {
                r++;
                foreach (var n in part.Notes.Distinct()) { ps.Cell(r, 1).Value = "• " + n; ps.Cell(r, 1).Style.Font.FontColor = Muted; r++; }
            }
            var back = ps.Cell(r + 1, 1);
            back.Value = "← Back to the summary";
            back.SetHyperlink(new XLHyperlink("'Summary'!A1"));
            back.Style.Font.FontColor = Band;
            Finish(ps);
        }

        var notes = design.Notes.Distinct().ToList();
        if (notes.Count > 0)
        {
            var ns = wb.Worksheets.Add("Notes");
            int r = Header(ns, "Notes", design.Title);
            foreach (var n in notes) { ns.Cell(r++, 1).Value = "• " + n; }
            ns.Cell(r + 1, 1).Value = "Calculated with TTW Pavement from the company design workbooks. Results are a design aid: check them before use.";
            ns.Cell(r + 1, 1).Style.Font.FontColor = Muted;
            Finish(ns);
        }

        wb.Worksheet("Summary").SetTabActive();
        wb.SaveAs(path);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ layout helpers ----------

    private static int Header(IXLWorksheet ws, string title, string reference)
    {
        var range = ws.Range(1, 1, 2, 4);
        range.Style.Fill.BackgroundColor = Band;
        ws.Cell(1, 1).Value = title;
        ws.Cell(1, 1).Style.Font.FontSize = 16;
        ws.Cell(1, 1).Style.Font.FontColor = Alabaster;
        ws.Cell(2, 1).Value = "TTW Pavement design calculation" + (reference.Length > 0 ? "   ·   " + reference : "");
        ws.Cell(2, 1).Style.Font.FontColor = XLColor.FromHtml("#8ECAEF");
        ws.Cell(2, 1).Style.Font.FontSize = 9;
        ws.Row(1).Height = 24;
        return 4;
    }

    private static int Meta(IXLWorksheet ws, int row, string? project, string? engineer, string? checker)
    {
        foreach (var (label, value) in new[]
        {
            ("Project", project), ("Designed", engineer), ("Checked", checker),
            ("Date", DateTime.Now.ToString("d MMM yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        })
        {
            ws.Cell(row, 1).Value = label;
            ws.Cell(row, 1).Style.Font.FontColor = Muted;
            ws.Cell(row, 2).Value = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) ? "-" : value.Trim();
            row++;
        }
        return row + 1;
    }

    private static void Section(IXLWorksheet ws, int row, string title, int width)
    {
        var c = ws.Cell(row, 1);
        c.Value = title;
        c.Style.Font.Bold = true;
        c.Style.Font.FontColor = Band;
        ws.Range(row, 1, row, Math.Max(1, width)).Style.Border.BottomBorder = XLBorderStyleValues.Medium;
        ws.Range(row, 1, row, Math.Max(1, width)).Style.Border.BottomBorderColor = Band;
    }

    private static void HeadCell(IXLCell c, string text)
    {
        c.Value = text;
        c.Style.Font.Bold = true;
        c.Style.Font.FontSize = 9;
        c.Style.Font.FontColor = Muted;
        c.Style.Border.BottomBorder = XLBorderStyleValues.Thin;
        c.Style.Border.BottomBorderColor = Line;
    }

    private static int Lines(IXLWorksheet ws, int row, string title, List<CalcLine> lines, bool withNote)
    {
        if (lines.Count == 0) return row;
        Section(ws, row++, title, withNote ? 4 : 3);
        HeadCell(ws.Cell(row, 1), withNote ? "Step" : "Input");
        HeadCell(ws.Cell(row, 2), "Value");
        HeadCell(ws.Cell(row, 3), "Unit");
        if (withNote) HeadCell(ws.Cell(row, 4), "Note");
        row++;
        foreach (var l in lines)
        {
            ws.Cell(row, 1).Value = l.Label;
            SetValue(ws.Cell(row, 2), l.Value);
            ws.Cell(row, 2).Style.Alignment.Horizontal = XLAlignmentHorizontalValues.Right;
            ws.Cell(row, 3).Value = l.Unit;
            if (withNote) ws.Cell(row, 4).Value = l.Reference;
            var style = ws.Range(row, 1, row, withNote ? 4 : 3).Style;
            style.Border.BottomBorder = XLBorderStyleValues.Hair;
            style.Border.BottomBorderColor = Line;
            switch (l.Kind)
            {
                case LineKind.Result: style.Font.Bold = true; break;
                case LineKind.Ok: style.Font.Bold = true; style.Font.FontColor = Ok; break;
                case LineKind.Fail or LineKind.Warning: style.Font.Bold = true; style.Font.FontColor = Bad; break;
            }
            ws.Cell(row, 4).Style.Font.FontColor = l.Kind is LineKind.Fail or LineKind.Warning ? Bad : Muted;
            row++;
        }
        return row;
    }

    /// <summary>Numbers as numbers (so they can be used in formulas), everything else as text.</summary>
    private static void SetValue(IXLCell cell, string text)
    {
        var t = text.Trim();
        if (double.TryParse(t, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var v) && !t.StartsWith('+'))
        {
            cell.Value = v;
            cell.Style.NumberFormat.Format = t.Contains('E', StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "0.00E+00" : "General";
        }
        else
            cell.Value = t;
    }

    private static void Finish(IXLWorksheet ws)
    {
        ws.Style.Font.FontName = "Arial";
        ws.Column(1).Width = 58;
        ws.Column(2).Width = 14;
        ws.Column(3).Width = 12;
        ws.Column(4).Width = 34;
        for (int c = 5; c <= 8; c++) ws.Column(c).Width = 12;
        ws.PageSetup.PageOrientation = XLPageOrientation.Portrait;
        ws.PageSetup.FitToPages(1, 0);
        ws.SheetView.FreezeRows(2);
        ws.ShowGridLines = false;
    }

    /// <summary>"3 Racks - interior post" (≤ 31 characters, no characters Excel refuses in sheet names).</summary>
    public static string SheetName(int index, string title)
    {
        var shortTitle = title
            .Replace("Uniformly distributed load (UDL)", "UDL")
            .Replace("Wheel stress - ", "T34 ")
            .Replace("Wheel loading - ", "Wheels ")
            .Replace("Post loading - ", "Posts ")
            .Replace("Punching shear - interior post", "Punching shear");
        shortTitle = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(shortTitle, @"\s*\([^)]*\)", ""); // drop "(Westergaard)" etc.
        var clean = new string(shortTitle.Where(ch => "[]:*?/\\".IndexOf(ch) < 0).ToArray()).Trim();
        var name = $"{index} {clean}";
        return name.Length <= 31 ? name : name[..31].TrimEnd();
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup/Brand.cs   (295 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace PavementBuildup;

/// <summary>
/// TTW brand for the plugin's windows (colours, fonts, buttons, header and status line), matching
/// TTW Advanced Tools: Water Blue header with the logo top right, flat square buttons, status line.
/// </summary>
internal static class Brand
{
    public static readonly Color HeaderBg = Hex("024360");     // Water Blue
    public static readonly Color HeaderFg = Hex("F7F4E7");     // Alabaster
    public static readonly Color HeaderSubtle = Hex("8ECAEF");
    public static readonly Color Accent = Hex("024360");
    public static readonly Color AccentHover = Hex("03587E");
    public static readonly Color AccentFg = Hex("F7F4E7");
    public static readonly Color Bg = Hex("F7F4E7");
    public static readonly Color Surface = Hex("FFFDF8");
    public static readonly Color Hover = Hex("EFEADB");
    public static readonly Color Selection = Hex("DDE8EC");
    public static readonly Color Border = Hex("DED7C4");
    public static readonly Color Text = Hex("1E1711");
    public static readonly Color Heading = Hex("024360");
    public static readonly Color Muted = Hex("6F6A5D");
    public static readonly Color Success = Hex("325442");
    public static readonly Color Failure = Hex("A84933");

    /// <summary>Marks a button as the main (Water Blue) one when it isn't the form's AcceptButton.</summary>
    public const string PrimaryTag = "brand-primary";

    /// <summary>Marks a control that draws its own brand look, so <see cref="Apply"/> leaves it alone.</summary>
    public const string SelfPaintTag = "brand-self";

    private static readonly string Family = Installed("Arial Nova") ? "Arial Nova" : "Arial";

    /// <summary>Body text (12.5 px).</summary>
    public static Font Body(FontStyle style = FontStyle.Regular) => new(Family, 9.5f, style);

    public static Font Sized(float points, FontStyle style = FontStyle.Regular) => new(Family, points, style);

    /// <summary>
    /// Brands a form: font and colours now (so child fonts built from it match), header and status line,
    /// and the controls' styles once everything has been added.
    /// </summary>
    public static void Decorate(Form form, string title, string subtitle, Control? status = null)
    {
        form.Font = Body();
        form.BackColor = Bg;
        form.ForeColor = Text;
        if (Image("brandmark-waterblue.png") is Bitmap mark)
        {
            try { form.Icon = Icon.FromHandle(mark.GetHicon()); }
            catch (System.Runtime.InteropServices.ExternalException) { /* keep the default icon */ }
        }
        // Docked controls added last are laid out first: header on top, status along the bottom.
        if (status is not null)
            form.Controls.Add(StatusBar(status));
        form.Controls.Add(Header(title, subtitle, out var titleLabel, out var subtitleLabel));
        form.Tag = new HeaderLabels(titleLabel, subtitleLabel);
        form.Load += (_, _) => Apply(form, form);
    }

    /// <summary>Screen scaling (1.0 at 100%, 1.5 at 150%): text sizes grow with it, so fixed pixel sizes must too.</summary>
    public static readonly float Scale = ScreenScale();

    /// <summary>A size in pixels at 100%, scaled for the screen.</summary>
    public static int Px(int pixels) => (int)Math.Round(pixels * Scale);

    public static int HeaderHeight => Px(70);
    public static int StatusHeight => Px(30);

    /// <summary>
    /// Window size for a form whose content is one top-docked panel under the header (docked panels
    /// don't make an AutoSize window wide enough, so the size is set from the panel's preferred size).
    /// </summary>
    public static Size FitClient(Control content, int minWidth, bool hasStatus)
    {
        var preferred = content.GetPreferredSize(new Size(minWidth, 0));
        int width = Math.Max(minWidth, preferred.Width);
        return new Size(width, HeaderHeight + preferred.Height + (hasStatus ? StatusHeight : 0));
    }

    /// <summary>Changes the header text after <see cref="Decorate"/>.</summary>
    public static void SetHeader(Form form, string title, string subtitle)
    {
        if (form.Tag is HeaderLabels h)
        {
            h.Title.Text = title;
            h.Subtitle.Text = subtitle;
        }
    }

    private sealed record HeaderLabels(Label Title, Label Subtitle);

    /// <summary>
    /// "Back to menu": closes the window with DialogResult.Retry, which TTWPVMT takes as "show the menu again".
    /// </summary>
    public static Button BackButton() => new() { Text = "Back to menu", AutoSize = true, DialogResult = DialogResult.Retry };

    /// <summary>A step heading in the window ("① Paste or type the build-up").</summary>
    public static Label Step(string text) => new()
    {
        Text = text, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Heading, Font = Sized(11f, FontStyle.Bold),
        Margin = new Padding(3, 8, 3, 6),
    };

    private static Control Header(string title, string subtitle, out Label titleLabel, out Label subtitleLabel)
    {
        var band = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = HeaderHeight, BackColor = HeaderBg, ColumnCount = 2, RowCount = 2,
            Padding = new Padding(22, 10, 22, 10),
        };
        band.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100));
        band.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        band.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        band.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));

        titleLabel = new Label
        {
            Text = title, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = HeaderFg, BackColor = HeaderBg,
            Font = Sized(18f), Margin = new Padding(0),
        };
        subtitleLabel = new Label
        {
            Text = subtitle, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = HeaderSubtle, BackColor = HeaderBg,
            Font = Sized(8.5f), Margin = new Padding(1, 1, 0, 0),
        };
        band.Controls.Add(titleLabel, 0, 0);
        band.Controls.Add(subtitleLabel, 0, 1);

        if (Image("logo-alabaster.png") is { } logo)
        {
            var box = new PictureBox
            {
                Image = logo, SizeMode = PictureBoxSizeMode.Zoom, BackColor = HeaderBg,
                Height = Px(30), Width = Px(30) * logo.Width / Math.Max(1, logo.Height),
                Anchor = AnchorStyles.Right, Margin = new Padding(12, 0, 0, 0),
            };
            band.Controls.Add(box, 1, 0);
            band.SetRowSpan(box, 2);
        }
        return band;
    }

    private static Control StatusBar(Control content)
    {
        var bar = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = StatusHeight, BackColor = Bg, Padding = new Padding(14, 7, 14, 6) };
        bar.Paint += (_, e) =>
        {
            using var pen = new Pen(Border);
            e.Graphics.DrawLine(pen, 0, 0, bar.Width, 0);
        };
        content.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        content.Font = Sized(8.75f);
        if (content is Label l) l.AutoEllipsis = true;
        bar.Controls.Add(content);
        return bar;
    }

    /// <summary>Flat brand styles for every control (buttons, boxes, tables, links).</summary>
    public static void Apply(Form form, Control parent)
    {
        foreach (Control c in parent.Controls)
        {
            switch (c)
            {
                case Button self when Equals(self.Tag, SelfPaintTag):
                    break; // paints itself (menu tiles)
                case Button b:
                    StyleButton(b, primary: ReferenceEquals(form.AcceptButton, b) || Equals(b.Tag, PrimaryTag));
                    break;
                case TextBoxBase t:
                    t.BackColor = t.ReadOnly && t is TextBox ? Bg : Surface;
                    t.ForeColor = Text;
                    t.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle;
                    break;
                case ComboBox cb:
                    cb.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
                    cb.BackColor = Surface;
                    break;
                case NumericUpDown n:
                    n.BackColor = Surface;
                    n.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle;
                    break;
                case DataGridView g:
                    StyleGrid(g);
                    break;
                case LinkLabel link:
                    link.LinkColor = Accent;
                    link.VisitedLinkColor = Accent;
                    link.ActiveLinkColor = AccentHover;
                    break;
                case TabPage page:
                    page.UseVisualStyleBackColor = false;
                    page.BackColor = Bg;
                    break;
                case CheckBox check:
                    check.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
                    break;
            }
            if (c.HasChildren && c is not DataGridView and not NumericUpDown)
                Apply(form, c);
        }
    }

    private static void StyleButton(Button b, bool primary)
    {
        b.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
        b.UseVisualStyleBackColor = false;
        b.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
        b.TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter;
        b.MinimumSize = new Size(b.MinimumSize.Width, Math.Max(b.MinimumSize.Height, primary ? 36 : 30));
        if (!b.AutoSize)
            b.Height = Math.Max(b.Height, b.MinimumSize.Height); // fixed width: no side padding, so the text stays on one line
        else if (b.Padding == Padding.Empty)
            b.Padding = new Padding(primary ? 14 : 8, 0, primary ? 14 : 8, 0);
        if (primary)
        {
            b.BackColor = Accent;
            b.ForeColor = AccentFg;
            b.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            b.FlatAppearance.MouseOverBackColor = AccentHover;
            b.FlatAppearance.MouseDownBackColor = AccentHover;
            b.Font = new Font(b.Font, FontStyle.Bold);
        }
        else
        {
            b.BackColor = Surface;
            b.ForeColor = Accent;
            b.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 1;
            b.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = Border;
            b.FlatAppearance.MouseOverBackColor = Hover;
            b.FlatAppearance.MouseDownBackColor = Selection;
        }
    }

    private static void StyleGrid(DataGridView g)
    {
        g.BackgroundColor = Surface;
        g.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle;
        g.GridColor = Border;
        g.EnableHeadersVisualStyles = false;
        g.ColumnHeadersBorderStyle = DataGridViewHeaderBorderStyle.Single;
        g.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = HeaderBg;
        g.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = HeaderFg;
        g.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = HeaderBg;
        g.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = HeaderFg;
        g.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.Font = new Font(g.Font, FontStyle.Bold);
        g.ColumnHeadersHeightSizeMode = DataGridViewColumnHeadersHeightSizeMode.AutoSize;
        g.RowHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = Bg;
        g.RowHeadersDefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = Selection;
        g.DefaultCellStyle.BackColor = Surface;
        g.DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Text;
        g.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = Selection;
        g.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Text;
    }

    /// <summary>A logo compiled into the plugin (Assets\*.png), or null if it's missing.</summary>
    public static Bitmap? Image(string name)
    {
        try
        {
            using var stream = typeof(Brand).Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream("PavementBuildup.Assets." + name);
            return stream is null ? null : new Bitmap(stream);
        }
        catch (ArgumentException)
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static float ScreenScale()
    {
        try
        {
            using var g = Graphics.FromHwnd(IntPtr.Zero);
            return Math.Max(1f, g.DpiX / 96f);
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ArgumentException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or OutOfMemoryException)
        {
            return 1f;
        }
    }

    private static bool Installed(string family)
    {
        using var fonts = new System.Drawing.Text.InstalledFontCollection();
        return fonts.Families.Any(f => string.Equals(f.Name, family, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }

    private static Color Hex(string rgb) => Color.FromArgb(
        Convert.ToInt32(rgb[..2], 16), Convert.ToInt32(rgb[2..4], 16), Convert.ToInt32(rgb[4..], 16));
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup/PreviewPanel.cs   (188 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using PavementBuildup.Core;

namespace PavementBuildup;

/// <summary>Schematic preview of the detail, drawn from the same layout the CAD drawer uses.</summary>
internal sealed class PreviewPanel : Panel
{
    private DetailGeometry? _geometry;
    private string? _error;

    // Zoom (mouse wheel, about the cursor) and pan (drag); double-click fits again.
    private float _zoom = 1f;
    private PointF _pan;
    private Point? _dragFrom;

    public PreviewPanel()
    {
        DoubleBuffered = true;
        BackColor = Color.White;
        BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle;
        ResizeRedraw = true;
        MouseWheel += (_, e) =>
        {
            float old = _zoom;
            _zoom = Math.Clamp(_zoom * (e.Delta > 0 ? 1.2f : 1 / 1.2f), 1f, 12f);
            // Keep the point under the cursor still.
            _pan = new PointF(e.X - (e.X - _pan.X) * _zoom / old, e.Y - (e.Y - _pan.Y) * _zoom / old);
            if (_zoom == 1f) _pan = PointF.Empty;
            Invalidate();
        };
        MouseDown += (_, e) => { if (e.Button == MouseButtons.Left) { _dragFrom = e.Location; Cursor = Cursors.SizeAll; } };
        MouseUp += (_, _) => { _dragFrom = null; Cursor = Cursors.Default; };
        MouseMove += (_, e) =>
        {
            if (_dragFrom is not { } from || _zoom <= 1f) return; // nothing to move at "fit"
            _pan = new PointF(_pan.X + e.X - from.X, _pan.Y + e.Y - from.Y);
            _dragFrom = e.Location;
            Invalidate();
        };
        DoubleClick += (_, _) => { _zoom = 1f; _pan = PointF.Empty; Invalidate(); };
        MouseEnter += (_, _) => Focus(); // the wheel goes to the control with focus
    }

    public void UpdatePreview(Buildup buildup, DetailSettings settings, CadStandard standard)
    {
        try
        {
            _geometry = DetailLayout.Build(buildup, settings, standard, 1.0);
            _error = null;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ArgumentException or FormatException)
        {
            _geometry = null;
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        Invalidate();
    }

    protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnPaint(e);
        var gr = e.Graphics;
        gr.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

        if (_geometry is not { } g)
        {
            TextRenderer.DrawText(gr, _error ?? "Add layers to see a preview.", Font, ClientRectangle, Color.Gray,
                TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter | TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.WordBreak);
            return;
        }

        // Fit everything, measuring the real text (labels to the right, the centred title block underneath).
        using var probe = new Font(Font.FontFamily, 100f, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
        using var probeBold = new Font(Font.FontFamily, 100f, FontStyle.Bold | FontStyle.Underline, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
        double WidthOf(string text, double height, bool bold) =>
            text.Split('\n').Max(line => gr.MeasureString(line, bold ? probeBold : probe).Width) / 100.0 * height;
        double th = g.TextHeight;
        double minX = g.Dimensions.Count > 0 ? g.Dimensions.Min(d => d.X) - th * 3 : -th;
        double maxX = g.Width + th;
        foreach (var l in g.Labels)
            maxX = Math.Max(maxX, l.TextAt.X + WidthOf(l.Text, th, false) + th);
        foreach (var t in g.Titles)
        {
            double w = WidthOf(t.Text, t.Height, t.Underline);
            minX = Math.Min(minX, (t.Centered ? t.At.X - w / 2 : t.At.X) - th);
            maxX = Math.Max(maxX, (t.Centered ? t.At.X + w / 2 : t.At.X + w) + th);
        }
        double minY = new[] { g.Subgrade?.Bottom ?? g.InterfaceLevels[^1] }
            .Concat(g.Labels.Select(l => l.TextAt.Y - l.LineCount * th))
            .Concat(g.Titles.Select(t => t.At.Y - t.Height)).Min() - th;
        double maxY = th * 1.5;

        float pad = 10;
        double fit = Math.Min((Width - 2 * pad) / (maxX - minX), (Height - 2 * pad) / (maxY - minY));
        // Text never goes below 6 px, so on a very small picture it is wider than its share: shrink to fit it.
        for (int i = 0; i < 3 && g.TextHeight * fit < 6; i++)
        {
            double grow = 6 / (g.TextHeight * fit);                 // how much bigger the text is drawn
            double textPart = g.Labels.Count == 0 ? 0 : g.Labels.Max(l => WidthOf(l.Text, th, false)) * (grow - 1);
            fit = Math.Min((Width - 2 * pad) / (maxX - minX + textPart), (Height - 2 * pad) / (maxY - minY));
        }
        double scale = fit * _zoom;
        // Centred in the panel at "fit"; zoom and pan move it from there.
        float offX = (float)((Width - (maxX - minX) * fit) / 2) * _zoom + _pan.X * (_zoom == 1f ? 0 : 1);
        float offY = (float)((Height - (maxY - minY) * fit) / 2) * _zoom + _pan.Y * (_zoom == 1f ? 0 : 1);
        PointF P(double x, double y) => new((float)(offX + (x - minX) * scale), (float)(offY + (maxY - y) * scale));

        float fontPx = Math.Max(6f, (float)(g.TextHeight * scale));
        using var font = new Font(Font.FontFamily, fontPx, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
        using var outline = new Pen(Color.Black, 1.5f);
        using var thin = new Pen(Color.DimGray, 1f);

        foreach (var band in g.Bands.Concat(g.Subgrade is null ? Array.Empty<Band>() : new[] { g.Subgrade }))
        {
            using var brush = BrushFor(band.Hatch?.Pattern);
            // Same as the drawing: the fill stays inside the side edges and their break lines.
            var left = g.Edges.Count > 0 ? g.Edges[0] : new List<Pt> { new(0, 0), new(0, -1) };
            var right = g.Edges.Count > 1 ? g.Edges[1] : new List<Pt> { new(g.Width, 0), new(g.Width, -1) };
            gr.FillPolygon(brush, EdgeProfile.Band(left, right, band.Top, band.Bottom).Select(p => P(p.X, p.Y)).ToArray());
        }
        foreach (var y in g.InterfaceLevels.Concat(g.LiftLevels))
            gr.DrawLine(outline, P(0, y), P(g.Width, y));
        using (var membrane = new Pen(Color.Red, 2.5f))
            foreach (var y in g.MembraneLevels)
                gr.DrawLine(membrane, P(0, y), P(g.Width, y));
        foreach (var edge in g.Edges)
            gr.DrawLines(outline, edge.Select(p => P(p.X, p.Y)).ToArray());

        using (var rebar = new Pen(Color.Firebrick, 2f))
            foreach (var bl in g.BarLines)
                gr.DrawLine(rebar, P(0, bl.Y), P(g.Width, bl.Y));
        foreach (var bar in g.Bars)
        {
            var c = P(bar.Center.X, bar.Center.Y);
            float r = Math.Max(1.5f, (float)(bar.Diameter * scale / 2));
            gr.FillEllipse(Brushes.Firebrick, c.X - r, c.Y - r, 2 * r, 2 * r);
        }

        foreach (var l in g.Labels)
        {
            gr.DrawLines(thin, new[] { P(l.Anchor.X, l.Anchor.Y), P(l.Elbow.X, l.Elbow.Y), P(l.TextAt.X, l.TextAt.Y) });
            foreach (var anchor in l.ExtraAnchors.Prepend(l.Anchor))
            {
                var a = P(anchor.X, anchor.Y);
                gr.DrawLine(thin, a, P(l.Elbow.X, l.Elbow.Y));
                gr.FillEllipse(Brushes.Black, a.X - 2, a.Y - 2, 4, 4);
            }
            var t = P(l.TextAt.X, l.TextAt.Y);
            var size = gr.MeasureString(l.Text, font);
            gr.DrawString(l.Text, font, Brushes.Black, t.X + 2, t.Y - size.Height / 2); // centred like MText middle-left
        }

        foreach (var d in g.Dimensions)
        {
            var top = P(d.X, d.Top);
            var bottom = P(d.X, d.Bottom);
            gr.DrawLine(thin, top, bottom);
            gr.DrawLine(thin, top.X - 3, top.Y, top.X + 3, top.Y);
            gr.DrawLine(thin, bottom.X - 3, bottom.Y, bottom.X + 3, bottom.Y);
        }

        foreach (var t in g.Titles)
        {
            using var f = new Font(Font.FontFamily, Math.Max(6f, (float)(t.Height * scale)), t.Underline ? FontStyle.Underline | FontStyle.Bold : FontStyle.Regular, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
            var p = P(t.At.X, t.At.Y);
            var size = gr.MeasureString(t.Text, f);
            gr.DrawString(t.Text, f, Brushes.Black, t.Centered ? p.X - size.Width / 2 : p.X, p.Y - size.Height / 2);
        }

        TextRenderer.DrawText(gr, _zoom > 1f ? "Double-click to fit" : "Scroll to zoom, drag to move", Font,
            new Rectangle(0, 0, Width - 6, Height - 4), Color.Gray, TextFormatFlags.Right | TextFormatFlags.Bottom);
    }

    private static Brush BrushFor(string? pattern) => pattern?.ToUpperInvariant() switch
    {
        "AR-SAND" => new HatchBrush(HatchStyle.Percent20, Color.DimGray, Color.FromArgb(235, 235, 235)),
        "AR-CONC" => new HatchBrush(HatchStyle.DottedGrid, Color.Gray, Color.FromArgb(225, 225, 225)),
        "GRAVEL" => new HatchBrush(HatchStyle.SolidDiamond, Color.Silver, Color.FromArgb(245, 240, 225)),
        "EARTH" => new HatchBrush(HatchStyle.WideUpwardDiagonal, Color.SaddleBrown, Color.FromArgb(240, 230, 215)),
        "ANSI37" => new HatchBrush(HatchStyle.DiagonalCross, Color.Gray, Color.White),
        "SOLID" => new SolidBrush(Color.DimGray),
        null => new SolidBrush(Color.White),
        _ => new HatchBrush(HatchStyle.ForwardDiagonal, Color.Gray, Color.White),
    };
}
