namespace PavementBuildup;

internal static class Program
{
    [STAThread]
    private static void Main()
    {
        // Default standard: _support\_Standards\TTW.json next to the Standalone Tool folder.
        PavementBuildup.Core.StandardStore.SearchFrom.Add(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
        // Anything dropped straight into _Exports goes into its date folder (_Exports\yyyy-MM-dd).
        AppFolders.FileLooseExports();
                Application.SetHighDpiMode(HighDpiMode.SystemAware);
        Application.EnableVisualStyles();
        Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
        Application.Run(new StandaloneForm());
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Standalone/AppFolders.cs   (142 lines)
################################################################################

namespace PavementBuildup;

/// <summary>
/// The release folders around the app (TTW_Pave_Tool):
///   Standalone Tool\TTW Pavement.exe, Standalone Tool\_Exports, _support\_Standards.
/// Saves go to _Exports\date\job number\pavement name (e.g. _Exports\2026-10-13\241001\Type A - Carpark) and the standards list reads
/// _support\_Standards when they're there.
/// </summary>
internal static class AppFolders
{
    private static string AppDir => AppContext.BaseDirectory;

    /// <summary>Standalone Tool\_Exports (created when first needed), or Documents if it can't be.</summary>
    public static string Exports
    {
        get
        {
            var dir = Path.Combine(AppDir, "_Exports");
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                return dir;
            }
            catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
            {
                return Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments);
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Today's export folder, _Exports\yyyy-MM-dd (created now, so a save dialog can open in it).</summary>
    public static string Today
    {
        get
        {
            var root = Exports;
            var dir = Path.Combine(root, DayFolder(DateTime.Today));
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                return dir;
            }
            catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
            {
                return root;
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>"2026-10-13": year, month, day (sorts in date order).</summary>
    public static string DayFolder(DateTime day) => day.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

    /// <summary>Removes today's folder again if nothing was saved into it (e.g. the save was cancelled).</summary>
    public static void TidyToday() => Tidy(Path.Combine(AppDir, "_Exports", DayFolder(DateTime.Today)));

    /// <summary>
    /// The folder for one design's files, created now: _Exports\date\job number\pavement name
    /// (e.g. _Exports\2026-10-13\241001\Type A - Carpark). Blank parts become "No job number" / "Untitled".
    /// </summary>
    public static string ForDesign(string? jobNumber, string? title)
    {
        var dir = Path.Combine(Today, Part(jobNumber, "No job number"), Part(title, "Untitled"));
        try
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
            return dir;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            return Today;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>After a cancelled save: removes the design folder and its parents up to _Exports if they're empty.</summary>
    public static void Tidy(string folder)
    {
        try
        {
            var root = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(AppDir, "_Exports"));
            var dir = new DirectoryInfo(folder);
            while (dir is not null && dir.FullName.Length > root.Length && dir.FullName.StartsWith(root, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                if (!dir.Exists || dir.EnumerateFileSystemInfos().Any()) break;
                dir.Delete();
                dir = dir.Parent;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or System.Security.SecurityException) { }
    }

    /// <summary>A folder name from free text: no characters Windows refuses, no trailing dots/spaces, not too long.</summary>
    public static string Part(string? text, string fallback)
    {
        var bad = Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars().Concat(new[] { '/', '\\', ':', '*', '?', '"', '<', '>', '|' }).ToHashSet();
        var s = new string((text ?? "").Select(c => bad.Contains(c) ? '-' : c).ToArray()).Trim().TrimEnd('.', ' ');
        if (s.Length > 60) s = s[..60].TrimEnd('.', ' ');
        return s.Length == 0 ? fallback : s;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Files put straight into _Exports are moved into a folder for the day they were last changed
    /// (_Exports\yyyy-MM-dd). A file with the same name already there keeps both ("name (2).dxf").
    /// </summary>
    public static void FileLooseExports()
    {
        try
        {
            var root = Path.Combine(AppDir, "_Exports");
            if (!Directory.Exists(root)) return;
            foreach (var file in Directory.EnumerateFiles(root).ToList())
            {
                var info = new FileInfo(file);
                if (info.Attributes.HasFlag(FileAttributes.Hidden) || info.Name.StartsWith('~')) continue; // temp / lock files
                var dir = Path.Combine(root, DayFolder(info.LastWriteTime.Date));
                Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                var target = Path.Combine(dir, info.Name);
                for (int i = 2; File.Exists(target); i++)
                    target = Path.Combine(dir, $"{Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(info.Name)} ({i}){info.Extension}");
                try { File.Move(file, target); }
                catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException) { /* open in another program: leave it */ }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException) { }
    }

    /// <summary>_support\_Standards next to the Standalone Tool folder, or null if it isn't there.</summary>
    public static string? Standards
    {
        get
        {
            try
            {
                var dir = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(AppDir, "..", "_support", "_Standards"));
                return Directory.Exists(dir) ? dir : null;
            }
            catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ArgumentException or IOException or NotSupportedException)
            {
                return null;
            }
        }
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Standalone/StandaloneSettings.cs   (41 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Text.Json;

namespace PavementBuildup;

/// <summary>What the standalone app remembers: the standards folder, the chosen standard and the scale.</summary>
internal sealed class StandaloneSettings
{
    public string? StandardsFolder { get; set; }
    public string? StandardFile { get; set; }
    public double Scale { get; set; } = 10;

    private static string FilePath => Path.Combine(
        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData), "PavementBuildup", "standalone.json");

    public static StandaloneSettings Load()
    {
        try
        {
            return File.Exists(FilePath)
                ? JsonSerializer.Deserialize<StandaloneSettings>(File.ReadAllText(FilePath)) ?? new()
                : new();
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or JsonException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            return new();
        }
    }

    public void Save()
    {
        try
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(FilePath)!);
            File.WriteAllText(FilePath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(this, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            // Not being able to remember the folder isn't worth interrupting the user for.
        }
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Standalone/StandaloneForm.cs   (398 lines)
################################################################################

using PavementBuildup.Core;
using PavementBuildup.Export;

namespace PavementBuildup;

/// <summary>
/// TTW Pavement without Civil 3D: paste a build-up, check the picture, save a DXF (or copy it for TTWPVMT).
/// Same reader, layout and standard as the plugin.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class StandaloneForm : Form
{
    private readonly TextBox _text = new()
    {
        Multiline = true, ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Vertical, Dock = DockStyle.Fill, AcceptsReturn = true,
        PlaceholderText = "e.g.  60mm pavers, 30mm sand, 150mm road base, subgrade\r\n\r\n" +
                          "Or paste anything: a list, a table from Excel, a spec paragraph, a note from a drawing.",
    };
    private readonly Label _summary = new() { AutoSize = true, Margin = new Padding(3, 8, 3, 2) };
    private readonly Label _status = new() { ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Text = "Type or paste a build-up. It's read as you type." };
    private readonly PreviewPanel _preview = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Fill };
    private readonly System.Windows.Forms.Timer _timer = new() { Interval = 500 };
    private readonly ToolTip _tips = new();

    private readonly ComboBox _scale = new() { DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDown, Width = 80 };
    private readonly NumericUpDown _width = Num(100, 20000, 1000, 100);
    private readonly ComboBox _units = new() { DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList, Width = 110 };
    private readonly CheckBox _dims = new() { Text = "Dimensions", AutoSize = true, Checked = true };
    private readonly CheckBox _breaks = new() { Text = "Break lines", AutoSize = true, Checked = true };
    private readonly CheckBox _title = new() { Text = "Title", AutoSize = true, Checked = true };
    private readonly CheckBox _subgrade = new() { Text = "Subgrade", AutoSize = true, Checked = true };
    private readonly ComboBox _standards = new() { DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList, Width = 230 };
    private readonly Label _folderLabel = new() { AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Brand.Muted, MaximumSize = new Size(Brand.Px(300), 0) };
    private readonly StandaloneSettings _settings = StandaloneSettings.Load();
    private bool _loadingStandards;

    private CadStandard _standard = CadStandard.CreateDefault();
    private readonly DesignWizard _design = new();
    private Buildup? _buildup;
    private readonly Button _save = new() { Text = "Save DXF…", AutoSize = true };

    public StandaloneForm()
    {
        Text = "TTW Pavement";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        Size = new Size(Brand.Px(1180), Brand.Px(760));
        MinimumSize = new Size(Brand.Px(860), Brand.Px(560));
        Brand.Decorate(this, "TTW Pavement", "Draw a build-up or design one  ·  no Civil 3D needed", _status);

        _units.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "Metres", "Millimetres" });
        _units.SelectedIndex = 0;
        _scale.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "1:1", "1:2", "1:5", "1:10", "1:20", "1:25", "1:50", "1:100" });
        _scale.Text = "1:" + CadStandard.Number(_settings.Scale > 0 ? _settings.Scale : 10);
        FillStandards();

        var split = new SplitContainer { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, BackColor = Brand.Bg };
        Load += (_, _) => { split.SplitterDistance = (int)(split.Width * 0.42); FitSummary(); };
        split.SplitterMoved += (_, _) => FitSummary();
        split.Panel1.Controls.Add(BuildLeft());
        split.Panel2.Controls.Add(BuildRight());

        // Two tabs: draw a detail, or design one (the company's calculation workbooks).
        var tabs = new TabControl { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Padding = new Point(14, 5) };
        var detailTab = new TabPage("Detail") { Padding = new Padding(4, 8, 4, 4) };
        detailTab.Controls.Add(split);
        var designTab = new TabPage("Design") { Padding = new Padding(4, 4, 4, 4) };
        var design = _design;
        design.UseBuildup += text =>
        {
            _text.Text = text;
            tabs.SelectedTab = detailTab;
            _status.ForeColor = Brand.Success;
            _status.Text = "Design sent to the Detail tab. Check the picture, then Save DXF.";
        };
        designTab.Controls.Add(design);
        tabs.TabPages.Add(detailTab);
        tabs.TabPages.Add(designTab);
        var root = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Padding = new Padding(18, 10, 18, 8) };
        root.Controls.Add(tabs);
        Controls.Add(root);
        root.BringToFront(); // fills what the header and status line leave

        _text.TextChanged += (_, _) => { _timer.Stop(); _timer.Start(); };
        _timer.Tick += (_, _) => { _timer.Stop(); ReadText(); };
        _width.ValueChanged += (_, _) => Redraw();
        _scale.TextChanged += (_, _) => Redraw();
        _scale.Leave += (_, _) => _scale.Text = "1:" + CadStandard.Number(ScaleValue());
        _standards.SelectedIndexChanged += (_, _) => { if (!_loadingStandards) UseSelectedStandard(); };
        Resize += (_, _) => FitSummary();
        _units.SelectedIndexChanged += (_, _) => Redraw();
        foreach (var c in new[] { _dims, _breaks, _title, _subgrade }) c.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => Redraw();
        FormClosed += (_, _) => { _settings.Scale = ScaleValue(); _settings.Save(); _timer.Dispose(); _tips.Dispose(); };
        ShowSummary(null);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- layout -------

    private Control BuildLeft()
    {
        var t = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ColumnCount = 1, Padding = new Padding(0, 0, 10, 0) };
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100));
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));

        t.Controls.Add(Brand.Step("① Paste or type the build-up (any wording)"));
        _text.Font = new Font("Consolas", 10f);
        t.Controls.Add(_text);
        t.Controls.Add(_summary);

        var paste = new Button { Text = "Paste", AutoSize = true };
        paste.Click += (_, _) => { if (Clipboard.ContainsText()) _text.Text = Clipboard.GetText(); };
        var ai = new Button { Text = "Ask AI (free)…", AutoSize = true };
        ai.Click += (_, _) => AskAi();
        var clear = new Button { Text = "Clear", AutoSize = true };
        clear.Click += (_, _) => _text.Clear();
        var buttons = new FlowLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, WrapContents = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top, Margin = new Padding(0, 6, 0, 0) };
        buttons.Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { paste, ai, clear });
        t.Controls.Add(buttons);

        // Fixed rows (a wrapping panel hid the second row).
        var options = new GroupBox { Text = "Drawing", Dock = DockStyle.Top, AutoSize = true, AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink, Margin = new Padding(3, 10, 3, 3) };
        var rows = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, AutoSize = true, AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink, ColumnCount = 1 };
        var folder = new Button { Text = "Standards folder…", AutoSize = true };
        folder.Click += (_, _) => ChooseFolder();
        rows.Controls.Add(Row(Lbl("Scale"), _scale, Lbl("Width (mm)"), _width, Lbl("Units"), _units));
        rows.Controls.Add(Row(_dims, _breaks, _title, _subgrade));
        rows.Controls.Add(Row(Lbl("CAD standard"), _standards));
        rows.Controls.Add(Row(folder, _folderLabel)); // own row so the button is never cut off
        options.Controls.Add(rows);
        t.Controls.Add(options);
        return t;
    }

    private Control BuildRight()
    {
        var t = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ColumnCount = 1, Padding = new Padding(10, 0, 0, 0) };
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100));
        t.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        t.Controls.Add(Brand.Step("② Check the picture"));
        t.Controls.Add(_preview);

        _save.Tag = Brand.PrimaryTag;
        _save.Font = Brand.Sized(11f, FontStyle.Bold);
        _save.MinimumSize = new Size(0, Brand.Px(40));
        _save.Click += (_, _) => SaveDxf();
        var copy = new Button { Text = "Copy for TTWPVMT", AutoSize = true, MinimumSize = new Size(0, Brand.Px(40)) };
        copy.Click += (_, _) => CopyForPlugin();
        _tips.SetToolTip(copy, "Copies the tidy build-up. In Civil 3D: TTWPVMT > New pavement detail, paste into box ①.");
        var bar = new FlowLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top, WrapContents = false, Margin = new Padding(0, 10, 0, 0) };
        bar.Controls.Add(Brand.Step("③"));
        bar.Controls.Add(_save);
        bar.Controls.Add(copy);
        copy.Margin = new Padding(10, 3, 3, 3);
        t.Controls.Add(bar);
        return t;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- behaviour ----

    private void ReadText()
    {
        var errors = new List<string>();
        var read = SmartReader.Read(_text.Text, errors);
        var parsed = read.Parsed;
        ShowSummary(parsed);
        var all = errors.Select(e => "Couldn't read: " + e).Concat(read.Notes).ToList();
        _status.ForeColor = errors.Count > 0 ? Brand.Failure : Brand.Muted;
        _status.Text = all.Count == 0
            ? (parsed.Layers.Count > 0 ? "Read it as written." : "Type or paste a build-up. It's read as you type.")
            : "Read: " + string.Join("  ·  ", all.Take(3)) + (all.Count > 3 ? $"  (+{all.Count - 3} more, hover)" : "");
        _tips.SetToolTip(_status, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, all));
        _buildup = parsed.Layers.Count == 0 ? null : parsed.ToBuildup(parsed.Name ?? "PAVEMENT BUILD-UP");
        Redraw();
    }

    private void ShowSummary(BuildupParseResult? parsed)
    {
        if (parsed is null || parsed.Layers.Count == 0)
        {
            _summary.ForeColor = parsed is null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_text.Text) ? Brand.Text : Brand.Failure;
            _summary.Font = Brand.Body();
            _summary.Text = parsed is null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_text.Text)
                ? "Write each layer with its thickness, top to bottom, e.g. 60mm pavers, 30mm sand, 150mm road base, subgrade."
                : "Can't find any layers yet. Give each layer a thickness (e.g. 150mm road base), or try Ask AI (free).";
            return;
        }
        double total = parsed.Layers.Sum(l => l.TotalMm);
        var parts = new List<string> { $"✔ {parsed.Layers.Count} layer{(parsed.Layers.Count == 1 ? "" : "s")}, {total:0.#} mm deep" };
        if (parsed.SubgradeText is { } sg) parts.Add("sits on: " + BuildupParser.Core(sg));
        if (parsed.Name is { } n) parts.Add("title: " + n);
        _summary.ForeColor = Brand.Success;
        _summary.Font = Brand.Body(FontStyle.Bold);
        _summary.Text = string.Join("  ·  ", parts);
    }

    /// <summary>"1:10", "10" or "1 : 20" → 10, 20; anything else keeps 10.</summary>
    private double ScaleValue()
    {
        var text = _scale.Text.Replace(" ", "");
        int colon = text.IndexOf(':');
        if (colon >= 0) text = text[(colon + 1)..];
        return double.TryParse(text, System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Float, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var v) && v is >= 1 and <= 1000 ? v : 10;
    }

    private DetailSettings Settings() => new()
    {
        ScaleDenominator = ScaleValue(),
        WidthMm = (double)_width.Value,
        UnitsOverride = _units.SelectedIndex == 1 ? "MM" : "M",
        ShowDimensions = _dims.Checked,
        ShowBreakLines = _breaks.Checked,
        ShowTitle = _title.Checked,
        CreateBlock = false,
    };

    private void Redraw()
    {
        _save.Enabled = _buildup is not null;
        if (_buildup is null)
        {
            _preview.UpdatePreview(new Buildup { Layers = new() }, Settings(), _standard);
            return;
        }
        _buildup.ShowSubgrade = _subgrade.Checked;
        _preview.UpdatePreview(_buildup, Settings(), _standard);
    }

    private void SaveDxf()
    {
        if (_buildup is null) return;
        // _Exports\date\job number (from the Design tab)\pavement name
        var folder = AppFolders.ForDesign(_design.JobNumber, _buildup.Name);
        using var dlg = new SaveFileDialog
        { InitialDirectory = folder,
            Filter = "DXF drawing (*.dxf)|*.dxf",
            Title = "Save the pavement detail",
            FileName = SafeFileName(_buildup.Name) + ".dxf",
        };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK)
        {
            AppFolders.Tidy(folder); // cancelled: no empty folders left behind
            return;
        }
        try
        {
            using (var fs = File.Create(dlg.FileName))
                DxfExport.Write(_buildup, Settings(), _standard, fs);
            _status.ForeColor = Brand.Success;
            _status.Text = $"Saved {Path.GetFileName(dlg.FileName)}. In CAD: INSERT it (or drag the file into the drawing).";
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or ArgumentException)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Could not save the DXF: " + ex.Message, Text, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        }
    }

    private void CopyForPlugin()
    {
        if (_buildup is null) return;
        Clipboard.SetText(BuildupParser.Format(_buildup));
        _status.ForeColor = Brand.Success;
        _status.Text = "Copied. In Civil 3D: TTWPVMT > New pavement detail, paste into box ①.";
    }

    private void AskAi()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_text.Text))
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Type or paste the build-up into the box first.", Text);
            return;
        }
        var message = AiPrompt.ForChat(AiPrompt.Load(_standard.AiPromptFile), _text.Text);
        Clipboard.SetText(message);
        var url = "https://claude.ai/new" + (message.Length < 6000 ? "?q=" + Uri.EscapeDataString(message) : "");
        try
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(url) { UseShellExecute = true });
        }
        catch (System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception) { /* no browser: the message is on the clipboard */ }
        _status.ForeColor = Brand.Muted;
        _status.Text = "Claude.ai opened (Ctrl+V if the box is empty). Send it, copy the reply, and paste it into box ①.";
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- CAD standard ---

    private sealed record StandardChoice(string Label, string? Path)
    {
        public override string ToString() => Label;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The standards to pick from: the built-in one, the personal one the plugin saves, and every .json
    /// in the chosen standards folder (e.g. the company folder on the shared drive).
    /// </summary>
    private void FillStandards()
    {
        _loadingStandards = true;
        _standards.Items.Clear();
        _standards.Items.Add(new StandardChoice("Built-in TTW standard", null));
        if (File.Exists(StandardStore.DefaultPath))
            _standards.Items.Add(new StandardChoice("My saved standard (plugin)", StandardStore.DefaultPath));
        // The company standards in _support\_Standards unless another folder was picked.
        var folder = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_settings.StandardsFolder) ? AppFolders.Standards : _settings.StandardsFolder;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(folder) && Directory.Exists(folder))
        {
            foreach (var file in Directory.EnumerateFiles(folder, "*.json").OrderBy(f => f, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                _standards.Items.Add(new StandardChoice(Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(file)
                    + (StandardStore.SamePath(file, StandardStore.CompanyDefault) ? " (default)" : ""), file));
            _folderLabel.Text = "Folder: " + folder;
        }
        else
        {
            _folderLabel.Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(folder)
                ? "Pick a standards folder to list the company standards (.json)."
                : "Standards folder not found: " + folder;
        }

        // A different folder was picked: still offer the company default.
        if (StandardStore.CompanyDefault is { } company && !_standards.Items.Cast<StandardChoice>().Any(c => StandardStore.SamePath(c.Path, company)))
            _standards.Items.Insert(1, new StandardChoice("TTW (default)", company));
        // The engineer's last choice, else the company default _support\_Standards\TTW.json.
        var wanted = _standards.Items.Cast<StandardChoice>()
            .FirstOrDefault(c => StandardStore.SamePath(c.Path, _settings.StandardFile))
            ?? _standards.Items.Cast<StandardChoice>().FirstOrDefault(c => StandardStore.SamePath(c.Path, StandardStore.CompanyDefault))
            ?? _standards.Items.Cast<StandardChoice>().FirstOrDefault(c => c.Path == StandardStore.DefaultPath)
            ?? (StandardChoice)_standards.Items[0];
        _standards.SelectedItem = wanted;
        _loadingStandards = false;
        UseSelectedStandard();
    }

    private void UseSelectedStandard()
    {
        if (_standards.SelectedItem is not StandardChoice choice) return;
        try
        {
            _standard = choice.Path is null ? CadStandard.CreateDefault() : StandardStore.Load(choice.Path);
            _settings.StandardFile = choice.Path;
            _settings.Save();
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or InvalidDataException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            _standard = CadStandard.CreateDefault();
            _status.ForeColor = Brand.Failure;
            _status.Text = $"Couldn't read {choice.Label}: {ex.Message} Using the built-in standard.";
        }
        Redraw();
    }

    private void ChooseFolder()
    {
        using var dlg = new FolderBrowserDialog
        {
            Description = "Folder with the CAD standards (.json), e.g. the company folder on the shared drive",
            UseDescriptionForTitle = true,
            InitialDirectory = Directory.Exists(_settings.StandardsFolder) ? _settings.StandardsFolder! : AppFolders.Standards ?? "",
        };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        _settings.StandardsFolder = dlg.SelectedPath;
        _settings.Save();
        FillStandards();
        // Pick the first standard in the folder straight away.
        var first = _standards.Items.Cast<StandardChoice>().FirstOrDefault(c => c.Path is not null && c.Path.StartsWith(dlg.SelectedPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        if (first is not null) _standards.SelectedItem = first;
    }

    /// <summary>The summary wraps to the width of the left column.</summary>
    private void FitSummary()
    {
        if (_summary.Parent is { } p)
            _summary.MaximumSize = new Size(Math.Max(200, p.ClientSize.Width - 12), 0);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers ------

    private static Label Lbl(string text) => new() { Text = text, AutoSize = true };

    private static FlowLayoutPanel Row(params Control[] controls)
    {
        var f = new FlowLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, WrapContents = false, Dock = DockStyle.Top, Margin = Padding.Empty };
        foreach (var c in controls)
            c.Margin = new Padding(3, c is Label ? 8 : 4, 10, 3);
        f.Controls.AddRange(controls);
        return f;
    }

    private static NumericUpDown Num(decimal min, decimal max, decimal value, decimal step) =>
        new() { Minimum = min, Maximum = max, Value = value, Increment = step, Width = 75 };

    private static string SafeFileName(string name)
    {
        var bad = Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars();
        var s = new string(name.Select(c => bad.Contains(c) ? '_' : c).ToArray()).Trim();
        return s.Length == 0 ? "pavement detail" : s;
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: src/PavementBuildup.Standalone/DesignWizard.cs   (781 lines)
################################################################################

using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.Json;
using PavementBuildup.Core;
using PavementBuildup.Core.Design;

namespace PavementBuildup;

/// <summary>
/// The design, one step at a time: ① Project → ② Ground → ③ Loads / traffic → ④ Result. Inputs are entered
/// once, read the way engineers type them ("20t", "1e6", "5%"), checked as you go, and the result updates
/// live. The result can be drawn, reported (HTML, prints to PDF), copied, or saved and opened again.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class DesignWizard : UserControl
{
    private enum Kind { Road, Slab }

    /// <summary>A number box with its unit, help line and live check.</summary>
    private sealed class Field
    {
        public required string Key { get; init; }
        public required string Unit { get; init; }
        public required TextBox Box { get; init; }
        public required Label Help { get; init; }
        public required string HelpText { get; init; }
        public double Value { get; set; }
        public bool Ok { get; set; } = true;
    }

    private static readonly string[] StepNames = { "Project", "Ground", "Loads", "Result" };

    private readonly Dictionary<string, Field> _fields = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, CheckBox> _checks = new();
    private readonly Label[] _stepLabels = new Label[4];
    private readonly Panel[] _pages = new Panel[4];
    private readonly Panel _pageHost = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Fill };
    private readonly Button _back = new() { Text = "◀  Back", AutoSize = true };
    private readonly Button _next = new() { Text = "Next  ▶", AutoSize = true, Tag = Brand.PrimaryTag };
    private readonly Label _live = new() { AutoSize = true, Margin = new Padding(16, 12, 3, 3) };
    private readonly ToolTip _tips = new();
    private int _step;
    private Kind _kind = Kind.Road;
    private CalcResult? _result;
    private string? _error;

    // Step 1
    private readonly TextBox _project = new() { Width = 340, PlaceholderText = "e.g. 123 Smith St carpark" };
    private readonly TextBox _jobNo = new() { Width = 140, PlaceholderText = "e.g. 241001" };
    private readonly TextBox _pavementName = new() { Width = 340, PlaceholderText = "e.g. Type A - Carpark" };

    /// <summary>The job number typed in step 1 (the Detail tab files its DXFs under it too).</summary>
    public string JobNumber => _jobNo.Text.Trim();

    /// <summary>The pavement's name: what the user called it, else the project.</summary>
    private string PavementTitle => _pavementName.Text.Trim().Length > 0 ? _pavementName.Text.Trim() : _project.Text.Trim();
    private readonly TextBox _engineer = new() { Width = 220 };
    private readonly TextBox _checker = new() { Width = 220 };
    private readonly RadioButton _road = new() { AutoSize = true, Checked = true };
    private readonly RadioButton _slab = new() { AutoSize = true };

    // Step 2
    private readonly Label _soilDerived = new() { AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Margin = new Padding(3, 2, 3, 8) };
    private readonly Panel _slabGround = new() { AutoSize = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top };

    // Step 3
    private readonly Panel _roadLoads = new() { AutoSize = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top };
    private readonly Panel _slabLoads = new() { AutoSize = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top };
    private readonly RadioButton _knownDesa = new() { Text = "I know the design traffic (DESA)", AutoSize = true, Checked = true };
    private readonly RadioButton _countDesa = new() { Text = "Work it out from traffic counts", AutoSize = true };
    private readonly ComboBox _desa = new() { Width = 360, DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDown };
    private readonly Panel _countsPanel = new() { AutoSize = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top };
    private readonly ComboBox _grade = new() { Width = 120, DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList };
    private readonly Panel _advanced = new() { AutoSize = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top, Visible = false };

    // Step 4
    private readonly Label _headline = new() { AutoSize = true, Font = Brand.Sized(13f, FontStyle.Bold), Margin = new Padding(3, 4, 3, 8) };
    private readonly DataGridView _grid = new();
    private readonly TextBox _tables = new() { Multiline = true, ReadOnly = true, ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Vertical, Font = new Font("Consolas", 9f), BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None };
    private readonly PreviewPanel _picture = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Fill };

    /// <summary>Raised with build-up text when the engineer sends the design to the Detail tab.</summary>
    public event Action<string>? UseBuildup;

    public DesignWizard()
    {
        Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        BackColor = Brand.Bg;

        Controls.Add(_pageHost);
        Controls.Add(BuildStepBar());
        Controls.Add(BuildFooter());
        _pageHost.BringToFront();

        _pages[0] = BuildProject();
        _pages[1] = BuildGround();
        _pages[2] = BuildLoads();
        _pages[3] = BuildResult();
        foreach (var page in _pages)
        {
            page.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            page.Visible = false;
            _pageHost.Controls.Add(page);
        }
        ShowStep(0);
        Recalculate();
    }

    // ================================================================ frame ====================

    private Control BuildStepBar()
    {
        var bar = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, AutoSize = true, WrapContents = false, Padding = new Padding(0, 6, 0, 8) };
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        {
            int step = i;
            var label = _stepLabels[i] = new Label
            {
                AutoSize = true, Font = Brand.Sized(11f, FontStyle.Bold), Padding = new Padding(10, 6, 12, 6),
                Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 4, 0), Cursor = Cursors.Hand, Text = $"{i + 1}  {StepNames[i]}",
            };
            label.Click += (_, _) => GoTo(step);
            bar.Controls.Add(label);
            if (i < 3)
                bar.Controls.Add(new Label { Text = "›", AutoSize = true, Font = Brand.Sized(14f), ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Margin = new Padding(0, 2, 4, 0) });
        }
        return bar;
    }

    private Control BuildFooter()
    {
        var bar = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = Brand.Px(56), Padding = new Padding(0, 10, 0, 0) };
        _back.MinimumSize = _next.MinimumSize = new Size(Brand.Px(110), Brand.Px(36));
        _back.Click += (_, _) => GoTo(_step - 1);
        _next.Click += (_, _) => GoTo(_step + 1);
        var left = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Left, AutoSize = true, WrapContents = false };
        left.Controls.Add(_back);
        left.Controls.Add(_next);
        left.Controls.Add(_live);
        bar.Controls.Add(left);
        return bar;
    }

    private void GoTo(int step)
    {
        step = Math.Clamp(step, 0, 3);
        // Moving forward: the steps before must be filled in correctly.
        for (int s = _step; s < step; s++)
        {
            var problem = ProblemOnStep(s);
            if (problem is not null)
            {
                ShowStep(s);
                _live.ForeColor = Brand.Failure;
                _live.Text = "✖ " + problem;
                return;
            }
        }
        ShowStep(step);
    }

    private void ShowStep(int step)
    {
        _step = step;
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        {
            _pages[i].Visible = i == step;
            var label = _stepLabels[i];
            label.BackColor = i == step ? Brand.Accent : i < step ? Brand.Selection : Brand.Bg;
            label.ForeColor = i == step ? Brand.AccentFg : i < step ? Brand.Accent : Brand.Muted;
            label.Text = (i < step ? "✔ " : $"{i + 1}  ") + StepNames[i] + (i == 2 ? (_kind == Kind.Road ? " (traffic)" : " (wheels, racks, storage)") : "");
        }
        _back.Enabled = step > 0;
        _next.Visible = step < 3;
        _next.Text = step == 2 ? "See the result  ▶" : "Next  ▶";
        if (step == 3) ShowResult();
        UpdateLive();
    }

    /// <summary>What stops the engineer leaving a step, or null.</summary>
    private string? ProblemOnStep(int step)
    {
        var keys = step switch
        {
            1 => _kind == Kind.Road ? new[] { "cbr" } : new[] { "cbr", "h", "es" },
            2 => _kind == Kind.Road ? RoadLoadKeys() : SlabLoadKeys(),
            _ => Array.Empty<string>(),
        };
        foreach (var key in keys)
            if (_fields.TryGetValue(key, out var f) && !f.Ok)
                return $"Check \"{f.Help.Tag}\": {f.Help.Text}";
        if (step == 2 && _error is not null) return _error;
        return null;
    }

    private string[] RoadLoadKeys() => _knownDesa.Checked ? Array.Empty<string>() : new[] { "aadt", "hv", "growth", "years", "esahv", "df", "ldf" };

    private string[] SlabLoadKeys()
    {
        var keys = new List<string>();
        if (_checks["wheels"].Checked) keys.AddRange(new[] { "axle", "ws", "passes", "life", "tyre" });
        if (_checks["posts"].Checked) keys.AddRange(new[] { "post", "px", "py", "plate", "lf" });
        if (_checks["udl"].Checked) keys.AddRange(new[] { "udl", "aisle", "udlreps" });
        if (_advanced.Visible) keys.AddRange(new[] { "k1w", "k3i", "k3e", "k1p", "k2p", "k1u" });
        return keys.ToArray();
    }

    // ================================================================ step 1: project ==========

    private Panel BuildProject()
    {
        var page = Page(out var t);
        t.Controls.Add(Brand.Step("Which job is this for?"));
        t.Controls.Add(Row(Lbl("Project"), _project, Lbl("Job no."), _jobNo));
        t.Controls.Add(Row(Lbl("Pavement name"), _pavementName));
        t.Controls.Add(Row(Lbl("Designed by"), _engineer, Lbl("Checked by"), _checker));
        t.Controls.Add(Hint("These go on the calculation report. Files are saved in _Exports\\date\\job no.\\pavement name."));
        _pavementName.TextChanged += (_, _) => Recalculate();

        t.Controls.Add(Brand.Step("What are you designing?"));
        t.Controls.Add(Choice(_road, "Road, car park or driveway",
            "Asphalt over crushed rock (granular with thin bituminous surfacing). Austroads + the company design table."));
        t.Controls.Add(Choice(_slab, "Concrete slab",
            "Warehouse floor, hardstand, loading dock: forklifts / trucks, racks and storage loads. CCAA T48 + T34 checks."));
        _road.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => SetKind(_road.Checked ? Kind.Road : Kind.Slab);

        var open = new Button { Text = "Open a saved design…", AutoSize = true };
        open.Click += (_, _) => OpenDesign();
        var advanced = new Button { Text = "Single checks (advanced)…", AutoSize = true };
        advanced.Click += (_, _) => OpenSingleChecks();
        _tips.SetToolTip(advanced, "Each spreadsheet calculation on its own: wheels, posts, punching shear, UDL, deflection, T34, soil.");
        t.Controls.Add(Row(open, advanced));
        return page;
    }

    private Control Choice(RadioButton radio, string title, string text)
    {
        radio.Text = title;
        radio.Font = Brand.Sized(11f, FontStyle.Bold);
        radio.ForeColor = Brand.Heading;
        var panel = new FlowLayoutPanel { FlowDirection = FlowDirection.TopDown, AutoSize = true, WrapContents = false, Margin = new Padding(3, 4, 3, 8) };
        panel.Controls.Add(radio);
        var help = Hint(text);
        help.Margin = new Padding(22, 0, 3, 0);
        panel.Controls.Add(help);
        return panel;
    }

    private void SetKind(Kind kind)
    {
        _kind = kind;
        _slabGround.Visible = kind == Kind.Slab;
        _roadLoads.Visible = kind == Kind.Road;
        _slabLoads.Visible = kind == Kind.Slab;
        ShowStep(_step);
        Recalculate();
    }

    }

    // ================================================================ step 3: loads ============

    private Panel BuildLoads()
    {
        var page = Page(out var t);

        // Road: traffic.
        var road = new TableLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, ColumnCount = 1, Dock = DockStyle.Top };
        road.Controls.Add(Brand.Step("How much traffic over the design life?"));

        slab.Controls.Add(Row(Lbl("Concrete f'c"), _grade, Check("edges", "Check free edges too", true, "Loads at joints without dowels and at slab edges."),
            Check("t34", "T34 cross-checks", true, "Also check wheels with Westergaard / Ioannides / Pickett.")));

        slab.Controls.Add(Section("wheels", "Forklifts / trucks (wheel loads)", true, new[]
        {
            FieldRow("axle", "Heaviest axle load", "tonne", "10", "Loaded forklift front axle or truck axle. Type tonnes or kN (e.g. 98kN)."),
            FieldRow("ws", "Wheel spacing", "m", "1.5", "Centre to centre across the axle."),
            FieldRow("passes", "Passes per day", "", "10", "Over the busiest point."),
            FieldRow("life", "Design life", "years", "20", "Repetitions = passes x 5 days x 52 weeks x years."),
            FieldRow("tyre", "Tyre pressure", "kPa", "700", "For the T34 checks. Solid tyres can be 1000+ kPa."),

        slab.Controls.Add(Section("posts", "Racks (post loads)", false, new[]
        {
            FieldRow("post", "Post load (unfactored)", "kN", "70", "Working load on one rack leg."),
            FieldRow("px", "Post spacing x", "m", "2", "Along the rack."),
            FieldRow("py", "Post spacing y", "m", "1.5", "Across the rack (back to back)."),
            FieldRow("plate", "Base plate width", "mm", "165", "For punching shear."),
            FieldRow("lf", "Load factor for punching", "", "1.5", "P* = factor x P (e.g. 1.5 for live load)."),
        }));
        slab.Controls.Add(Section("udl", "Storage (distributed load)", false, new[]
        {

        adv.Controls.Add(FieldRow("k3e", "k3 edge", "", "1.05", "Geotechnical calibration (T48)."));
        adv.Controls.Add(FieldRow("k1p", "k1 posts", "", "0.8", "0.75-0.85."));
        adv.Controls.Add(FieldRow("k2p", "k2 posts", "", "0.85", "T48 uses 1; lower if racks are loaded and unloaded often."));

    private Control Section(string key, string title, bool on, Control[] rows)
    {
        var box = new GroupBox { AutoSize = true, Dock = DockStyle.Top, Padding = new Padding(8, 4, 8, 6), Margin = new Padding(3, 8, 3, 3), Text = "" };
        var inner = new TableLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, ColumnCount = 1, Dock = DockStyle.Fill };
        var check = Check(key, title, on, "");
        check.Font = Brand.Sized(10.5f, FontStyle.Bold);
        check.ForeColor = Brand.Heading;
        inner.Controls.Add(check);
        var body = new TableLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, ColumnCount = 1, Dock = DockStyle.Top, Visible = on, Margin = new Padding(18, 0, 3, 0) };
        foreach (var r in rows) body.Controls.Add(r);
        inner.Controls.Add(body);
        check.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => body.Visible = check.Checked;
        box.Controls.Add(inner);
        return box;
    }

    // ================================================================ step 4: result ===========

    private Panel BuildResult()
    {
        var page = new Panel { Padding = new Padding(4) };
        var draw = new Button { Text = "Draw this build-up", AutoSize = true, Tag = Brand.PrimaryTag };
        draw.Click += (_, _) => { if (_result?.BuildupText is { } t) UseBuildup?.Invoke(t); };
        var report = new Button { Text = "Calculation report…", AutoSize = true };
        report.Click += (_, _) => SaveReport();
        _tips.SetToolTip(report, "Saves the report as HTML (opens in your browser; print it to PDF) and as an Excel workbook next to it, linked to each other.");
        var copy = new Button { Text = "Copy as text", AutoSize = true };
        copy.Click += (_, _) => { if (_result is not null) Clipboard.SetText(_result.ToText(ProjectLine(), _engineer.Text)); };
        var save = new Button { Text = "Save design…", AutoSize = true };
        save.Click += (_, _) => SaveDesign();
        var again = new Button { Text = "Start a new design", AutoSize = true };
        again.Click += (_, _) => ShowStep(0);
        var actions = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, AutoSize = true, WrapContents = true, Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 6) };
        actions.Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { draw, report, copy, save, again });
        foreach (Control c in actions.Controls) c.Margin = new Padding(0, 3, 8, 3);

        _grid.ReadOnly = true;
        _grid.AllowUserToAddRows = false;
        _grid.RowHeadersVisible = false;
        _grid.AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill;
        _grid.SelectionMode = DataGridViewSelectionMode.FullRowSelect;
        _grid.Columns.Add("label", "Check / step");
        _grid.Columns.Add("value", "Value");
        _grid.Columns.Add("unit", "Unit");
        _grid.Columns.Add("note", "Note");
        _grid.Columns[0].FillWeight = 220;
        _grid.Columns[1].FillWeight = 60;
        _grid.Columns[2].FillWeight = 45;
        _grid.Columns[3].FillWeight = 120;
        _grid.Columns[1].DefaultCellStyle.Alignment = DataGridViewContentAlignment.MiddleRight;
        _grid.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;

        var split = new SplitContainer { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Orientation = Orientation.Vertical, BackColor = Brand.Bg };
        page.HandleCreated += (_, _) => { if (split.Width > 400) split.SplitterDistance = (int)(split.Width * 0.58); };
        var left = new SplitContainer { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, BackColor = Brand.Bg };
        left.Panel1.Controls.Add(_grid);
        _tables.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _tables.BackColor = Brand.Surface;
        left.Panel2.Controls.Add(_tables);
        split.Panel1.Controls.Add(left);
        split.Panel2.Controls.Add(_picture);
        split.Panel2.Padding = new Padding(8, 0, 0, 0);

        page.Controls.Add(split);
        page.Controls.Add(actions);
        page.Controls.Add(_headline);
        _headline.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
        return page;
    }

    private void ShowResult()
    {
        Recalculate();
        _grid.Rows.Clear();
        if (_result is null)
        {
            _headline.ForeColor = Brand.Failure;
            _headline.Text = "✖ " + (_error ?? "Fill in the steps first.");
            _tables.Text = "";
            return;
        }
        _headline.ForeColor = _result.Pass == false ? Brand.Failure : Brand.Success;
        _headline.Text = (_result.Pass == false ? "✖ " : "✔ ") + _result.Headline;
        foreach (var line in _result.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind != LineKind.Input))
        {
            int row = _grid.Rows.Add(line.Label, line.Value, line.Unit, line.Reference);
            var style = _grid.Rows[row].DefaultCellStyle;
            switch (line.Kind)
            {
                case LineKind.Result: style.Font = Brand.Body(FontStyle.Bold); break;
                case LineKind.Ok: style.ForeColor = Brand.Success; style.Font = Brand.Body(FontStyle.Bold); break;
                case LineKind.Fail or LineKind.Warning: style.ForeColor = Brand.Failure; style.Font = Brand.Body(FontStyle.Bold); break;
            }
        }
        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        foreach (var (title, columns, rows) in _result.Tables)
        {
            sb.AppendLine(title.ToUpperInvariant());
            var widths = columns.Select((c, i) => Math.Max(c.Length, rows.Max(r => r[i].Length)) + 2).ToArray();
            sb.AppendLine(string.Concat(columns.Select((c, i) => c.PadRight(widths[i]))));
            foreach (var r in rows) sb.AppendLine(string.Concat(r.Select((c, i) => c.PadRight(widths[i]))));
            sb.AppendLine();
        }
        if (_result.Notes.Count > 0)
        {
            sb.AppendLine("NOTES");
            foreach (var n in _result.Notes.Distinct()) sb.AppendLine("• " + n);
        }
        _tables.Text = sb.ToString().Replace("\n", Environment.NewLine);

        if (_result.BuildupText is { } text)
        {
            var read = SmartReader.Read(text, new List<string>());
            _picture.UpdatePreview(read.Parsed.ToBuildup(read.Parsed.Name ?? "PAVEMENT"), new DetailSettings { ScaleDenominator = 10 }, CadStandard.CreateDefault());
        }
    }

    // ================================================================ calculation ==============

    private double V(string key) => _fields[key].Value;

    private double? Optional(string key) => _fields[key].Box.Text.Trim().Length == 0 ? null : _fields[key].Value;

    private double Desa()
    {
        var text = _desa.Text;
        int i = _desa.Items.IndexOf(text);
        if (i >= 0) return FlexibleDesign.TrafficRows[i].Desa;
        var head = text.Split(new[] { "  -  " }, StringSplitOptions.None)[0];
        if (Inputs.TryRead(head, "ESA", out var v, out var error)) return v;
        throw new ArgumentException("Design traffic: " + error);
    }

    private void Recalculate()
    {
        if (_fields.Count == 0 || !_checks.ContainsKey("wheels")) return;
        _error = null;
        try
        {
            if (!_fields["cbr"].Ok) throw new ArgumentException("Check the subgrade CBR (step 2).");
            string? title = PavementTitle.Length == 0 ? null : PavementTitle.ToUpperInvariant();
            if (_kind == Kind.Road)
            {
                var traffic = _knownDesa.Checked
                    ? new TrafficInputs(Desa())
                    : new TrafficInputs(0, V("aadt"), V("hv"), V("df"), V("ldf"), V("growth"), V("years"), V("esahv"));
                _result = RoadDesign.Design(V("cbr"), traffic, title);
            }
            else
            {
                double fc = ConcreteDesign.Grades[Math.Max(0, _grade.SelectedIndex)];
                var s = new SlabInputs
                {
                    Fc = fc, Cbr = V("cbr"), EsOverride = Optional("es") ?? 0, SoilDepth = V("h"),
                    CheckEdges = _checks["edges"].Checked, T34Checks = _checks["t34"].Checked,
                    Wheels = _checks["wheels"].Checked, AxleTonnes = V("axle"), WheelSpacing = V("ws"), PassesPerDay = V("passes"),
                    Years = V("life"), TyreKpa = V("tyre"), K1Wheel = V("k1w"), K3Interior = V("k3i"), K3Edge = V("k3e"),
                    Posts = _checks["posts"].Checked, PostKn = V("post"), PostX = V("px"), PostY = V("py"), BasePlateMm = V("plate"),
                    LoadFactor = V("lf"), K1Post = V("k1p"), K2Post = V("k2p"),
                    Udl = _checks["udl"].Checked, UdlKpa = V("udl"), AisleWidth = V("aisle"), UdlRepetitions = V("udlreps"), K1Udl = V("k1u"),
                };
                _result = SlabDesign.Design(s, title);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ArgumentException or FormatException or OverflowException)
        {
            _result = null;
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        UpdateDerived();
        UpdateLive();
    }

    private void UpdateDerived()
    {
        var cbr = _fields["cbr"];
        _soilDerived.Text = cbr.Ok && cbr.Value > 0
            ? $"→ k ≈ {ConcreteDesign.KFromCbr(cbr.Value):0.0} kPa/mm,  Es ≈ {ConcreteDesign.EsFromCbr(cbr.Value):0.0} MPa" +
              (cbr.Value < 3 ? "   ·  CBR < 3%: subgrade stabilisation is generally required." : "")
            : "";
    }

    private void UpdateLive()
    {
        if (_step == 0)
        {
            _live.ForeColor = Brand.Muted;
            _live.Text = "Fill in the job, choose what you're designing, then Next.";
            return;
        }
        if (_result is null)
        {
            _live.ForeColor = Brand.Failure;
            _live.Text = "✖ " + (_error ?? "");
            return;
        }
        _live.ForeColor = _result.Pass == false ? Brand.Failure : Brand.Success;
        _live.Text = (_step < 3 ? "Live result: " : "") + _result.Headline;
    }

    // ================================================================ report / save / open =====

    private string ProjectLine() =>
        string.Join("  ·  ", new[] { _project.Text.Trim(), _jobNo.Text.Trim().Length > 0 ? "Job " + _jobNo.Text.Trim() : "" }.Where(s => s.Length > 0));

    private void SaveReport()
    {
        if (_result is null) return;
        var folder = AppFolders.ForDesign(JobNumber, PavementTitle);
        using var dlg = new SaveFileDialog
        { InitialDirectory = folder,
            Filter = "Calculation report (*.html; an Excel copy is saved next to it)|*.html", Title = "Save the calculation report (HTML + Excel)",
            FileName = SafeName($"{JobNumber} {PavementTitle} {(_kind == Kind.Road ? "pavement" : "slab")} design".Trim()) + ".html",
        };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK)
        {
            AppFolders.Tidy(folder); // cancelled: no empty folders left behind
            return;
        }
        try
        {
            // The same report twice, side by side and linked to each other: HTML (to read / print) and Excel (to reuse the numbers).
            var html = dlg.FileName;
            var xlsx = Path.ChangeExtension(html, ".xlsx");
            File.WriteAllText(html, CalcReport.Html(_result, ProjectLine(), _engineer.Text, _checker.Text, Path.GetFileName(xlsx)));
            PavementBuildup.Reports.ExcelReport.Save(_result, xlsx, ProjectLine(), _engineer.Text, _checker.Text, Path.GetFileName(html));
            Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(html) { UseShellExecute = true });
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Could not save or open the report: " + ex.Message, "TTW Pavement", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        }
    }

    private sealed class SavedDesign
    {
        public string Kind { get; set; } = "Road";
        public string Project { get; set; } = "";
        public string JobNo { get; set; } = "";
        public string PavementName { get; set; } = "";
        public string Engineer { get; set; } = "";
        public string Checker { get; set; } = "";
        public string Desa { get; set; } = "";
        public bool FromCounts { get; set; }
        public int Grade { get; set; } = 1;
        public Dictionary<string, string> Fields { get; set; } = new();
        public Dictionary<string, bool> Checks { get; set; } = new();
    }

    private void SaveDesign()
    {
        var saved = new SavedDesign
        {
            Kind = _kind.ToString(), Project = _project.Text, JobNo = _jobNo.Text, PavementName = _pavementName.Text, Engineer = _engineer.Text, Checker = _checker.Text,
            Desa = _desa.Text, FromCounts = _countDesa.Checked, Grade = _grade.SelectedIndex,
            Fields = _fields.ToDictionary(f => f.Key, f => f.Value.Box.Text),
            Checks = _checks.ToDictionary(c => c.Key, c => c.Value.Checked),
        };
        var folder = AppFolders.ForDesign(JobNumber, PavementTitle);
        using var dlg = new SaveFileDialog
        { InitialDirectory = folder,
            Filter = "TTW pavement design (*.ttwdesign)|*.ttwdesign", Title = "Save the design (to open and change later)",
            FileName = SafeName($"{JobNumber} {PavementTitle}".Trim() is { Length: > 0 } n ? n : "pavement design") + ".ttwdesign",
        };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK)
        {
            AppFolders.Tidy(folder); // cancelled: no empty folders left behind
            return;
        }
        try { File.WriteAllText(dlg.FileName, JsonSerializer.Serialize(saved, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true })); }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Could not save: " + ex.Message, "TTW Pavement", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        }
    }

    private void OpenDesign()
    {
        using var dlg = new OpenFileDialog { InitialDirectory = AppFolders.Exports, Filter = "TTW pavement design (*.ttwdesign)|*.ttwdesign", Title = "Open a saved design" };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        try
        {
            var saved = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<SavedDesign>(File.ReadAllText(dlg.FileName)) ?? throw new InvalidDataException("The file is empty.");
            _project.Text = saved.Project;
            _jobNo.Text = saved.JobNo;
            _pavementName.Text = saved.PavementName;
            _engineer.Text = saved.Engineer;
            _checker.Text = saved.Checker;
            foreach (var (key, text) in saved.Fields)
                if (_fields.TryGetValue(key, out var f)) f.Box.Text = text;
            foreach (var (key, on) in saved.Checks)
                if (_checks.TryGetValue(key, out var c)) c.Checked = on;
            _desa.Text = saved.Desa;
            _countDesa.Checked = saved.FromCounts;
            _knownDesa.Checked = !saved.FromCounts;
            if (saved.Grade >= 0 && saved.Grade < _grade.Items.Count) _grade.SelectedIndex = saved.Grade;
            (saved.Kind == "Slab" ? _slab : _road).Checked = true;
            SetKind(saved.Kind == "Slab" ? Kind.Slab : Kind.Road);
            GoTo(3);
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or JsonException or InvalidDataException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Could not open the design: " + ex.Message, "TTW Pavement", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        }
    }

    private void OpenSingleChecks()
    {
        using var form = new Form { Text = "Single checks (advanced)", Size = new Size(Brand.Px(1150), Brand.Px(720)), StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent };
        var panel = new DesignPanel();
        panel.UseBuildup += text => { form.Close(); UseBuildup?.Invoke(text); };
        var host = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Padding = new Padding(16, 8, 16, 8) };
        host.Controls.Add(panel);
        form.Controls.Add(host);
        Brand.Decorate(form, "Single checks", "Each workbook calculation on its own");
        host.BringToFront();
        form.ShowDialog(this);
    }

    private static string SafeName(string name)
    {
        var bad = Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars();
        var s = new string(name.Select(c => bad.Contains(c) ? '_' : c).ToArray()).Trim();
        return s.Length == 0 ? "design" : s;
    }

    // ================================================================ building blocks ==========

    private static Panel Page(out TableLayoutPanel table)
    {
        var page = new Panel { AutoScroll = true, Padding = new Padding(4, 0, 4, 0) };
        table = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, AutoSize = true, ColumnCount = 1 };
        page.Controls.Add(table);
        return page;
    }

    /// <summary>"Label  [ box ] unit" with a help line underneath that turns red when the value is wrong.</summary>
    private Control FieldRow(string key, string label, string unit, string value, string help)
    {
        var box = new TextBox { Text = value, Width = Brand.Px(130), TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Right, BackColor = Brand.Surface, BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle };
        var helpLabel = new Label { Text = help, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Font = Brand.Sized(8.5f), Tag = label, MaximumSize = new Size(Brand.Px(620), 0) };
        var field = new Field { Key = key, Unit = unit, Box = box, Help = helpLabel, HelpText = help };
        _fields[key] = field;
        box.TextChanged += (_, _) => { Validate(field); Recalculate(); };
        Validate(field);

        var t = new TableLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, ColumnCount = 3, Margin = new Padding(0, 4, 0, 2) };
        t.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Absolute, Brand.Px(250)));
        t.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        t.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        t.Controls.Add(new Label { Text = label, AutoSize = true, Margin = new Padding(3, 6, 6, 0) }, 0, 0);
        t.Controls.Add(box, 1, 0);
        t.Controls.Add(new Label { Text = unit, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Margin = new Padding(4, 6, 3, 0) }, 2, 0);
        t.Controls.Add(helpLabel, 1, 1);
        t.SetColumnSpan(helpLabel, 2);
        return t;
    }

    private static void Validate(Field f)
    {
        var text = f.Box.Text.Trim();
        if (text.Length == 0 && f.Key == "es")
        {
            f.Ok = true;
            f.Value = 0;
        }
        else if (Inputs.TryRead(text, f.Unit is "veh/day" or "years" or "" ? "" : f.Unit, out var v, out var error))
        {
            f.Ok = v >= 0;
            f.Value = v;
            if (!f.Ok) error = "Can't be negative.";
            f.Help.Text = f.Ok ? f.HelpText : error;
        }
        else
        {
            f.Ok = false;
            f.Help.Text = error;
        }
        if (text.Length == 0 && f.Key == "es") f.Help.Text = f.HelpText;
        f.Help.ForeColor = f.Ok ? Brand.Muted : Brand.Failure;
        f.Box.BackColor = f.Ok ? Brand.Surface : Color.FromArgb(251, 236, 232);
    }

    private CheckBox Check(string key, string text, bool on, string tip)
    {
        var c = new CheckBox { Text = text, AutoSize = true, Checked = on, Margin = new Padding(12, 6, 3, 3) };
        if (tip.Length > 0) _tips.SetToolTip(c, tip);
        c.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => Recalculate();
        _checks[key] = c;
        return c;
    }

    private static FlowLayoutPanel Row(params Control[] controls)
    {
        var f = new FlowLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, WrapContents = true, Margin = new Padding(0, 2, 0, 2) };
        foreach (var c in controls) c.Margin = new Padding(3, c is Label ? 7 : 3, 10, 3);
        f.Controls.AddRange(controls);
        return f;
    }

    private static Label Lbl(string text) => new() { Text = text, AutoSize = true };

    private static Label Hint(string text) => new()
    {
        Text = text, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Font = Brand.Sized(8.5f), MaximumSize = new Size(Brand.Px(700), 0), Margin = new Padding(3, 0, 3, 6),
    };
}

using System.Globalization;

/// <summary>
/// Pavement design calculations for engineers (the company workbooks): pick a calculation, fill in the
/// inputs, read the worked result. Results can be copied or saved for the design report, and a design
/// can be sent to the Detail tab to draw.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class DesignPanel : UserControl
{
    private sealed record Input(string Key, string Label, string Unit, double Default, string Hint = "", (string Text, double Value)[]? Choices = null);

    private sealed record Calculation(string Group, string Name, Input[] Inputs, Func<Func<string, double>, CalcResult> Run);

        new("x", "Post spacing x", "m", 2),

        new("h", "Soil depth H", "m", 5),
        new("e", "Soil modulus E", "MPa", 30, "Long-term Es"),
        new("p", "Post load P (unfactored)", "kN", 70),
    };

    private static Input[] EquivalentInputs()
    {
        var list = new List<Input>
        {
            new("sw", "Wheel spacing", "m", 1.8),
            new("sp", "Post spacing", "m", 2),
            new("su", "UDL spacing", "m", 2),
        };
        double[] h = { 1.5, 2.5, 2, 3 }, cbr = { 5, 0, 0, 0 }, n = { 0, 12, 22, 35 }, pi = { 0, 0, 20, 20 }, b = { 1, 0.8, 0.6, 0.6 };
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        {
            string l = $"Layer {i + 1}: ";
            list.Add(new($"h{i + 1}", l + "depth H", "m", h[i], i == 0 ? "Layers with H = 0 are left out" : ""));
            list.Add(new($"cbr{i + 1}", l + "CBR", "%", cbr[i], "0 if unknown"));
            list.Add(new($"n{i + 1}", l + "SPT N", "", n[i], "0 if unknown"));
            list.Add(new($"pi{i + 1}", l + "PI", "", pi[i]));
            list.Add(new($"qc{i + 1}", l + "CPT qc", "MPa", 0, "0 if unknown"));
            list.Add(new($"a{i + 1}", l + "a factor (Es = a qc)", "", 0, "T48 Table 1.10"));
            list.Add(new($"b{i + 1}", l + "b factor (EsL = Ess / b)", "", b[i], "T48 Table 1.8"));
        }
        return list.ToArray();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ UI -------------------

    private readonly ListBox _list = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, IntegralHeight = false, BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle, FormattingEnabled = true, BackColor = Brand.Surface };
    private readonly TableLayoutPanel _inputs = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Top, AutoSize = true, ColumnCount = 3 };
    private readonly Label _headline = new() { AutoSize = true, Font = Brand.Sized(11.5f, FontStyle.Bold), Margin = new Padding(3, 4, 3, 6) };
    private readonly DataGridView _results = new();
    private readonly Label _notes = new() { AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Margin = new Padding(3, 6, 3, 3) };
    private readonly TextBox _project = new() { Width = 260, PlaceholderText = "Project / job" };
    private readonly TextBox _engineer = new() { Width = 180, PlaceholderText = "Engineer" };
    private readonly Button _use = new() { Text = "Draw this build-up", AutoSize = true, Tag = Brand.PrimaryTag };
    private readonly ToolTip _tips = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, Control> _fields = new();
    private Calculation? _current;
    private CalcResult? _result;

    /// <summary>Raised with build-up text when the engineer sends a design to the Detail tab.</summary>
    public event Action<string>? UseBuildup;

    public DesignPanel()
    {
        Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        BackColor = Brand.Bg;
        Padding = new Padding(0, 8, 0, 0);

        foreach (var c in Calculations) _list.Items.Add(c);
        _list.Format += (_, e) => e.Value = e.ListItem is Calculation c ? $"{c.Group}:  {c.Name}" : "";
        _list.SelectedIndexChanged += (_, _) => Select(_list.SelectedItem as Calculation);

        var left = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Left, Width = Brand.Px(330), Padding = new Padding(0, 0, 12, 0) };
        left.Controls.Add(_list);
        left.Controls.Add(Brand.Step("① Choose a calculation"));
        left.Controls[1].Dock = DockStyle.Top;

        var inputsScroll = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Left, Width = Brand.Px(420), AutoScroll = true, Padding = new Padding(0, 0, 12, 0) };
        inputsScroll.Controls.Add(_inputs);
        var inputsStep = Brand.Step("② Inputs");
        inputsStep.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
        inputsScroll.Controls.Add(inputsStep);
        _inputs.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        _inputs.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Absolute, Brand.Px(150)));
        _inputs.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));

        SetUpGrid();
        var copy = new Button { Text = "Copy calculation", AutoSize = true };
        copy.Click += (_, _) => { if (_result is not null) Clipboard.SetText(_result.ToText(_project.Text, _engineer.Text)); };
        var save = new Button { Text = "Save calculation…", AutoSize = true };
        save.Click += (_, _) => SaveReport();
        _use.Click += (_, _) => { if (_result?.BuildupText is { } t) UseBuildup?.Invoke(t); };
        var actions = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, AutoSize = true, WrapContents = true, Padding = new Padding(0, 6, 0, 0) };
        actions.Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { _use, copy, save, _project, _engineer });
        foreach (Control c in actions.Controls) c.Margin = new Padding(0, 3, 8, 3);

        var right = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill };
        right.Controls.Add(_results);
        right.Controls.Add(_notes);
        right.Controls.Add(_headline);
        var resultStep = Brand.Step("③ Result");
        resultStep.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
        _headline.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
        _notes.Dock = DockStyle.Bottom;
        right.Controls.Add(resultStep);
        right.Controls.Add(actions);
        _results.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        right.Resize += (_, _) => _notes.MaximumSize = new Size(Math.Max(200, right.Width - 10), 0);

        Controls.Add(right);
        Controls.Add(inputsScroll);
        Controls.Add(left);
        _list.SelectedIndex = 0;
    }

    private void SetUpGrid()
    {
        _results.ReadOnly = true;
        _results.AllowUserToAddRows = false;
        _results.AllowUserToDeleteRows = false;
        _results.RowHeadersVisible = false;
        _results.AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill;
        _results.SelectionMode = DataGridViewSelectionMode.FullRowSelect;
        _results.Columns.Add("label", "Step");
        _results.Columns.Add("value", "Value");
        _results.Columns.Add("unit", "Unit");
        _results.Columns.Add("ref", "Note");
        _results.Columns[0].FillWeight = 230;
        _results.Columns[1].FillWeight = 70;
        _results.Columns[2].FillWeight = 55;
        _results.Columns[3].FillWeight = 90;
        _results.Columns[1].DefaultCellStyle.Alignment = DataGridViewContentAlignment.MiddleRight;
    }

    private void Select(Calculation? calc)
    {
        if (calc is null) return;
        _current = calc;
        _inputs.SuspendLayout();
        _inputs.Controls.Clear();
        _inputs.RowStyles.Clear();
        _fields.Clear();
        foreach (var input in calc.Inputs)
        {
            var label = new Label { Text = input.Label, AutoSize = true, Margin = new Padding(3, 8, 6, 3) };
            Control field;
            if (input.Choices is { } choices)
            {
                var combo = new ComboBox { DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDown, Dock = DockStyle.Fill, BackColor = Brand.Surface, FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat };
                foreach (var (text, _) in choices) combo.Items.Add(text);
                int match = Array.FindIndex(choices, c => Math.Abs(c.Value - input.Default) < 1e-9);
                if (match >= 0) combo.SelectedIndex = match; else combo.Text = Format(input.Default);
                if (input.Key is "fc" or "pos") combo.DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList;
                combo.TextChanged += (_, _) => Recalculate();
                combo.SelectedIndexChanged += (_, _) => Recalculate();
                field = combo;
            }
            else
            {
                var box = new TextBox { Text = Format(input.Default), Dock = DockStyle.Fill, BackColor = Brand.Surface, BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle, TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Right };
                box.TextChanged += (_, _) => Recalculate();
                field = box;
            }
            field.Margin = new Padding(3, 4, 3, 3);
            if (input.Hint.Length > 0) { _tips.SetToolTip(field, input.Hint); _tips.SetToolTip(label, input.Hint); }
            _fields[input.Key] = field;
            _inputs.Controls.Add(label);
            _inputs.Controls.Add(field);
            _inputs.Controls.Add(new Label { Text = input.Unit, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Brand.Muted, Margin = new Padding(3, 8, 3, 3) });
        }
        _inputs.ResumeLayout();
        Recalculate();
    }

    private static string Format(double v) => Math.Abs(v) >= 1e5 ? v.ToString("0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) : v.ToString("0.####", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

    /// <summary>The number in a field: plain numbers, "1e5", "1 x 10^5", "100,000", or a listed choice.</summary>
    private double Value(Input input)
    {
        var control = _fields[input.Key];
        if (input.Choices is { } choices && control is ComboBox combo)
        {
            int i = Array.FindIndex(choices, c => c.Text == combo.Text);
            if (i >= 0) return choices[i].Value;
        }
        var text = control.Text.Trim().Replace(",", "").Replace(" ", "").ToLowerInvariant();
        var power = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(text, @"^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)x10\^(\d+)");
        if (power.Success)
            return double.Parse(power.Groups[1].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) * Math.Pow(10, int.Parse(power.Groups[2].Value));
        var number = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(text, @"^-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:e[+-]?\d+)?");
        if (number.Success && double.TryParse(number.Value, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var v))
            return v;
        throw new FormatException($"\"{control.Text}\" is not a number ({input.Label}).");
    }

    private void Recalculate()
    {
        if (_current is null) return;
        _results.Rows.Clear();
        try
        {
            _result = _current.Run(key => Value(_current.Inputs.First(i => i.Key == key)));
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is FormatException or ArgumentException or OverflowException)
        {
            _result = null;
            _headline.ForeColor = Brand.Failure;
            _headline.Text = ex.Message;
            _notes.Text = "";
            _use.Enabled = false;
            return;
        }

        foreach (var line in _result.Lines.Where(l => l.Kind != LineKind.Input))
        {
            int row = _results.Rows.Add(line.Label, line.Value, line.Unit, line.Reference);
            var style = _results.Rows[row].DefaultCellStyle;
            switch (line.Kind)
            {
                case LineKind.Result: style.Font = Brand.Body(FontStyle.Bold); break;
                case LineKind.Ok: style.ForeColor = Brand.Success; style.Font = Brand.Body(FontStyle.Bold); break;
                case LineKind.Fail: style.ForeColor = Brand.Failure; style.Font = Brand.Body(FontStyle.Bold); break;
                case LineKind.Warning: style.ForeColor = Brand.Failure; break;
            }
        }
        _headline.ForeColor = _result.Pass == false ? Brand.Failure : Brand.Success;
        _headline.Text = (_result.Pass == false ? "✖ " : "✔ ") + _result.Headline;
        _notes.Text = string.Join(Environment.NewLine, _result.Notes.Distinct().Select(n => "• " + n)) +
                      (_result.Reference.Length > 0 ? Environment.NewLine + "Reference: " + _result.Reference : "");

        // Concrete thickness → a simple build-up for the detail.
        if (_result.BuildupText is null && _result.ThicknessMm is { } t && _fields.ContainsKey("fc") && _result.Pass != false)
            _result.BuildupText = $"[{_current.Name.ToUpperInvariant()}]\n{t:0}mm F'c {Value(_current.Inputs.First(i => i.Key == "fc")):0}MPa CONCRETE PAVEMENT\nSUBGRADE";
        _use.Enabled = _result.BuildupText is not null;
    }

    private void SaveReport()
    {
        if (_result is null) return;
        using var dlg = new SaveFileDialog { InitialDirectory = AppFolders.Today, Filter = "Text file (*.txt)|*.txt", FileName = _result.Title.Replace(" - ", " ").Replace("/", "-") + ".txt", Title = "Save the calculation" };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK)
        {
            AppFolders.TidyToday(); // cancelled: no empty date folder left behind
            return;
        }
        try { File.WriteAllText(dlg.FileName, _result.ToText(_project.Text, _engineer.Text)); }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Could not save: " + ex.Message, "TTW Pavement", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        }
    }
}


################################################################################
# FILE: build.ps1   (62 lines)
################################################################################

<#
.SYNOPSIS
  Builds the Pavement Build-up plugin and assembles dist\PavementBuildup.bundle.
.PARAMETER Install
  Also copies the bundle to %APPDATA%\Autodesk\ApplicationPlugins so Civil 3D 2026 autoloads it.
.EXAMPLE
  .\build.ps1 -Install
#>
param([switch]$Install, [string]$Configuration = "Release")
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
Set-Location $PSScriptRoot

dotnet test tests\PavementBuildup.Core.Tests -c $Configuration
if ($LASTEXITCODE) { throw "Tests failed" }

dotnet build src\PavementBuildup\PavementBuildup.csproj -c $Configuration
if ($LASTEXITCODE) { throw "Build failed" }

$out = "src\PavementBuildup\bin\$Configuration\net8.0-windows"
$bundle = "dist\PavementBuildup.bundle"
if (Test-Path $bundle) { Remove-Item $bundle -Recurse -Force }
New-Item -ItemType Directory "$bundle\Contents" | Out-Null
Copy-Item "bundle\PavementBuildup.bundle\PackageContents.xml" $bundle
Copy-Item "$out\PavementBuildup.dll", "$out\PavementBuildup.Core.dll" "$bundle\Contents"
Copy-Item "standards" "$bundle\Contents\standards" -Recurse
# Default company standard inside the bundle (the plugin looks for _support\_Standards\TTW.json).
New-Item -ItemType Directory "$bundle\Contents\_support\_Standards" -Force | Out-Null
Copy-Item "standards\TTW.json", "standards\TTW_Pave_Temp.dwg" "$bundle\Contents\_support\_Standards"

# Interpret with AI: published with its own dependencies into Contents\ai (loaded in an isolated context).
dotnet publish src\PavementBuildup.Ai\PavementBuildup.Ai.csproj -c $Configuration -o "$bundle\Contents\ai"
if ($LASTEXITCODE) { throw "AI component publish failed" }
Get-ChildItem "$bundle\Contents\ai" -Filter *.pdb | Remove-Item
Copy-Item "AI Pavement Build-up Prompt.txt", "TTW_Pvmt - Instructions.txt" "dist"
Write-Host "Bundle ready: $bundle"

# Standalone Windows app (no Civil 3D): one .exe that saves a DXF.
$standalone = "dist\TTW Pavement Standalone"
dotnet publish src\PavementBuildup.Standalone\PavementBuildup.Standalone.csproj -c $Configuration -o $standalone
if ($LASTEXITCODE) { throw "Standalone publish failed" }
Get-ChildItem $standalone -Filter *.pdb | Remove-Item
Copy-Item "TTW Pavement Standalone - Instructions.txt", "AI Pavement Build-up Prompt.txt" $standalone
Write-Host "Standalone app ready: $standalone"

# Release folder (TTW_Pave_Tool): Civil3D Tool, _support\_Standards, Standalone Tool\_Exports.
$release = "dist\TTW_Pave_Tool V1.0.0"
if (Test-Path $release) { Remove-Item $release -Recurse -Force }
New-Item -ItemType Directory "$release\Civil3D Tool", "$release\_support\_Standards", "$release\Standalone Tool\_Exports" | Out-Null
Copy-Item $bundle "$release\Civil3D Tool" -Recurse
Copy-Item "TTW_Pvmt - Instructions.txt" "$release\Civil3D Tool"
Copy-Item "AI Pavement Build-up Prompt.txt" "$release\_support"
Copy-Item "standards\TTW.json", "standards\TTW_Pave_Temp.dwg" "$release\_support\_Standards"   # company standard + template drawing
Copy-Item "$standalone\TTW Pavement.exe", "TTW Pavement Standalone - Instructions.txt" "$release\Standalone Tool"
Compress-Archive -Path $release -DestinationPath "$release.zip" -Force
Write-Host "Release ready: $release.zip"

if ($Install) {
    $target = Join-Path $env:APPDATA "Autodesk\ApplicationPlugins\PavementBuildup.bundle"
    if (Test-Path $target) { Remove-Item $target -Recurse -Force }
    Copy-Item $bundle $target -Recurse
    Write-Host "Installed to $target - restart Civil 3D 2026, then type TTWPVMT."
}
